// ===== lib/features/hub/data/hub_watch.dart
import 'dart:math' as math;

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/features/cash/data/cash_repository.dart';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  Watch list — things an owner wants to know about but staff won't mention:
//  discounts, cancelled bills, removed payments, deleted expenses, price and
//  commission changes, who took the money and who signed in when.
//  Read from records the app already keeps, plus the activity record.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

enum HubChangeKind {
  invoiceCancelled,
  paymentRemoved,
  expenseDeleted,
  rateChanged,
  priceEdited,
  userRemoved,
  signInFailed,
}

class HubChange {
  const HubChange({
    required this.kind,
    required this.at,
    required this.title,
    required this.detail,
    required this.who,
    this.amount,
  });

  final HubChangeKind kind;
  final DateTime at;
  final String title, detail, who;
  final int? amount;
}

class HubDiscount {
  const HubDiscount({
    required this.at,
    required this.patient,
    required this.invoiceNo,
    required this.amount,
    required this.subtotal,
    required this.dentist,
    this.by = '',
  });

  final DateTime at;
  final String patient, invoiceNo, dentist;

  /// Who gave it — recorded from the phase O update on, '' before that.
  final String by;
  final int amount, subtotal;
  double get share => subtotal <= 0 ? 0 : amount / subtotal;
  bool get big => share >= .3;
}

class HubSignIns {
  const HubSignIns({
    required this.name,
    required this.count,
    required this.afterHours,
    required this.failed,
    required this.last,
    required this.devices,
  });

  final String name;
  final int count, afterHours, failed;
  final DateTime? last;
  final Set<String> devices;
}

class HubWatch {
  const HubWatch({
    required this.discountTotal,
    required this.discountCount,
    required this.billedTotal,
    required this.discountByDentist,
    required this.discounts,
    required this.cancelledCount,
    required this.cancelledTotal,
    required this.removedCount,
    required this.removedTotal,
    required this.deletedCount,
    required this.deletedTotal,
    required this.noShows,
    required this.cancelledVisits,
    required this.changes,
    required this.takenBy,
    required this.signIns,
    required this.failedSignIns,
    required this.afterHoursSignIns,
    required this.hubVisits,
  });

  final int discountTotal, discountCount, billedTotal;
  final List<({String name, int total, int count})> discountByDentist;

  /// Biggest discounts first.
  final List<HubDiscount> discounts;

  final int cancelledCount, cancelledTotal;
  final int removedCount, removedTotal;
  final int deletedCount, deletedTotal;
  final int noShows, cancelledVisits;

  /// Newest first.
  final List<HubChange> changes;

  /// Money received, by the staff member who recorded it.
  final List<({String name, int total, int count})> takenBy;

  final List<HubSignIns> signIns;
  final int failedSignIns, afterHoursSignIns;
  final List<ActivityEventRow> hubVisits;

  double get discountShare => billedTotal + discountTotal <= 0
      ? 0
      : discountTotal / (billedTotal + discountTotal);

  /// Items worth a red mark on the rail.
  int get flags =>
      discounts.where((d) => d.big).length +
      cancelledCount +
      removedCount +
      deletedCount +
      failedSignIns;
}

Future<HubWatch> loadHubWatch({
  required AppDatabase db,
  required List<Branch> branches,
  required String? branchId,
  required DateTime start,
  required DateTime end,
}) async {
  Expression<bool> inBranch(GeneratedColumn<String> c) =>
      branchId == null ? const Constant(true) : c.equals(branchId);

  // The activity record for the period, newest first. Not branch-filtered
  // here: an owner's lines carry no branch, but the discount, price or rate
  // they touched still belongs in a branch view.
  final allEvents =
      await (db.select(db.activityEvents)
            ..where(
              (t) =>
                  t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                  t.happenedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                  t.happenedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end),
            )
            ..orderBy([(t) => OrderingTerm.desc(t.happenedAt)]))
          .get();
  List<String> parts(ActivityEventRow e) => e.detail.split('|');
  final discountBy = <String, String>{
    for (final e in allEvents.reversed)
      if (e.kind == 'discount_given') parts(e).first: e.userName,
  };

  // ── discounts: invoices whose adjustment took money off ──
  final discRows =
      await (db.select(db.invoices).join([
            innerJoin(
              db.patients,
              db.patients.id.equalsExp(db.invoices.patientId),
            ),
          ])..where(
            db.invoices.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                db.invoices.status.equals('cancelled').not() &
                db.invoices.adjustment.isBiggerThanValue(0) &
                db.invoices.issuedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                db.invoices.issuedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end) &
                inBranch(db.invoices.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final apptIds = <int>{
    for (final r in discRows)
      if (r.readTable(db.invoices).appointmentId != null)
        r.readTable(db.invoices).appointmentId!,
  };
  final dentistOf = apptIds.isEmpty
      ? const <int, String>{}
      : {
          for (final a in await (db.select(
            db.appointments,
          )..where((t) => t.id.isIn(apptIds))).get())
            a.id: a.dentist.trim(),
        };
  final discounts = [
    for (final r in discRows)
      () {
        final i = r.readTable(db.invoices);
        return HubDiscount(
          at: i.issuedAt,
          patient: r.readTable(db.patients).fullName,
          invoiceNo: i.invoiceNo,
          amount: i.adjustment,
          subtotal: i.subtotal,
          dentist: i.appointmentId == null
              ? ''
              : (dentistOf[i.appointmentId] ?? ''),
          by: discountBy[i.invoiceNo] ?? '',
        );
      }(),
  ]..sort((a, b) => b.amount.compareTo(a.amount));
  final byDentist = <String, ({String name, int total, int count})>{};
  for (final d in discounts) {
    final name = d.dentist.isEmpty ? 'Not linked to a visit' : d.dentist;
    final k = d.dentist.isEmpty ? '' : dentistKey(d.dentist);
    final prev = byDentist[k];
    byDentist[k] = (
      name: prev?.name ?? name,
      total: (prev?.total ?? 0) + d.amount,
      count: (prev?.count ?? 0) + 1,
    );
  }

  final billed =
      await (db.select(db.invoices)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.equals('cancelled').not() &
                t.issuedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                t.issuedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();

  final changes = <HubChange>[];

  // ── cancelled invoices ──
  final cancelled =
      await (db.select(db.invoices).join([
            innerJoin(
              db.patients,
              db.patients.id.equalsExp(db.invoices.patientId),
            ),
          ])..where(
            db.invoices.status.equals('cancelled') &
                db.invoices.cancelledAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                db.invoices.cancelledAt.isSmallerThanValue(end) &
                inBranch(db.invoices.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  var cancelledTotal = 0;
  for (final r in cancelled) {
    final i = r.readTable(db.invoices);
    cancelledTotal += i.total;
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.invoiceCancelled,
        at: i.cancelledAt ?? i.updatedAt,
        title: 'Invoice ${i.invoiceNo} cancelled',
        detail: r.readTable(db.patients).fullName,
        who: i.cancelledBy ?? '',
        amount: i.total,
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── payments removed ──
  final removed =
      await (db.select(db.invoicePayments).join([
            innerJoin(
              db.invoices,
              db.invoices.id.equalsExp(db.invoicePayments.invoiceId),
            ),
            innerJoin(
              db.patients,
              db.patients.id.equalsExp(db.invoices.patientId),
            ),
          ])..where(
            db.invoicePayments.isDeleted.equals(true) &
                db.invoicePayments.deletedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                db.invoicePayments.deletedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end) &
                inBranch(db.invoicePayments.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  var removedTotal = 0;
  for (final r in removed) {
    final p = r.readTable(db.invoicePayments);
    removedTotal += p.amount;
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.paymentRemoved,
        at: p.deletedAt ?? p.updatedAt,
        title:
            'Payment removed · invoice ${r.readTable(db.invoices).invoiceNo}',
        detail:
            '${r.readTable(db.patients).fullName} · paid ${p.method}'
            '${(p.receivedByName ?? '').isEmpty ? '' : ', taken by ${p.receivedByName}'}',
        who: p.deletedByName ?? '',
        amount: p.amount,
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── expenses deleted ──
  final deleted =
      await (db.select(db.expenses)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(true) &
                t.deletedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                t.deletedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  var deletedTotal = 0;
  for (final e in deleted) {
    deletedTotal += e.amount;
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.expenseDeleted,
        at: e.deletedAt ?? e.updatedAt,
        title: 'Expense deleted',
        detail: [
          if ((e.description ?? '').trim().isNotEmpty) e.description!.trim(),
          if ((e.deleteReason ?? '').trim().isNotEmpty)
            'reason: ${e.deleteReason!.trim()}',
        ].join(' · '),
        who: e.deletedByName ?? '',
        amount: e.amount,
      ),
    );
  }

  String pct(String v) {
    final d = double.tryParse(v);
    return d == null ? v : '${d.toStringAsFixed(d % 1 == 0 ? 0 : 1)}%';
  }

  // ── commission rates changed ──
  final ratesLogged = <String>{};
  for (final e in allEvents.where((e) => e.kind == 'rate_changed')) {
    final p = parts(e);
    if (p.length < 3) continue;
    ratesLogged.add(dentistKey(p[0]));
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.rateChanged,
        at: e.happenedAt,
        title: 'Commission rate changed · ${p[0]}',
        detail: p[1].isEmpty
            ? 'set to ${pct(p[2])}'
            : '${pct(p[1])} → ${pct(p[2])}',
        who: e.userName,
      ),
    );
  }
  for (final r
      in await (db.select(db.dentistRates)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.updatedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                t.updatedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end),
          ))
          .get()) {
    if (ratesLogged.contains(dentistKey(r.dentistName))) continue;
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.rateChanged,
        at: r.updatedAt,
        title: r.isDeleted
            ? 'Commission rate removed · ${r.dentistName}'
            : 'Commission rate set · ${r.dentistName}',
        detail: r.isDeleted
            ? ''
            : '${r.percent.toStringAsFixed(r.percent % 1 == 0 ? 0 : 1)}% of what their patients pay',
        who: '',
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── price list edits ──
  final pricesLogged = <String>{};
  for (final e in allEvents.where(
    (e) =>
        e.kind == 'price_changed' ||
        e.kind == 'procedure_added' ||
        e.kind == 'procedure_removed',
  )) {
    final p = parts(e);
    if (p.length < 3) continue;
    pricesLogged.add(p[0].trim().toLowerCase());
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.priceEdited,
        at: e.happenedAt,
        title: switch (e.kind) {
          'procedure_added' => 'Procedure added · ${p[0]}',
          'procedure_removed' => 'Procedure removed · ${p[0]}',
          _ => 'Price changed · ${p[0]}',
        },
        detail: switch (e.kind) {
          'procedure_added' => 'Rs ${p[2]}',
          'procedure_removed' => 'was Rs ${p[1]}',
          _ => 'Rs ${p[1]} → Rs ${p[2]}',
        },
        who: e.userName,
      ),
    );
  }
  for (final t
      in await (db.select(db.treatments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.updatedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                t.updatedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end),
          ))
          .get()) {
    if (pricesLogged.contains(t.name.trim().toLowerCase())) continue;
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.priceEdited,
        at: t.updatedAt,
        title: t.isDeleted
            ? 'Procedure removed · ${t.name}'
            : 'Procedure added or changed · ${t.name}',
        detail: t.isDeleted ? '' : 'Price now Rs ${t.price}',
        who: '',
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── staff logins removed ──
  for (final u
      in await (db.select(db.users)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(true) &
                t.updatedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                t.updatedAt.isSmallerThanValue(end),
          ))
          .get()) {
    changes.add(
      HubChange(
        kind: HubChangeKind.userRemoved,
        at: u.updatedAt,
        title: 'Staff login removed · ${u.fullName}',
        detail: u.role,
        who: '',
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── visits that didn't happen ──
  final visits =
      await (db.select(db.appointments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.isIn(const ['noShow', 'cancelled']) &
                t.startsAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                t.startsAt.isSmallerThanValue(end) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();

  // ── who took the money ──
  final pays =
      await (db.select(db.invoicePayments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.paidAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(start) &
                t.paidAt.isSmallerThanValue(end) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final taken = <String, ({String name, int total, int count})>{};
  for (final p in pays) {
    final taker = (p.receivedByName ?? '').trim();
    final name = taker.isEmpty ? 'Name not recorded' : taker;
    final prev = taken[name];
    taken[name] = (
      name: name,
      total: (prev?.total ?? 0) + p.amount,
      count: (prev?.count ?? 0) + 1,
    );
  }

  // ── sign-ins (from the activity record) ──
  final events = branchId == null
      ? allEvents
      : allEvents.where((e) => e.branchId == branchId).toList();

  // Opening hours of the branches in view: earliest open, latest close;
  // a weekday is "closed" only if every branch is closed then.
  final open = branches.isEmpty
      ? 9 * 60
      : branches.map((b) => b.openMinutes).reduce(math.min);
  final close = branches.isEmpty
      ? 21 * 60
      : branches.map((b) => b.closeMinutes).reduce(math.max);
  bool closedDay(int weekday) =>
      branches.isNotEmpty &&
      branches.every(
        (b) => b.closedDays
            .split(',')
            .map((s) => int.tryParse(s.trim()))
            .contains(weekday),
      );
  bool afterHours(DateTime t) {
    final m = t.hour * 60 + t.minute;
    // 30 minutes either side is normal opening-up and closing time
    return closedDay(t.weekday) || m < open - 30 || m > close + 30;
  }

  final people = <String, HubSignIns>{};
  var failed = 0, outside = 0;
  for (final e in events) {
    if (e.kind != 'sign_in' && e.kind != 'sign_in_failed') continue;
    final name = e.userName.isEmpty ? 'Unknown' : e.userName;
    final prev = people[name];
    final ok = e.kind == 'sign_in';
    final odd = ok && afterHours(e.happenedAt);
    if (!ok) {
      failed++;
      changes.add(
        HubChange(
          kind: HubChangeKind.signInFailed,
          at: e.happenedAt,
          title: 'Failed sign-in · "$name"',
          detail: [
            if (e.device.isNotEmpty) 'Computer ${e.device}',
            if (e.detail.isNotEmpty) e.detail,
          ].join(' · '),
          who: '',
        ),
      );
    }
    if (odd) outside++;
    people[name] = HubSignIns(
      name: name,
      count: (prev?.count ?? 0) + (ok ? 1 : 0),
      afterHours: (prev?.afterHours ?? 0) + (odd ? 1 : 0),
      failed: (prev?.failed ?? 0) + (ok ? 0 : 1),
      // events are newest first, so the first one seen is the latest
      last: prev?.last ?? (ok ? e.happenedAt : null),
      devices: {...?prev?.devices, if (e.device.isNotEmpty) e.device},
    );
  }

  changes.sort((a, b) => b.at.compareTo(a.at));
  int byTotal(
    ({String name, int total, int count}) a,
    ({String name, int total, int count}) b,
  ) => b.total.compareTo(a.total);

  return HubWatch(
    discountTotal: discounts.fold(0, (s, d) => s + d.amount),
    discountCount: discounts.length,
    billedTotal: billed.fold(0, (s, i) => s + i.total),
    discountByDentist: byDentist.values.toList()..sort(byTotal),
    discounts: discounts.take(10).toList(),
    cancelledCount: cancelled.length,
    cancelledTotal: cancelledTotal,
    removedCount: removed.length,
    removedTotal: removedTotal,
    deletedCount: deleted.length,
    deletedTotal: deletedTotal,
    noShows: visits.where((a) => a.status == 'noShow').length,
    cancelledVisits: visits.where((a) => a.status == 'cancelled').length,
    changes: changes.take(40).toList(),
    takenBy: taken.values.toList()..sort(byTotal),
    signIns: people.values.toList()..sort((a, b) => b.count.compareTo(a.count)),
    failedSignIns: failed,
    afterHoursSignIns: outside,
    hubVisits: events.where((e) => e.kind == 'hub_open').take(12).toList(),
  );
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/data/hub_tables.dart
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

/// Things an owner may want to look back on: who opened the Detailed
/// Reporting Hub and when, wrong PINs, lockouts, PIN changes and resets.
///
/// Append-only and synced, so the owner sees what happened on every
/// computer. Phase 3's Watch list adds more kinds (discounts, price
/// changes, sign-ins) to the same table.
@DataClassName('ActivityEventRow')
class ActivityEvents extends Table {
  IntColumn get id => integer().autoIncrement()();
  TextColumn get uuid => text().unique()();
  TextColumn get clinicId => text()();
  TextColumn get branchId => text().withDefault(const Constant(''))();

  /// hub_open · hub_exit · hub_pin_wrong · hub_pin_locked · hub_pin_set ·
  /// hub_pin_changed · hub_pin_reset · hub_idle_lock · hub_idle_exit
  TextColumn get kind => text()();

  /// Who did it — names are snapshots, so they survive the user being
  /// removed later.
  TextColumn get userUuid => text().withDefault(const Constant(''))();
  TextColumn get userName => text().withDefault(const Constant(''))();

  /// Computer letter (A, B …).
  TextColumn get device => text().withDefault(const Constant(''))();
  TextColumn get detail => text().withDefault(const Constant(''))();
  DateTimeColumn get happenedAt => dateTime().withDefault(currentDateAndTime)();

  BoolColumn get isDeleted => boolean().withDefault(const Constant(false))();
  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt => dateTime().withDefault(currentDateAndTime)();
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/data/hub_security.dart
import 'dart:isolate';

import 'package:bcrypt/bcrypt.dart';
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/utils/monotonic_clock.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/licensing/data/license_verifier.dart';

import 'activity_log.dart';
import 'hub_pin.dart';

/// Who may open the Detailed Reporting Hub, and whether the plan has it.
///
/// [allowed]: the owner always; an admin only when the owner ticked
/// "Admins can open the Hub". [onPlan]: Standard or Premium.
/// A record, so listeners only fire when a value really changes.
typedef HubAccess = ({bool allowed, bool onPlan});

final hubAccessProvider = Provider<HubAccess>((ref) {
  final role = ref.watch(authControllerProvider.select((s) => s?.role));
  final onPlan = ref.watch(entitlementsProvider.select((e) => e.reportingHub));
  final adminOk =
      role == AppRole.admin && ref.watch(canProvider(Perm.viewReportingHub));
  return (allowed: role == AppRole.owner || adminOk, onPlan: onPlan);
});

enum HubCheckResult { ok, wrong, locked }

class HubCheck {
  const HubCheck.ok() : result = HubCheckResult.ok, triesLeft = 0, until = null;
  const HubCheck.wrong(this.triesLeft)
    : result = HubCheckResult.wrong,
      until = null;
  const HubCheck.locked(DateTime this.until)
    : result = HubCheckResult.locked,
      triesLeft = 0;

  final HubCheckResult result;
  final int triesLeft;
  final DateTime? until;

  bool get ok => result == HubCheckResult.ok;
}

/// PIN, lockout, reset codes and the activity record for the Hub.
///
/// Lockout counters live in this computer's settings (5 wrong → 5 minutes
/// here). The PIN hash lives on the user row and syncs, so one PIN works on
/// every computer.
class HubSecurity {
  HubSecurity(this._db, this._clock);

  final AppDatabase _db;
  final MonotonicClock _clock;
  final _verifier = LicenseVerifier();

  static const maxTries = 5;
  static const lockFor = Duration(minutes: 5);
  static const _kNonces = 'hub_reset_nonces';

  String _key(User u) => u.uuid.isEmpty ? 'id${u.id}' : u.uuid;

  /// Fresh copy of the signed-in user's row (PIN hash included).
  Future<User?> userFor(AuthSession s) => _db.findActiveUser(s.username);

  bool hasPin(User u) => (u.hubPinHash ?? '').isNotEmpty;

  /// When this computer's lockout ends, or null when not locked.
  Future<DateTime?> lockedUntil(User u) async {
    final ms = int.tryParse(
      await _db.getSetting('hub_pin_lock:${_key(u)}') ?? '',
    );
    if (ms == null) return null;
    final at = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(ms);
    return at.isAfter(DateTime.now()) ? at : null;
  }

  Future<HubCheck> checkPin(User u, AuthSession s, String pin) async {
    final until = await lockedUntil(u);
    if (until != null) return HubCheck.locked(until);
    final stored = u.hubPinHash ?? '';
    if (stored.isNotEmpty && await HubPin.verify(pin, stored)) {
      await _clearFails(u);
      return const HubCheck.ok();
    }
    return _fail(u, s, 'PIN');
  }

  /// The login password, asked once before the first PIN is set.
  Future<HubCheck> checkPassword(User u, AuthSession s, String password) async {
    final until = await lockedUntil(u);
    if (until != null) return HubCheck.locked(until);
    final hash = u.passwordHash;
    final ok = await Isolate.run(() {
      try {
        return BCrypt.checkpw(password, hash);
      } catch (_) {
        return false;
      }
    });
    if (ok) {
      await _clearFails(u);
      return const HubCheck.ok();
    }
    return _fail(u, s, 'password');
  }

  /// Saves a new PIN (hash only) and records [kind] in the activity log.
  Future<void> setPin(
    User u,
    AuthSession s,
    String pin, {
    required String kind,
    String detail = '',
  }) async {
    final hash = await HubPin.hash(pin);
    final now = DateTime.now();
    await (_db.update(_db.users)..where((t) => t.id.equals(u.id))).write(
      UsersCompanion(
        hubPinHash: Value(hash),
        hubPinSetAt: Value(now),
        updatedAt: Value(now), // → pushed on the next sync
      ),
    );
    await _clearFails(u);
    await _db.setSetting('hub_pin_lock:${_key(u)}', '');
    await log(s, kind, detail: detail);
  }

  /// Checks a pasted reset code without using it.
  Future<({HubResetCode? code, String? error})> checkResetCode(
    User u,
    String raw,
  ) async {
    if (raw.trim().isEmpty) {
      return (code: null, error: 'Paste the reset code first.');
    }
    final c = HubResetCode.parse(raw, _verifier.verifyCanonical);
    if (c == null) {
      return (
        code: null,
        error:
            'This is not a valid reset code. Copy the whole code and try again.',
      );
    }
    final clinicId = await _db.currentClinicId();
    if (clinicId == null || c.clinicId != clinicId) {
      return (code: null, error: 'This code was made for a different clinic.');
    }
    if (c.username.trim().toLowerCase() != u.username.trim().toLowerCase()) {
      return (
        code: null,
        error: 'This code was made for the user "${c.username}", not you.',
      );
    }
    // Monotonic clock — moving the computer's date back can't revive it.
    if ((await _clock.now()).isAfter(c.expiresAt)) {
      return (
        code: null,
        error: 'This code has expired. Ask Inover Studio for a new one.',
      );
    }
    if (await _nonceUsed(c.nonce)) {
      return (code: null, error: 'This code has already been used.');
    }
    return (code: c, error: null);
  }

  /// Uses a reset code and sets [newPin]. Returns an error message or null.
  Future<String?> redeemReset(
    User u,
    AuthSession s,
    String raw,
    String newPin,
  ) async {
    final r = await checkResetCode(u, raw);
    if (r.error != null) return r.error;
    final nonce = r.code!.nonce;
    final used =
        (await _db.getSetting(_kNonces) ?? '')
            .split(',')
            .where((e) => e.isNotEmpty)
            .toList()
          ..add(nonce);
    await _db.setSetting(
      _kNonces,
      used.sublist(used.length > 30 ? used.length - 30 : 0).join(','),
    );
    // The nonce also goes into the synced log, so the same code can't be
    // used again on another computer.
    await setPin(u, s, newPin, kind: 'hub_pin_reset', detail: 'code:$nonce');
    return null;
  }

  Future<bool> _nonceUsed(String nonce) async {
    final local = (await _db.getSetting(_kNonces) ?? '').split(',');
    if (local.contains(nonce)) return true;
    final row =
        await (_db.select(_db.activityEvents)
              ..where(
                (t) =>
                    t.kind.equals('hub_pin_reset') &
                    t.detail.equals('code:$nonce'),
              )
              ..limit(1))
            .getSingleOrNull();
    return row != null;
  }

  Future<HubCheck> _fail(User u, AuthSession s, String what) async {
    final k = _key(u);
    final n =
        (int.tryParse(await _db.getSetting('hub_pin_fail:$k') ?? '') ?? 0) + 1;
    if (n >= maxTries) {
      final until = DateTime.now().add(lockFor);
      await _db.setSetting(
        'hub_pin_lock:$k',
        '${until.millisecondsSinceEpoch}',
      );
      await _db.setSetting('hub_pin_fail:$k', '0');
      await log(s, 'hub_pin_locked', detail: '$maxTries wrong tries ($what)');
      return HubCheck.locked(until);
    }
    await _db.setSetting('hub_pin_fail:$k', '$n');
    await log(s, 'hub_pin_wrong', detail: what);
    return HubCheck.wrong(maxTries - n);
  }

  Future<void> _clearFails(User u) =>
      _db.setSetting('hub_pin_fail:${_key(u)}', '0');

  /// Adds one line to the activity record. Never throws — a failed log line
  /// must not stop the owner using the Hub.
  Future<void> log(AuthSession? s, String kind, {String detail = ''}) =>
      logActivity(_db, kind, session: s, detail: detail);

  /// Latest Hub activity, newest first — Settings and System health.
  Stream<List<ActivityEventRow>> watchHubEvents({int limit = 12}) =>
      (_db.select(_db.activityEvents)
            ..where((t) => t.kind.like('hub_%') & t.isDeleted.equals(false))
            ..orderBy([(t) => OrderingTerm.desc(t.happenedAt)])
            ..limit(limit))
          .watch();
}

final hubSecurityProvider = Provider<HubSecurity>(
  (ref) => HubSecurity(
    ref.watch(appDatabaseProvider),
    ref.watch(monotonicClockProvider),
  ),
);

final hubEventsProvider = StreamProvider.autoDispose<List<ActivityEventRow>>(
  (ref) => ref.watch(hubSecurityProvider).watchHubEvents(),
);

/// When the signed-in user's Hub PIN was set (null = not set yet).
final myHubPinSetAtProvider = StreamProvider.autoDispose<DateTime?>((ref) {
  final username = ref.watch(authControllerProvider.select((s) => s?.username));
  if (username == null) return Stream.value(null);
  final db = ref.watch(appDatabaseProvider);
  return (db.select(db.users)
        ..where((t) => t.username.equals(username) & t.isDeleted.equals(false)))
      .watchSingleOrNull()
      .map((u) => (u?.hubPinHash ?? '').isEmpty ? null : u?.hubPinSetAt);
});

/// Human words for an activity kind.
String hubEventLabel(String kind) => switch (kind) {
  'hub_open' => 'Opened the Hub',
  'hub_exit' => 'Left the Hub',
  'hub_pin_wrong' => 'Wrong PIN or password',
  'hub_pin_locked' => 'Locked out for 5 minutes',
  'hub_pin_set' => 'Set a Hub PIN',
  'hub_pin_changed' => 'Changed the Hub PIN',
  'hub_pin_reset' => 'Reset the PIN with a code',
  'hub_idle_lock' => 'Hub locked',
  'hub_idle_exit' => 'Hub closed (no activity)',
  'hub_admins_on' => 'Allowed admins into the Hub',
  'hub_admins_off' => 'Stopped admins opening the Hub',
  'hub_export' => 'Downloaded the owner report',
  'sign_in' => 'Signed in',
  'sign_in_failed' => 'Failed sign-in',
  'sign_out' => 'Signed out',
  'auto_sign_out' => 'Signed out (no activity)',
  'discount_given' => 'Gave a discount',
  'price_changed' => 'Changed a price',
  'procedure_added' => 'Added a procedure',
  'procedure_removed' => 'Removed a procedure',
  'rate_changed' => 'Changed a commission rate',
  _ => kind,
};
// ===== lib/features/hub/data/hub_pdf.dart
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:pdf/pdf.dart';
import 'package:pdf/widgets.dart' as pw;

import 'hub_data.dart';
import 'hub_forecast.dart';

// The built-in PDF font only knows Latin-1: plain hyphens, no ≈ or ▲.

String _rs(int v) =>
    '${v < 0 ? '-' : ''}Rs ${v.abs().toString().replaceAllMapped(RegExp(r'(\d)(?=(\d{3})+$)'), (m) => '${m[1]},')}';

const _mon = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', //
  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
];
String _date(DateTime d) => '${d.day} ${_mon[d.month - 1]} ${d.year}';
String _time(DateTime t) {
  final h = t.hour % 12 == 0 ? 12 : t.hour % 12;
  return '$h:${t.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')} ${t.hour < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM'}';
}

String _pct(int cur, int prev) {
  if (prev <= 0) return '';
  final p = ((cur - prev) / prev * 100).round();
  return p == 0 ? 'same as before' : '${p > 0 ? '+' : ''}$p% vs before';
}

/// The owner report: one A4 document with everything the Hub shows for the
/// chosen branch and period.
Future<Uint8List> buildHubPdf(
  HubData data, {
  required String clinicName,
  required String scopeName,
  required String periodLabel,
  required String preparedFor,
}) async {
  final doc = pw.Document(
    title: 'Owner report - $clinicName',
    author: 'DentOS',
  );
  const ink = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF0D1626);
  const muted = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF64748B);
  const line = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFE2E8F0);
  const accent = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFF0EA5C6);
  const soft = PdfColor.fromInt(0xFFF1F5F9);

  pw.TextStyle st(double size, {bool bold = false, PdfColor color = ink}) =>
      pw.TextStyle(
        fontSize: size,
        color: color,
        fontWeight: bold ? pw.FontWeight.bold : pw.FontWeight.normal,
      );

  pw.Widget heading(String text, [String? sub]) => pw.Padding(
    padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(top: 18, bottom: 8),
    child: pw.Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        pw.Text(text, style: st(14, bold: true)),
        if (sub != null) pw.Text(sub, style: st(9, color: muted)),
      ],
    ),
  );

  pw.Widget tiles(List<(String, String, String)> items) => pw.Wrap(
    spacing: 8,
    runSpacing: 8,
    children: [
      for (final t in items)
        pw.Container(
          width: 160,
          padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(10),
          decoration: pw.BoxDecoration(
            color: soft,
            borderRadius: pw.BorderRadius.circular(6),
          ),
          child: pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              pw.Text(t.$1, style: st(8.5, color: muted)),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 3),
              pw.Text(t.$2, style: st(13, bold: true)),
              if (t.$3.isNotEmpty) pw.Text(t.$3, style: st(8, color: muted)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
    ],
  );

  pw.Widget table(
    List<String> head,
    List<List<String>> rows, {
    List<int>? rightCols,
  }) {
    final right = rightCols ?? const <int>[];
    pw.Widget cell(String text, int col, {bool header = false}) => pw.Padding(
      padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 5),
      child: pw.Text(
        text,
        textAlign: right.contains(col) ? pw.TextAlign.right : pw.TextAlign.left,
        style: header ? st(8.5, bold: true, color: muted) : st(9),
      ),
    );
    return pw.Table(
      border: const pw.TableBorder(
        horizontalInside: pw.BorderSide(color: line, width: .5),
        bottom: pw.BorderSide(color: line, width: .5),
      ),
      children: [
        pw.TableRow(
          decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(color: soft),
          children: [
            for (var i = 0; i < head.length; i++)
              cell(head[i], i, header: true),
          ],
        ),
        for (final r in rows)
          pw.TableRow(
            children: [for (var i = 0; i < r.length; i++) cell(r[i], i)],
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  final t = data.today;
  final m = data.money;
  final f = data.forecast;
  final w = data.watch;
  final h = data.health;

  doc.addPage(
    pw.MultiPage(
      pageFormat: PdfPageFormat.a4,
      margin: const pw.EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(36, 34, 36, 34),
      header: (ctx) => ctx.pageNumber == 1
          ? pw.SizedBox()
          : pw.Padding(
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
              child: pw.Text(
                '$clinicName · owner report · ${_date(data.loadedAt)}',
                style: st(8, color: muted),
              ),
            ),
      footer: (ctx) => pw.Align(
        alignment: pw.Alignment.centerRight,
        child: pw.Text(
          'Confidential · page ${ctx.pageNumber} of ${ctx.pagesCount}',
          style: st(8, color: muted),
        ),
      ),
      build: (ctx) => [
        // ── cover block ──
        pw.Container(
          padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.all(16),
          // pdf allows rounded corners only with an even border all round,
          // so the accent stripe stays square-cornered
          decoration: const pw.BoxDecoration(
            color: soft,
            border: pw.Border(left: pw.BorderSide(color: accent, width: 4)),
          ),
          child: pw.Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: pw.CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              pw.Text(
                'DETAILED REPORTING HUB',
                style: st(8, bold: true, color: accent),
              ),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
              pw.Text('Owner report · $clinicName', style: st(18, bold: true)),
              pw.SizedBox(height: 4),
              pw.Text(
                '$scopeName · $periodLabel · prepared ${_date(data.loadedAt)} '
                '${_time(data.loadedAt)} for $preparedFor',
                style: st(9, color: muted),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),

        heading('Right now', 'Today and this month so far'),
        tiles([
          (
            'Collected today',
            _rs(t.collected),
            _pct(t.collected, t.lastWeekSameTime),
          ),
          (
            'Patients today',
            '${t.patients}',
            '${t.count('completed')} seen · ${t.count('noShow')} no-show',
          ),
          (
            'Collected this month',
            _rs(t.monthCollected),
            _pct(t.monthCollected, t.lastMonthToDate),
          ),
          (
            'Net profit this month',
            _rs(t.monthNet),
            'after ${_rs(t.monthExpenses)} expenses',
          ),
          (
            'Still owed',
            _rs(t.owedTotal),
            '${t.owedCount} invoices · ${_rs(t.owedOver60)} over 60 days',
          ),
        ]),
        if (data.alerts.isNotEmpty) ...[
          heading('Needs you'),
          table(
            ['Level', 'What', 'Detail'],
            [
              for (final a in data.alerts)
                [
                  switch (a.level) {
                    HubLevel.urgent => 'Urgent',
                    HubLevel.today => 'Today',
                    HubLevel.later => 'When you can',
                  },
                  a.title,
                  a.detail,
                ],
            ],
          ),
        ],

        heading('Money', periodLabel),
        tiles([
          ('Collected', _rs(m.collected), _pct(m.collected, m.prevCollected)),
          ('Expenses', _rs(m.expenses), _pct(m.expenses, m.prevExpenses)),
          ('Net profit', _rs(m.net), _pct(m.net, m.prevNet)),
          ('Billed', _rs(m.billed), '${m.billedCount} invoices'),
        ]),
        if (m.byCategory.isNotEmpty) ...[
          pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
          table(
            ['Expenses by category', 'Amount'],
            [
              for (final c in m.byCategory) [c.name, _rs(c.total)],
            ],
            rightCols: [1],
          ),
        ],
        if (m.byMethod.isNotEmpty) ...[
          pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
          table(
            ['Collected by method', 'Amount'],
            [
              for (final c in m.byMethod) [c.name, _rs(c.total)],
            ],
            rightCols: [1],
          ),
        ],
        pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
        table(
          ['Owed', '0-30 days', '31-60 days', '61-90 days', 'Over 90 days'],
          [
            ['Amount', for (final b in m.owedBuckets) _rs(b)],
            ['Invoices', for (final c in m.owedBucketCounts) '$c'],
          ],
          rightCols: [1, 2, 3, 4],
        ),

        if (data.dentists.isNotEmpty) ...[
          heading('Dentists', periodLabel),
          table(
            [
              'Dentist',
              'Visits',
              'No-shows',
              'Collected',
              'Avg bill',
              'Commission',
              'Still to pay',
            ],
            [
              for (final x in data.dentists)
                [
                  x.name,
                  '${x.completed} / ${x.booked}',
                  '${x.noShows}',
                  _rs(x.collected),
                  _rs(x.avgBill),
                  _rs(x.commission),
                  x.paidKnown ? _rs(x.due > 0 ? x.due : 0) : '-',
                ],
            ],
            rightCols: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6],
          ),
          if (data.unassigned > 0)
            pw.Padding(
              padding: const pw.EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
              child: pw.Text(
                '${_rs(data.unassigned)} collected is not linked to any dentist.',
                style: st(8.5, color: muted),
              ),
            ),
        ],

        heading("What's coming", 'Estimates from the clinic\'s own records'),
        if (!f.ready)
          pw.Text(
            'Forecasts start on ${_date(f.startsOn)} - they need $kForecastMinDays '
            'days of records (${f.dataDays} so far).',
            style: st(9.5, color: muted),
          )
        else ...[
          tiles([
            (
              'This month should end at',
              _rs(f.monthMid),
              '${_rs(f.monthLow)} to ${_rs(f.monthHigh)}',
            ),
            (
              'Next 30 days in',
              _rs(f.in30),
              'usual monthly spending ${_rs(f.out30)}',
            ),
            (
              'Owed likely to come back',
              _rs(f.owedLikely),
              'of ${_rs(f.owedTotal)} owed',
            ),
            (
              'This year should end at',
              _rs(f.yearMid),
              '${_rs(f.yearLow)} to ${_rs(f.yearHigh)}',
            ),
            (
              'Regulars at risk',
              '${f.atRiskTotal}',
              'late for their usual visit',
            ),
          ]),
          pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
          table(
            ['Next 7 days', 'Booked', 'Usual', 'A usual day brings'],
            [
              for (final d in f.week)
                [
                  '${_mon[d.day.month - 1]} ${d.day.day}',
                  d.closed ? 'Closed' : '${d.booked}',
                  d.closed ? '' : d.usual.toStringAsFixed(1),
                  d.closed ? '' : _rs(d.usualMoney),
                ],
            ],
            rightCols: [1, 2, 3],
          ),
          if (f.atRisk.isNotEmpty) ...[
            pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
            table(
              ['Regular at risk', 'Phone', 'Usually every', 'Days since'],
              [
                for (final p in f.atRisk)
                  [p.name, p.phone, '${p.usualGap} days', '${p.daysSince}'],
              ],
              rightCols: [2, 3],
            ),
          ],
        ],

        heading('Watch list', periodLabel),
        tiles([
          ('Discounts', _rs(w.discountTotal), '${w.discountCount} bills'),
          ('Bills cancelled', '${w.cancelledCount}', _rs(w.cancelledTotal)),
          ('Payments removed', '${w.removedCount}', _rs(w.removedTotal)),
          ('Expenses deleted', '${w.deletedCount}', _rs(w.deletedTotal)),
          (
            'Failed sign-ins',
            '${w.failedSignIns}',
            '${w.afterHoursSignIns} outside hours',
          ),
        ]),
        if (w.changes.isNotEmpty) ...[
          pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
          table(
            ['When', 'What', 'Who', 'Amount'],
            [
              for (final c in w.changes.take(25))
                [
                  '${c.at.day} ${_mon[c.at.month - 1]} ${_time(c.at)}',
                  (c.detail.isEmpty ? c.title : '${c.title} - ${c.detail}')
                      .replaceAll('→', '->'),
                  c.who,
                  c.amount == null ? '' : _rs(c.amount!),
                ],
            ],
            rightCols: [3],
          ),
        ],
        if (w.takenBy.isNotEmpty) ...[
          pw.SizedBox(height: 10),
          table(
            ['Money taken by', 'Payments', 'Amount'],
            [
              for (final x in w.takenBy) [x.name, '${x.count}', _rs(x.total)],
            ],
            rightCols: [1, 2],
          ),
        ],

        if (data.branches.isNotEmpty) ...[
          heading('Branches', periodLabel),
          table(
            [
              'Branch',
              'Collected',
              'Expenses',
              'Net',
              'Visits',
              'No-shows',
              'Owed',
              'Last closed',
            ],
            [
              for (final b in data.branches)
                [
                  b.name,
                  _rs(b.collected),
                  _rs(b.expenses),
                  _rs(b.net),
                  '${b.completed}/${b.appts}',
                  '${b.noShows}',
                  _rs(b.owed),
                  b.lastClosed ?? 'never',
                ],
            ],
            rightCols: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6],
          ),
        ],

        heading('System health'),
        table(
          ['Check', 'Status'],
          [
            [
              'Plan',
              '${h.plan}${h.expiresAt == null ? '' : ' until ${_date(h.expiresAt!)}'}',
            ],
            ['Staff logins', '${h.users} of ${h.maxUsers}'],
            ['Branches', '${h.branches} of ${h.maxBranches}'],
            for (final dv in h.devices)
              [
                'Computer ${dv.letter}${dv.name.isEmpty ? '' : ' (${dv.name})'}',
                dv.lastSeen == null
                    ? 'never synced'
                    : 'last sync ${_date(dv.lastSeen!)} ${_time(dv.lastSeen!)}',
              ],
            for (final x in h.wa)
              [
                'WhatsApp · ${x.branch}',
                !x.on
                    ? 'off'
                    : '${x.connected ? 'connected' : 'DISCONNECTED'}'
                          '${x.failed == null ? '' : ' · ${x.failed} failed this week'}',
              ],
          ],
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
  return doc.save();
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/data/activity_log.dart
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/utils/uuids.dart';

/// Adds one line to the synced activity record (`activity_events`).
///
/// Pass [session] for something a signed-in user did, or [userName] for
/// a name typed at sign-in. Never throws — a failed log line must never
/// stop the work it describes.
Future<void> logActivity(
  AppDatabase db,
  String kind, {
  AuthSession? session,
  String? userName,
  String detail = '',
}) async {
  try {
    final clinicId = await db.currentClinicId() ?? '';
    final dev = await db.localDevice();
    final u = session == null
        ? null
        : await db.findActiveUser(session.username);
    await db
        .into(db.activityEvents)
        .insert(
          ActivityEventsCompanion.insert(
            uuid: Uuids.v4(),
            clinicId: clinicId,
            kind: kind,
            branchId: Value(session?.branchId ?? ''),
            userUuid: Value(u?.uuid ?? ''),
            userName: Value(session?.fullName ?? userName ?? ''),
            device: Value(dev.letter),
            detail: Value(detail),
          ),
        );
  } catch (_) {}
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/data/hub_pin.dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:isolate';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:pointycastle/export.dart';

/// The Detailed Reporting Hub PIN.
///
/// Stored as `pbkdf2$<iterations>$<salt>$<hash>` (PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, random
/// 16-byte salt) — never the digits. Hashing runs on a background isolate so
/// the screen never freezes while it works.
class HubPin {
  HubPin._();

  static const minLength = 4;
  static const maxLength = 6;
  static const _iterations = 60000;
  static const _scheme = 'pbkdf2';

  /// Why [pin] can't be used, or null when it is fine.
  static String? problem(String pin) {
    if (!RegExp(r'^\d{4,6}$').hasMatch(pin)) return 'Use 4 to 6 digits.';
    if (pin.split('').toSet().length == 1) {
      return 'The same digit repeated is too easy to guess.';
    }
    bool run(int step) {
      for (var i = 1; i < pin.length; i++) {
        if (pin.codeUnitAt(i) - pin.codeUnitAt(i - 1) != step) return false;
      }
      return true;
    }

    if (run(1) || run(-1)) return 'A run like 1234 is too easy to guess.';
    return null;
  }

  static Future<String> hash(String pin) {
    final salt = _randomBytes(16);
    return Isolate.run(() => _encode(pin, salt, _iterations));
  }

  static Future<bool> verify(String pin, String stored) =>
      Isolate.run(() => _check(pin, stored));

  // ── internals (top-level-safe: no closures over objects) ──

  static String _encode(String pin, Uint8List salt, int iterations) {
    final key = _derive(pin, salt, iterations);
    return '$_scheme\$$iterations\$${base64.encode(salt)}\$${base64.encode(key)}';
  }

  static bool _check(String pin, String stored) {
    final p = stored.split(r'$');
    if (p.length != 4 || p[0] != _scheme) return false;
    final it = int.tryParse(p[1]);
    if (it == null || it < 1000 || it > 2000000) return false;
    try {
      final salt = base64.decode(p[2]);
      final want = base64.decode(p[3]);
      final got = _derive(pin, salt, it);
      if (got.length != want.length) return false;
      var diff = 0; // constant time — no early exit
      for (var i = 0; i < got.length; i++) {
        diff |= got[i] ^ want[i];
      }
      return diff == 0;
    } catch (_) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  static Uint8List _derive(String pin, Uint8List salt, int iterations) {
    final kdf = PBKDF2KeyDerivator(HMac(SHA256Digest(), 64))
      ..init(Pbkdf2Parameters(salt, iterations, 32));
    return kdf.process(Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(pin)));
  }

  static Uint8List _randomBytes(int n) {
    final r = Random.secure();
    return Uint8List.fromList(List.generate(n, (_) => r.nextInt(256)));
  }
}

/// A one-time Hub PIN reset code issued by the vendor licence tool:
///
///   DRH1.<base64url(payload)>.<base64url(RSA-SHA256 signature)>
///
/// payload = {"t":"hub-pin-reset","cid":clinicId,"user":username,
///            "exp":ISO-8601 UTC,"n":nonce}
///
/// Signed with the same private key as licences, so nobody but the vendor
/// can make one. The `t` field means a licence or another vendor code can
/// never stand in for it.
class HubResetCode {
  const HubResetCode({
    required this.clinicId,
    required this.username,
    required this.expiresAt,
    required this.nonce,
  });

  static const prefix = 'DRH1';
  static const type = 'hub-pin-reset';

  final String clinicId, username, nonce;
  final DateTime expiresAt;

  /// Null when [raw] isn't a genuine code. [verify] receives the exact
  /// signed text and the signature in standard base64 — pass
  /// `LicenseVerifier().verifyCanonical`.
  static HubResetCode? parse(
    String raw,
    bool Function(String canonical, String signatureB64) verify,
  ) {
    // People paste codes out of WhatsApp with line breaks and spaces.
    final s = raw.replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s'), '');
    final parts = s.split('.');
    if (parts.length != 3 || parts[0] != prefix) return null;
    try {
      final payload = utf8.decode(
        base64Url.decode(base64Url.normalize(parts[1])),
      );
      final sig = base64Url.decode(base64Url.normalize(parts[2]));
      if (!verify(payload, base64.encode(sig))) return null;

      final j = jsonDecode(payload);
      if (j is! Map || j['t'] != type) return null;
      final cid = j['cid'], user = j['user'], exp = j['exp'], n = j['n'];
      if (cid is! String || user is! String || exp is! String || n is! String) {
        return null;
      }
      final at = DateTime.tryParse(exp);
      if (at == null || n.isEmpty) return null;
      return HubResetCode(
        clinicId: cid,
        username: user,
        expiresAt: at,
        nonce: n,
      );
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/data/hub_data.dart
import 'dart:math' as math;

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:supabase_flutter/supabase_flutter.dart' show Supabase;
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import 'hub_forecast.dart';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  What the Detailed Reporting Hub shows. Everything is read from the local
//  database (so it works offline); only the WhatsApp queue is asked of the
//  cloud, and that check is allowed to fail quietly.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

int _daysIn(int year, int month) => DateTime(year, month + 1, 0).day;

const _mo = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', //
  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
];

enum HubPreset {
  today('Today'),
  thisWeek('This week'),
  thisMonth('This month'),
  lastMonth('Last month'),
  last90('Last 90 days'),
  thisYear('This year'),
  custom('Custom range');

  const HubPreset(this.label);
  final String label;
}

/// The stretch of time Money, Dentists, Watch list and Branches look at,
/// and the stretch it is compared with. [end] and [prevEnd] are exclusive.
class HubPeriod {
  const HubPeriod._(
    this.preset,
    this.start,
    this.end,
    this.prevStart,
    this.prevEnd,
  );

  final HubPreset preset;
  final DateTime start, end, prevStart, prevEnd;

  /// Longest custom range — dentist commission is worked out month by month.
  static const maxCustomDays = 731;

  factory HubPeriod.of(HubPreset p, {DateTime? now}) {
    final n = now ?? DateTime.now();
    final d0 = DateTime(n.year, n.month, n.day);
    final d1 = d0.add(const Duration(days: 1));
    switch (p) {
      case HubPreset.today:
        // compared with the same weekday last week (a whole day each)
        return HubPeriod._(
          p,
          d0,
          d1,
          d0.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
          d1.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
        );
      case HubPreset.thisWeek:
        final mon = d0.subtract(Duration(days: d0.weekday - 1));
        return HubPeriod._(
          p,
          mon,
          d1,
          mon.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
          d1.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
        );
      case HubPreset.thisMonth:
        final start = DateTime(n.year, n.month, 1);
        final prevStart = DateTime(n.year, n.month - 1, 1);
        final day = math.min(n.day, _daysIn(prevStart.year, prevStart.month));
        return HubPeriod._(
          p,
          start,
          d1,
          prevStart,
          DateTime(prevStart.year, prevStart.month, day + 1),
        );
      case HubPreset.lastMonth:
        final start = DateTime(n.year, n.month - 1, 1);
        return HubPeriod._(
          p,
          start,
          DateTime(n.year, n.month, 1),
          DateTime(start.year, start.month - 1, 1),
          start,
        );
      case HubPreset.last90:
        final start = d1.subtract(const Duration(days: 90));
        return HubPeriod._(
          p,
          start,
          d1,
          start.subtract(const Duration(days: 90)),
          start,
        );
      case HubPreset.thisYear:
        final start = DateTime(n.year, 1, 1);
        final prevStart = DateTime(n.year - 1, 1, 1);
        return HubPeriod._(
          p,
          start,
          d1,
          prevStart,
          DateTime(
            n.year - 1,
            n.month,
            math.min(n.day, _daysIn(n.year - 1, n.month)) + 1,
          ),
        );
      case HubPreset.custom:
        return HubPeriod.range(d0, d0);
    }
  }

  /// A custom range, both days included.
  factory HubPeriod.range(DateTime from, DateTime to) {
    final a = DateTime(from.year, from.month, from.day);
    final b = DateTime(to.year, to.month, to.day).add(const Duration(days: 1));
    final len = b.difference(a);
    return HubPeriod._(HubPreset.custom, a, b, a.subtract(len), a);
  }

  static const presets = [
    HubPreset.today,
    HubPreset.thisWeek,
    HubPreset.thisMonth,
    HubPreset.lastMonth,
    HubPreset.last90,
    HubPreset.thisYear,
  ];

  int get days => end.difference(start).inDays;

  /// The calendar month this period sits in, when it is one month (or the
  /// part of this month so far). Commission payouts are monthly.
  DateTime? get month {
    final whole =
        start.day == 1 && end == DateTime(start.year, start.month + 1, 1);
    return preset == HubPreset.thisMonth ||
            preset == HubPreset.lastMonth ||
            whole
        ? DateTime(start.year, start.month, 1)
        : null;
  }

  static String _d(DateTime d, {bool year = true}) =>
      '${d.day} ${_mo[d.month - 1]}${year ? ' ${d.year}' : ''}';

  /// "1 Oct – 22 Oct 2026", "Thu 22 Oct 2026".
  String get describe {
    final last = end.subtract(const Duration(days: 1));
    if (days <= 1) return _d(start);
    final sameYear = start.year == last.year;
    return '${_d(start, year: !sameYear)} – ${_d(last)}';
  }

  /// The chip text: the preset's name, or the dates for a custom range.
  String get label => preset == HubPreset.custom ? describe : preset.label;

  /// "vs …" words for the change pills.
  String get vs => switch (preset) {
    HubPreset.today => 'same day last week',
    HubPreset.thisWeek => 'same days last week',
    HubPreset.thisMonth => 'same days last month',
    HubPreset.lastMonth => 'the month before',
    HubPreset.last90 => 'the 90 days before',
    HubPreset.thisYear => 'same days last year',
    HubPreset.custom => 'the $days days before',
  };

  @override
  bool operator ==(Object o) =>
      o is HubPeriod && o.preset == preset && o.start == start && o.end == end;

  @override
  int get hashCode => Object.hash(preset, start, end);
}

/// How finely the Money chart splits a period.
enum HubBarUnit { hour, day, week, month }

// ─────────────────────────────── models ───────────────────────────────────

class HubAppt {
  const HubAppt({
    required this.id,
    required this.patientId,
    required this.patient,
    required this.dentist,
    required this.procedure,
    required this.status,
    required this.startsAt,
    required this.minutes,
    required this.billed,
  });

  final int id, patientId, minutes;
  final String patient, dentist, procedure, status;
  final DateTime startsAt;
  final bool billed;

  DateTime get endsAt => startsAt.add(Duration(minutes: minutes));
}

enum HubLevel { urgent, today, later }

/// Where an alert's button goes when the owner leaves the Hub.
enum HubGo {
  appointments,
  billing,
  closeDay,
  expenses,
  requests,
  whatsapp,
  settings,

  /// Stays in the Hub — opens the Watch list section.
  watchList,
}

class HubAlert {
  const HubAlert({
    required this.level,
    required this.icon,
    required this.title,
    required this.detail,
    required this.action,
    required this.go,
    this.arg,
  });

  final HubLevel level;

  /// cash · wa · sync · licence · request · wait · bill · noshow · owed · payout
  final String icon;
  final String title, detail, action;
  final HubGo go;

  /// Extra target, e.g. the day key for [HubGo.closeDay].
  final String? arg;
}

class HubOwing {
  const HubOwing({
    required this.patientId,
    required this.patient,
    required this.code,
    required this.amount,
    required this.oldestDays,
    required this.invoices,
  });

  final int patientId, amount, oldestDays, invoices;
  final String patient, code;
}

class HubToday {
  const HubToday({
    required this.appts,
    required this.collected,
    required this.collectedCash,
    required this.lastWeekSameTime,
    required this.monthCollected,
    required this.lastMonthToDate,
    required this.monthExpenses,
    required this.owedTotal,
    required this.owedCount,
    required this.owedOver60,
    required this.pendingRequests,
    required this.dayStart,
    required this.dayEnd,
  });

  final List<HubAppt> appts;
  final int collected, collectedCash, lastWeekSameTime;
  final int monthCollected, lastMonthToDate, monthExpenses;
  final int owedTotal, owedCount, owedOver60;
  final int pendingRequests;

  /// Timeline window, minutes after midnight (whole hours).
  final int dayStart, dayEnd;

  int count(String status) => appts.where((a) => a.status == status).length;
  int get patients => {
    for (final a in appts)
      if (a.status != 'cancelled') a.patientId,
  }.length;
  int get monthNet => monthCollected - monthExpenses;
}

class HubMoney {
  const HubMoney({
    required this.collected,
    required this.prevCollected,
    required this.expenses,
    required this.prevExpenses,
    required this.billed,
    required this.billedCount,
    required this.bars,
    required this.unit,
    required this.byCategory,
    required this.byMethod,
    required this.owedBuckets,
    required this.owedBucketCounts,
    required this.topOwing,
  });

  final int collected, prevCollected, expenses, prevExpenses;
  final int billed, billedCount;

  /// Collected per hour / day / week / month across the period ([unit]).
  /// [future] bars are still to come (this week, month or year).
  final List<({DateTime start, int amount, bool future})> bars;
  final HubBarUnit unit;
  final List<({String name, int total})> byCategory, byMethod;

  /// Owed money by age: 0–30, 31–60, 61–90, 90+ days.
  final List<int> owedBuckets, owedBucketCounts;
  final List<HubOwing> topOwing;

  int get net => collected - expenses;
  int get prevNet => prevCollected - prevExpenses;
}

class HubDentist {
  const HubDentist({
    required this.name,
    required this.booked,
    required this.completed,
    required this.noShows,
    required this.cancelled,
    required this.collected,
    required this.percent,
    required this.commission,
    required this.paid,
    this.paidKnown = true,
  });

  final String name;
  final int booked, completed, noShows, cancelled;
  final int collected, commission, paid;
  final double percent;

  /// Payouts are monthly: what is still to pay is only known when the
  /// period is one calendar month.
  final bool paidKnown;

  int get due => paidKnown ? commission - paid : 0;
  int get avgBill => completed == 0 ? 0 : (collected / completed).round();

  /// No-shows as a share of visits that were due to happen.
  double get noShowRate {
    final due = completed + noShows;
    return due == 0 ? 0 : noShows / due;
  }
}

class HubBranch {
  const HubBranch({
    required this.uuid,
    required this.name,
    required this.collected,
    required this.expenses,
    required this.appts,
    required this.completed,
    required this.noShows,
    required this.owed,
    required this.lastClosed,
    required this.waOn,
    required this.waConnected,
  });

  final String uuid, name;
  final int collected, expenses, appts, completed, noShows, owed;

  /// Last cash-closing day as yyyy-mm-dd, null when never closed.
  final String? lastClosed;
  final bool waOn, waConnected;

  int get net => collected - expenses;
}

class HubDevice {
  const HubDevice({
    required this.letter,
    required this.name,
    required this.platform,
    required this.lastSeen,
    required this.isThis,
  });

  final String letter, name, platform;
  final DateTime? lastSeen;
  final bool isThis;
}

class HubWa {
  const HubWa({
    required this.branch,
    required this.on,
    required this.connected,
    required this.failed,
    required this.waiting,
  });

  final String branch;
  final bool on, connected;

  /// From the cloud queue, last 7 days. Null when it couldn't be checked.
  final int? failed, waiting;
}

class HubHealth {
  const HubHealth({
    required this.devices,
    required this.lastSyncHere,
    required this.skipped,
    required this.wa,
    required this.waChecked,
    required this.cloud,
    required this.plan,
    required this.expiresAt,
    required this.users,
    required this.maxUsers,
    required this.branches,
    required this.maxBranches,
  });

  final List<HubDevice> devices;
  final DateTime? lastSyncHere;
  final int skipped;
  final List<HubWa> wa;
  final bool waChecked, cloud;
  final String plan;
  final DateTime? expiresAt;
  final int users, maxUsers, branches, maxBranches;

  int? get licenceDaysLeft => expiresAt == null
      ? null
      : expiresAt!.difference(DateTime.now()).inHours ~/ 24;

  /// Things on this page that need doing.
  int get issues {
    var n = 0;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    for (final d in devices) {
      if (!d.isThis &&
          (d.lastSeen == null || now.difference(d.lastSeen!).inDays >= 3)) {
        n++;
      }
    }
    if (cloud &&
        (lastSyncHere == null || now.difference(lastSyncHere!).inHours >= 24)) {
      n++;
    }
    if (skipped > 0) n++;
    for (final w in wa) {
      if (w.on && !w.connected) n++;
      if ((w.failed ?? 0) > 0) n++;
    }
    final left = licenceDaysLeft;
    if (left != null && left <= 14) n++;
    return n;
  }
}

class HubData {
  const HubData({
    required this.loadedAt,
    required this.today,
    required this.money,
    required this.dentists,
    required this.unassigned,
    required this.commissionDue,
    required this.branches,
    required this.health,
    required this.alerts,
    required this.forecast,
    required this.watch,
  });

  final DateTime loadedAt;
  final HubForecast forecast;

  /// For the chosen period.
  final HubWatch watch;
  final HubToday today;
  final HubMoney money;
  final List<HubDentist> dentists;

  /// Collected in the period that couldn't be linked to any dentist.
  final int unassigned;
  final int commissionDue;

  /// Side by side — empty for a single branch or a branch-scoped view.
  final List<HubBranch> branches;
  final HubHealth health;
  final List<HubAlert> alerts;

  int get urgent => alerts.where((a) => a.level == HubLevel.urgent).length;
}

// ─────────────────────────────── loader ───────────────────────────────────

/// The loading screen's checklist, in the order [loadHubData] works.
const hubLoadSteps = [
  "Reading today's appointments",
  'Adding up money in and out',
  'Working out each dentist',
  'Looking ahead from your history',
  'Checking the watch list',
  'Comparing branches',
  'Checking computers, WhatsApp and licence',
];

/// Loads everything the Hub shows for [branchId] (null = all branches).
/// [onStep] is called with the index of the step about to start.
Future<HubData> loadHubData({
  required AppDatabase db,
  required CashRepository cash,
  required List<Branch> allBranches,
  required License? licence,
  required Entitlements ent,
  required String? branchId,
  required HubPeriod period,
  required bool showPayouts,
  void Function(int step)? onStep,
}) async {
  final now = DateTime.now();
  final d0 = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
  final d1 = d0.add(const Duration(days: 1));
  final monthStart = DateTime(now.year, now.month, 1);
  final r = period;

  Expression<bool> inBranch(GeneratedColumn<String> c) =>
      branchId == null ? const Constant(true) : c.equals(branchId);

  final branches = branchId == null
      ? allBranches
      : allBranches.where((b) => b.uuid == branchId).toList();

  Future<List<InvoicePaymentRow>> payments(DateTime a, DateTime b) =>
      (db.select(db.invoicePayments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.paidAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(a) &
                t.paidAt.isSmallerThanValue(b) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();

  Future<List<ExpenseRow>> expenses(DateTime a, DateTime b) =>
      (db.select(db.expenses)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.paidAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(a) &
                t.paidAt.isSmallerThanValue(b) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();

  int sumPay(Iterable<InvoicePaymentRow> l) =>
      l.fold(0, (s, p) => s + p.amount);
  int sumExp(Iterable<ExpenseRow> l) => l.fold(0, (s, e) => s + e.amount);
  bool isCash(String m) => m.trim().toLowerCase() == 'cash';

  // ── 1. today ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  onStep?.call(0);
  final apptRows =
      await (db.select(db.appointments).join([
              innerJoin(
                db.patients,
                db.patients.id.equalsExp(db.appointments.patientId),
              ),
            ])
            ..where(
              db.appointments.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                  db.appointments.startsAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(d0) &
                  db.appointments.startsAt.isSmallerThanValue(d1) &
                  inBranch(db.appointments.branchId),
            )
            ..orderBy([OrderingTerm.asc(db.appointments.startsAt)]))
          .get();
  final appts = [
    for (final row in apptRows)
      () {
        final a = row.readTable(db.appointments);
        final p = row.readTable(db.patients);
        return HubAppt(
          id: a.id,
          patientId: a.patientId,
          patient: p.fullName,
          dentist: a.dentist.trim(),
          procedure: a.procedure,
          status: a.status,
          startsAt: a.startsAt,
          minutes: a.durationMin <= 0 ? 30 : a.durationMin,
          billed: a.billed,
        );
      }(),
  ];

  final todayPays = await payments(d0, d1);
  final lastWeekPays = await payments(
    d0.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
    now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)),
  );
  final mtdPays = await payments(monthStart, d1);
  final lmStart = DateTime(now.year, now.month - 1, 1);
  final lmDay = math.min(now.day, _daysIn(lmStart.year, lmStart.month));
  final lmPays = await payments(
    lmStart,
    DateTime(lmStart.year, lmStart.month, lmDay + 1),
  );
  final mtdExp = await expenses(monthStart, d1);

  // Everything still owed, whatever the period — age matters, not the month.
  final owedRows =
      await (db.select(db.invoices).join([
            innerJoin(
              db.patients,
              db.patients.id.equalsExp(db.invoices.patientId),
            ),
          ])..where(
            db.invoices.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                db.invoices.status.equals('cancelled').not() &
                db.invoices.total.isBiggerThan(db.invoices.amountPaid) &
                inBranch(db.invoices.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final owed = [
    for (final row in owedRows)
      () {
        final i = row.readTable(db.invoices);
        final p = row.readTable(db.patients);
        return (
          patientId: p.id,
          patient: p.fullName,
          code: p.code,
          branch: i.branchId ?? '',
          due: i.total - i.amountPaid,
          days: d0
              .difference(
                DateTime(i.issuedAt.year, i.issuedAt.month, i.issuedAt.day),
              )
              .inDays,
        );
      }(),
  ];
  final owedTotal = owed.fold<int>(0, (s, o) => s + o.due);
  final owedOver60 = owed
      .where((o) => o.days > 60)
      .fold<int>(0, (s, o) => s + o.due);

  final reqCount = countAll();
  final pendingRequests =
      (await (db.selectOnly(db.bookingRequests)
                ..addColumns([reqCount])
                ..where(
                  db.bookingRequests.status.equals('pending') &
                      inBranch(db.bookingRequests.branchId),
                ))
              .getSingle())
          .read(reqCount) ??
      0;

  // timeline window — branch hours, widened to fit every visit
  var open = branches.isEmpty
      ? 9 * 60
      : branches.map((b) => b.openMinutes).reduce(math.min);
  var close = branches.isEmpty
      ? 21 * 60
      : branches.map((b) => b.closeMinutes).reduce(math.max);
  for (final a in appts) {
    final s = a.startsAt.hour * 60 + a.startsAt.minute;
    open = math.min(open, s);
    close = math.max(close, s + a.minutes);
  }
  open = (open ~/ 60) * 60;
  close = math.min(24 * 60, ((close + 59) ~/ 60) * 60);
  if (close - open < 4 * 60) close = math.min(24 * 60, open + 4 * 60);

  final today = HubToday(
    appts: appts,
    collected: sumPay(todayPays),
    collectedCash: sumPay(todayPays.where((p) => isCash(p.method))),
    lastWeekSameTime: sumPay(lastWeekPays),
    monthCollected: sumPay(mtdPays),
    lastMonthToDate: sumPay(lmPays),
    monthExpenses: sumExp(mtdExp),
    owedTotal: owedTotal,
    owedCount: owed.length,
    owedOver60: owedOver60,
    pendingRequests: pendingRequests,
    dayStart: open,
    dayEnd: close,
  );

  // ── 2. money for the period ─────────────────────────────────────────────
  onStep?.call(1);
  final pays = await payments(r.start, r.end);
  final prevPays = await payments(r.prevStart, r.prevEnd);
  final exps = await expenses(r.start, r.end);
  final prevExps = await expenses(r.prevStart, r.prevEnd);

  final billedRows =
      await (db.select(db.invoices)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.equals('cancelled').not() &
                t.issuedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(r.start) &
                t.issuedAt.isSmallerThanValue(r.end) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();

  // Chart buckets. A running week / month / year is drawn to its end so
  // the days still to come show as empty bars.
  final span = r.end.difference(r.start).inDays;
  final unit = span <= 1
      ? HubBarUnit.hour
      : span <= 62
      ? HubBarUnit.day
      : span <= 190
      ? HubBarUnit.week
      : HubBarUnit.month;
  final barEnd = switch (r.preset) {
    HubPreset.thisWeek => r.start.add(const Duration(days: 7)),
    HubPreset.thisMonth => DateTime(r.start.year, r.start.month + 1, 1),
    HubPreset.thisYear => DateTime(r.start.year + 1, 1, 1),
    _ => r.end,
  };
  DateTime next(DateTime t) => switch (unit) {
    HubBarUnit.hour => t.add(const Duration(hours: 1)),
    HubBarUnit.day => DateTime(t.year, t.month, t.day + 1),
    HubBarUnit.week => DateTime(t.year, t.month, t.day + 7),
    HubBarUnit.month => DateTime(t.year, t.month + 1, 1),
  };
  final firstBar = unit == HubBarUnit.month
      ? DateTime(r.start.year, r.start.month, 1)
      : r.start;
  final starts = <DateTime>[];
  for (var t = firstBar; t.isBefore(barEnd); t = next(t)) {
    starts.add(t);
  }
  final amounts = List<int>.filled(starts.length, 0);
  for (final p in pays) {
    // last bar that starts at or before the payment
    var lo = 0, hi = starts.length - 1, at = -1;
    while (lo <= hi) {
      final mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
      if (!starts[mid].isAfter(p.paidAt)) {
        at = mid;
        lo = mid + 1;
      } else {
        hi = mid - 1;
      }
    }
    if (at >= 0) amounts[at] += p.amount;
  }
  final bars = [
    for (var i = 0; i < starts.length; i++)
      (
        start: starts[i],
        amount: amounts[i],
        future: unit == HubBarUnit.hour
            ? starts[i].isAfter(now)
            : !starts[i].isBefore(d1),
      ),
  ];

  final catNames = {
    for (final l in await db.lookups('expense_category')) l.uuid: l.name,
  };
  final byCat = <String, int>{};
  for (final e in exps) {
    final n = catNames[e.categoryUuid] ?? 'Other';
    byCat[n] = (byCat[n] ?? 0) + e.amount;
  }
  final byMethod = <String, int>{};
  for (final p in pays) {
    final m = p.method.trim().isEmpty ? 'Cash' : p.method.trim();
    byMethod[m] = (byMethod[m] ?? 0) + p.amount;
  }
  List<({String name, int total})> sorted(Map<String, int> m) =>
      [for (final e in m.entries) (name: e.key, total: e.value)]
        ..sort((a, b) => b.total.compareTo(a.total));

  final buckets = List<int>.filled(4, 0), bucketCounts = List<int>.filled(4, 0);
  final byPatient = <int, HubOwing>{};
  for (final o in owed) {
    final b = o.days <= 30
        ? 0
        : o.days <= 60
        ? 1
        : o.days <= 90
        ? 2
        : 3;
    buckets[b] += o.due;
    bucketCounts[b]++;
    final prev = byPatient[o.patientId];
    byPatient[o.patientId] = HubOwing(
      patientId: o.patientId,
      patient: o.patient,
      code: o.code,
      amount: (prev?.amount ?? 0) + o.due,
      oldestDays: math.max(prev?.oldestDays ?? 0, o.days),
      invoices: (prev?.invoices ?? 0) + 1,
    );
  }
  final topOwing = byPatient.values.toList()
    ..sort((a, b) => b.amount.compareTo(a.amount));

  final money = HubMoney(
    collected: sumPay(pays),
    prevCollected: sumPay(prevPays),
    expenses: sumExp(exps),
    prevExpenses: sumExp(prevExps),
    billed: billedRows.fold(0, (s, i) => s + i.total),
    billedCount: billedRows.length,
    bars: bars,
    unit: unit,
    byCategory: sorted(byCat),
    byMethod: sorted(byMethod),
    owedBuckets: buckets,
    owedBucketCounts: bucketCounts,
    topOwing: topOwing.take(6).toList(),
  );

  // ── 3. dentists ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  onStep?.call(2);
  final periodAppts =
      await (db.select(db.appointments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.startsAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(r.start) &
                t.startsAt.isSmallerThanValue(r.end) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  // Commission is worked out per calendar month; a longer or shorter
  // period adds up the payments that fall inside it, month by month.
  final oneMonth = r.month;
  final sheets = <CommissionSheet>[];
  for (
    var m = DateTime(r.start.year, r.start.month, 1);
    m.isBefore(r.end);
    m = DateTime(m.year, m.month + 1, 1)
  ) {
    sheets.add(await cash.commission(m, branchId));
  }
  bool inRange(DateTime t) => !t.isBefore(r.start) && t.isBefore(r.end);
  final merged =
      <String, ({String name, int collected, double percent, int paid})>{};
  var unassigned = 0;
  for (final sh in sheets) {
    for (final l in sh.lines) {
      final got = l.payments
          .where((x) => inRange(x.paidAt))
          .fold<int>(0, (s, x) => s + x.amount);
      if (l.unassigned) {
        unassigned += got;
        continue;
      }
      final k = dentistKey(l.dentist);
      final prev = merged[k];
      merged[k] = (
        name: prev?.name ?? l.dentist,
        collected: (prev?.collected ?? 0) + got,
        percent: l.percent,
        paid: (prev?.paid ?? 0) + l.paid,
      );
    }
  }

  final visitStats =
      <String, ({String name, int booked, int done, int noShow, int cancel})>{};
  for (final a in periodAppts) {
    final name = a.dentist.trim();
    if (name.isEmpty) continue;
    final k = dentistKey(name);
    final s =
        visitStats[k] ?? (name: name, booked: 0, done: 0, noShow: 0, cancel: 0);
    visitStats[k] = (
      name: s.name,
      booked: s.booked + 1,
      done: s.done + (a.status == 'completed' ? 1 : 0),
      noShow: s.noShow + (a.status == 'noShow' ? 1 : 0),
      cancel: s.cancel + (a.status == 'cancelled' ? 1 : 0),
    );
  }
  final dentistMap = <String, HubDentist>{};
  for (final e in merged.entries) {
    final l = e.value;
    final v = visitStats.remove(e.key);
    if (l.collected == 0 && v == null) continue;
    dentistMap[e.key] = HubDentist(
      name: l.name,
      booked: v?.booked ?? 0,
      completed: v?.done ?? 0,
      noShows: v?.noShow ?? 0,
      cancelled: v?.cancel ?? 0,
      collected: l.collected,
      percent: l.percent,
      commission: (l.collected * l.percent / 100).round(),
      paid: oneMonth == null ? 0 : l.paid,
      paidKnown: oneMonth != null,
    );
  }
  for (final e in visitStats.entries) {
    dentistMap[e.key] = HubDentist(
      name: e.value.name,
      booked: e.value.booked,
      completed: e.value.done,
      noShows: e.value.noShow,
      cancelled: e.value.cancel,
      collected: 0,
      percent: 0,
      commission: 0,
      paid: 0,
    );
  }
  final dentists = dentistMap.values.toList()
    ..sort((a, b) {
      final c = b.collected.compareTo(a.collected);
      return c != 0 ? c : b.booked.compareTo(a.booked);
    });

  // ── 4. what's coming ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  onStep?.call(3);
  final forecast = await loadHubForecast(
    db: db,
    branches: branches,
    branchId: branchId,
    owed: [for (final o in owed) (due: o.due, days: o.days)],
  );

  // ── 5. watch list ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  onStep?.call(4);
  final watch = await loadHubWatch(
    db: db,
    branches: branches,
    branchId: branchId,
    start: r.start,
    end: r.end,
  );
  final weekAgo = d0.subtract(const Duration(days: 7));
  final failedWeek =
      await (db.select(db.activityEvents)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.kind.equals('sign_in_failed') &
                t.happenedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(weekAgo),
          ))
          .get();

  // ── 6. branches side by side ────────────────────────────────────────────
  onStep?.call(5);
  final branchStats = <HubBranch>[];
  if (branchId == null && allBranches.length > 1) {
    for (final b in allBranches) {
      final last =
          await (db.select(db.cashClosings)
                ..where(
                  (t) => t.isDeleted.equals(false) & t.branchId.equals(b.uuid),
                )
                ..orderBy([(t) => OrderingTerm.desc(t.day)])
                ..limit(1))
              .getSingleOrNull();
      final bAppts = periodAppts.where((a) => a.branchId == b.uuid);
      branchStats.add(
        HubBranch(
          uuid: b.uuid,
          name: b.name,
          collected: sumPay(pays.where((p) => p.branchId == b.uuid)),
          expenses: sumExp(exps.where((e) => e.branchId == b.uuid)),
