          appts: bAppts.where((a) => a.status != 'cancelled').length,
          completed: bAppts.where((a) => a.status == 'completed').length,
          noShows: bAppts.where((a) => a.status == 'noShow').length,
          owed: owed
              .where((o) => o.branch == b.uuid)
              .fold(0, (s, o) => s + o.due),
          lastClosed: last?.day,
          waOn: b.waEnabled,
          waConnected: b.qrConnected || b.officialConnected,
        ),
      );
    }
  }

  // ── 7. system health ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  onStep?.call(6);
  final here = await db.localDevice();
  final devices = [
    for (final d in await db.activeDevices())
      HubDevice(
        letter: d.letter,
        name: d.name,
        platform: d.platform,
        lastSeen: d.lastSeenAt,
        isThis: d.uuid == here.uuid,
      ),
  ];
  final skipped =
      int.tryParse(await db.getSetting('sync_skipped_count') ?? '') ?? 0;

  final failed = <String, int>{}, waiting = <String, int>{};
  var waChecked = false;
  if (ent.cloud && branches.any((b) => b.waEnabled)) {
    try {
      var q = Supabase.instance.client
          .from('wa_outbox')
          .select('status, branch_id')
          .gte(
            'created_at',
            now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)).toUtc().toIso8601String(),
          )
          .neq('status', 'sent');
      if (branchId != null) q = q.eq('branch_id', branchId);
      final rows = await q.limit(1000).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 6));
      for (final row in rows) {
        final b = '${row['branch_id'] ?? ''}';
        final s = '${row['status'] ?? ''}';
        if (s == 'failed') failed[b] = (failed[b] ?? 0) + 1;
        if (s == 'pending' || s == 'sending') {
          waiting[b] = (waiting[b] ?? 0) + 1;
        }
      }
      waChecked = true;
    } catch (_) {
      // offline or the cloud is slow — the page says it couldn't check
    }
  }
  final wa = [
    for (final b in branches)
      HubWa(
        branch: b.name,
        on: b.waEnabled,
        connected: b.qrConnected || b.officialConnected,
        failed: waChecked ? (failed[b.uuid] ?? 0) : null,
        waiting: waChecked ? (waiting[b.uuid] ?? 0) : null,
      ),
  ];

  final health = HubHealth(
    devices: devices,
    lastSyncHere: await db.lastSyncAt(),
    skipped: skipped,
    wa: wa,
    waChecked: waChecked,
    cloud: ent.cloud,
    plan: ent.tierLabel,
    expiresAt: licence?.expiresAt,
    users: await db.userCount(),
    maxUsers: ent.maxUsers,
    branches: allBranches.length,
    maxBranches: ent.maxBranches,
  );

  final unclosed = await cash.unclosedDays(before: d0, branchId: branchId);

  return HubData(
    loadedAt: DateTime.now(),
    today: today,
    money: money,
    dentists: dentists,
    unassigned: unassigned,
    commissionDue: dentists.fold(0, (s, x) => s + (x.due > 0 ? x.due : 0)),
    branches: branchStats,
    health: health,
    forecast: forecast,
    watch: watch,
    alerts: _alerts(
      now: now,
      today: today,
      health: health,
      unclosed: unclosed,
      owed60Count: owed.where((o) => o.days > 60).length,
      commissionDue: showPayouts && period.preset == HubPreset.thisMonth
          ? dentists.fold(0, (s, x) => s + (x.due > 0 ? x.due : 0))
          : 0,
      failedSignIns: failedWeek.length,
    ),
  );
}

String _n(int n, String one, [String? many]) =>
    '$n ${n == 1 ? one : (many ?? '${one}s')}';

String _rs(int v) =>
    'Rs ${v.toString().replaceAllMapped(RegExp(r'(\d)(?=(\d{3})+$)'), (m) => '${m[1]},')}';

List<HubAlert> _alerts({
  required DateTime now,
  required HubToday today,
  required HubHealth health,
  required List<({DateTime day, int cashPayments})> unclosed,
  required int owed60Count,
  required int commissionDue,
  required int failedSignIns,
}) {
  final out = <HubAlert>[];

  // ── urgent ──
  if (unclosed.isNotEmpty) {
    final first = unclosed.first.day;
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.urgent,
        icon: 'cash',
        title: unclosed.length == 1
            ? 'A day was not closed'
            : '${unclosed.length} days were not closed',
        detail:
            'Cash was taken but never counted · oldest '
            '${first.day}/${first.month}/${first.year}',
        action: 'Close day',
        go: HubGo.closeDay,
        arg: dayKey(first),
      ),
    );
  }
  for (final w in health.wa) {
    if (w.on && !w.connected) {
      out.add(
        HubAlert(
          level: HubLevel.urgent,
          icon: 'wa',
          title: 'WhatsApp is disconnected · ${w.branch}',
          detail: 'Reminders and prescriptions are not going out',
          action: 'Reconnect',
          go: HubGo.whatsapp,
        ),
      );
    }
  }
  final failed = health.wa.fold<int>(0, (s, w) => s + (w.failed ?? 0));
  if (failed > 0) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.urgent,
        icon: 'wa',
        title: '${_n(failed, 'WhatsApp message')} failed this week',
        detail: 'Open the message log to retry them',
        action: 'Open WhatsApp',
        go: HubGo.whatsapp,
      ),
    );
  }
  for (final d in health.devices) {
    if (d.isThis) continue;
    final days = d.lastSeen == null ? null : now.difference(d.lastSeen!).inDays;
    if (days == null || days >= 3) {
      out.add(
        HubAlert(
          level: HubLevel.urgent,
          icon: 'sync',
          title:
              'Computer ${d.letter}${d.name.isEmpty ? '' : ' (${d.name})'} '
              'is not syncing',
          detail: days == null
              ? 'It has never synced'
              : 'Last synced ${_n(days, 'day')} ago — its work may be missing',
          action: 'See computers',
          go: HubGo.settings,
        ),
      );
    }
  }
  final left = health.licenceDaysLeft;
  if (left != null && left <= 14) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.urgent,
        icon: 'licence',
        title: left <= 0
            ? 'Your licence ends today'
            : 'Your licence ends in ${_n(left, 'day')}',
        detail: 'Contact Inover Studio to renew before it locks',
        action: 'Licence',
        go: HubGo.settings,
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── today ──
  if (failedSignIns >= 3) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.today,
        icon: 'lock',
        title: '${_n(failedSignIns, 'failed sign-in')} this week',
        detail: 'Wrong passwords — see who in the Watch list',
        action: 'Watch list',
        go: HubGo.watchList,
      ),
    );
  }
  if (today.pendingRequests > 0) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.today,
        icon: 'request',
        title: '${_n(today.pendingRequests, 'booking request')} waiting',
        detail: 'Patients asked for a slot from the app',
        action: 'Review',
        go: HubGo.requests,
      ),
    );
  }
  final longWait = today.appts
      .where(
        (a) =>
            a.status == 'waiting' &&
            now.difference(a.startsAt) > const Duration(minutes: 20),
      )
      .toList();
  if (longWait.isNotEmpty) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.today,
        icon: 'wait',
        title: '${_n(longWait.length, 'patient')} waiting over 20 minutes',
        detail: longWait.take(3).map((a) => a.patient).join(', '),
        action: 'Appointments',
        go: HubGo.appointments,
      ),
    );
  }
  final unbilled = today.appts
      .where((a) => a.status == 'completed' && !a.billed)
      .toList();
  if (unbilled.isNotEmpty) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.today,
        icon: 'bill',
        title: '${_n(unbilled.length, 'finished visit')} not billed',
        detail: unbilled.take(3).map((a) => a.patient).join(', '),
        action: 'Bill now',
        go: HubGo.appointments,
      ),
    );
  }
  final noShows = today.count('noShow');
  if (noShows > 0) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.today,
        icon: 'noshow',
        title: '${_n(noShows, 'no-show')} today',
        detail: 'A quick call today often rebooks them',
        action: 'Appointments',
        go: HubGo.appointments,
      ),
    );
  }

  // ── when you can ──
  if (today.owedOver60 > 0) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.later,
        icon: 'owed',
        title: '${_rs(today.owedOver60)} owed for over 60 days',
        detail:
            '${_n(owed60Count, 'invoice')} — older debts get harder to collect',
        action: 'Billing',
        go: HubGo.billing,
      ),
    );
  }
  if (commissionDue > 0) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.later,
        icon: 'payout',
        title: '${_rs(commissionDue)} commission due to dentists',
        detail: 'For this month so far',
        action: 'Payouts',
        go: HubGo.expenses,
      ),
    );
  }
  if (health.skipped > 0) {
    out.add(
      HubAlert(
        level: HubLevel.later,
        icon: 'sync',
        title: '${_n(health.skipped, 'record')} did not sync',
        detail: 'They stay on this computer until fixed',
        action: 'Settings',
        go: HubGo.settings,
      ),
    );
  }
  return out;
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/data/hub_forecast.dart
import 'dart:math' as math;

import 'package:drift/drift.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart' show kDebugMode;
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  What's coming — every estimate is worked out from this clinic's own
//  history (no outside numbers), and only once there are 60 days of it.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

/// Days of a clinic's own data needed before anything is forecast.
///
/// While developing (`flutter run`, debug build) it is 14, so the page can
/// be checked on a test clinic. Clinics always run release builds → 60.
const kForecastMinDays = kDebugMode ? 14 : 60;

class HubDayAhead {
  const HubDayAhead({
    required this.day,
    required this.closed,
    required this.booked,
    required this.usual,
    required this.usualMoney,
  });

  final DateTime day;
  final bool closed;
  final int booked;

  /// Average visits on this weekday over the last 8 weeks.
  final double usual;

  /// What this weekday usually brings in.
  final int usualMoney;

  /// Booked well under a usual day — room to fill.
  bool get light => !closed && usual >= 2 && booked < usual * .6;
}

class HubAtRisk {
  const HubAtRisk({
    required this.patientId,
    required this.name,
    required this.code,
    required this.phone,
    required this.visits,
    required this.usualGap,
    required this.daysSince,
  });

  final int patientId, visits, usualGap, daysSince;
  final String name, code, phone;
  int get overdue => daysSince - usualGap;
}

class HubOwedBack {
  const HubOwedBack(this.label, this.owed, this.rate);
  final String label;
  final int owed;

  /// Share of this kind of debt that past bills show does get paid.
  final double rate;
  int get likely => (owed * rate).round();
}

class HubForecast {
  const HubForecast({
    required this.ready,
    required this.dataDays,
    required this.startsOn,
    this.confidence = 0,
    this.monthSoFar = 0,
    this.monthLow = 0,
    this.monthMid = 0,
    this.monthHigh = 0,
    this.lastMonthTotal = 0,
    this.cumulative = const [],
    this.projected = const [],
    this.week = const [],
    this.in30 = 0,
    this.out30 = 0,
    this.expenseMonths = 0,
    this.owedBack = const [],
    this.owedHistory = 0,
    this.atRisk = const [],
    this.atRiskTotal = 0,
    this.heat = const [],
    this.heatFrom = 9,
    this.heatTo = 21,
    this.months = const [],
    this.monthIndex = 0,
    this.yearLow = 0,
    this.yearMid = 0,
    this.yearHigh = 0,
    this.lastYearTotal = 0,
    this.weekdayMean = const [],
    this.weekdayLow = const [],
    this.weekdayHigh = const [],
    this.weekdayVisits = const [],
    this.closedWeekdays = const {},
    this.todaySoFar = 0,
    this.monthSpread = 0,
  });

  /// False until [kForecastMinDays] of data exist.
  final bool ready;
  final int dataDays;
  final DateTime startsOn;

  /// 1–5: how far the numbers can be trusted (more history, steadier days).
  final int confidence;

  // ── month end ──
  final int monthSoFar, monthLow, monthMid, monthHigh, lastMonthTotal;

  /// Collected so far, running total by day of this month (index = day-1).
  final List<int> cumulative;

  /// Expected running total for the rest of the month (index = day-1;
  /// 0 for days already past).
  final List<int> projected;

  // ── next 7 days ──
  final List<HubDayAhead> week;

  // ── next 30 days ──
  final int in30, out30, expenseMonths;

  // ── owed money coming back ──
  final List<HubOwedBack> owedBack;

  /// Bills the recovery rates were learned from (0 = not enough history).
  final int owedHistory;

  // ── regulars at risk ──
  final List<HubAtRisk> atRisk;
  final int atRiskTotal;

  // ── chairs: average bookings, [weekday 0=Mon][hour - heatFrom] ──
  final List<List<double>> heat;
  final int heatFrom, heatTo;

  // ── year ahead: 12 months, actual up to [monthIndex], expected after ──
  final List<int> months;
  final int monthIndex;
  final int yearLow, yearMid, yearHigh, lastYearTotal;

  // ── the pace everything above is built from (index 0 = Monday) ──
  final List<double> weekdayMean, weekdayLow, weekdayHigh, weekdayVisits;
  final Set<int> closedWeekdays;
  final int todaySoFar;

  /// How much a month usually differs from the average (standard deviation).
  final double monthSpread;

  int get owedLikely => owedBack.fold(0, (s, o) => s + o.likely);
  int get owedTotal => owedBack.fold(0, (s, o) => s + o.owed);
  int get net30 => in30 - out30;
}

// ─────────────────────────────── helpers ──────────────────────────────────

DateTime _day(DateTime t) => DateTime(t.year, t.month, t.day);
int _daysIn(int y, int m) => DateTime(y, m + 1, 0).day;
String _key(DateTime d) => '${d.year}-${d.month}-${d.day}';

double _pct(List<int> sorted, double p) {
  if (sorted.isEmpty) return 0;
  final i = (sorted.length - 1) * p;
  final lo = i.floor(), hi = i.ceil();
  return sorted[lo] + (sorted[hi] - sorted[lo]) * (i - lo);
}

double _mean(Iterable<num> xs) =>
    xs.isEmpty ? 0 : xs.fold<double>(0, (s, x) => s + x) / xs.length;

double _sd(List<num> xs) {
  if (xs.length < 2) return 0;
  final m = _mean(xs);
  return math.sqrt(
    xs.fold<double>(0, (s, x) => s + (x - m) * (x - m)) / (xs.length - 1),
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────── horizons ─────────────────────────────────

enum HubHorizon {
  today('Today', 1),
  week('Week', 7),
  month('Month', 30),
  months6('6 months', 182),
  year1('1 year', 365),
  year2('2 years', 730),
  year3('3 years', 1095),
  year4('4 years', 1461),
  year5('5 years', 1826);

  const HubHorizon(this.label, this.days);
  final String label;
  final int days;
}

class HubOutlook {
  const HubOutlook({
    required this.low,
    required this.mid,
    required this.high,
    required this.visits,
    required this.spend,
    required this.openDays,
    required this.confidence,
  });

  final int low, mid, high, visits, spend, openDays, confidence;
  int get net => mid - spend;
}

/// Money expected from today over [h], at the clinic's recent pace.
HubOutlook hubOutlook(HubForecast f, HubHorizon h) {
  final now = DateTime.now();
  final d0 = DateTime(now.year, now.month, now.day);
  var mid = 0.0, low = 0.0, high = 0.0, visits = 0.0;
  var open = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < h.days; i++) {
    final d = DateTime(d0.year, d0.month, d0.day + i);
    if (f.closedWeekdays.contains(d.weekday)) continue;
    open++;
    final w = d.weekday - 1;
    if (i == 0) {
      // today counts as whichever is more: what came in already or a usual day
      mid += math.max(f.todaySoFar.toDouble(), f.weekdayMean[w]);
      low += math.max(f.todaySoFar.toDouble(), f.weekdayLow[w]);
      high += math.max(f.todaySoFar.toDouble(), f.weekdayHigh[w]);
    } else {
      mid += f.weekdayMean[w];
      low += f.weekdayLow[w];
      high += f.weekdayHigh[w];
    }
    visits += f.weekdayVisits[w];
  }
  final months = h.days / 30.44;
  // Over months, good and bad weeks even out: the range grows with how
  // much whole months vary, not day by day.
  if (h.days > 31 && f.monthSpread > 0) {
    final band = f.monthSpread * math.sqrt(months);
    low = math.max(0.0, mid - band);
    high = mid + band;
  }
  return HubOutlook(
    low: low.round(),
    mid: mid.round(),
    high: high.round(),
    visits: visits.round(),
    spend: (f.out30 * months).round(),
    openDays: open,
    confidence:
        (f.confidence - (h.days >= 365 ? 1 : 0) - (h.days >= 1095 ? 1 : 0))
            .clamp(1, 5),
  );
}

// ─────────────────────────────── loader ───────────────────────────────────

Future<HubForecast> loadHubForecast({
  required AppDatabase db,
  required List<Branch> branches,
  required String? branchId,
  required List<({int due, int days})> owed,
}) async {
  final now = DateTime.now();
  final today = _day(now);

  Expression<bool> inBranch(GeneratedColumn<String> c) =>
      branchId == null ? const Constant(true) : c.equals(branchId);

  // ── how much history is there? ──
  final firstPay =
      await (db.select(db.invoicePayments)
            ..where((t) => t.isDeleted.equals(false) & inBranch(t.branchId))
            ..orderBy([(t) => OrderingTerm.asc(t.paidAt)])
            ..limit(1))
          .getSingleOrNull();
  final firstAppt =
      await (db.select(db.appointments)
            ..where((t) => t.isDeleted.equals(false) & inBranch(t.branchId))
            ..orderBy([(t) => OrderingTerm.asc(t.startsAt)])
            ..limit(1))
          .getSingleOrNull();
  final firsts = [
    if (firstPay != null) firstPay.paidAt,
    if (firstAppt != null) firstAppt.startsAt,
  ];
  final first = firsts.isEmpty
      ? today
      : _day(firsts.reduce((a, b) => a.isBefore(b) ? a : b));
  final dataDays = today.difference(first).inDays.clamp(0, 100000);
  final startsOn = first.add(const Duration(days: kForecastMinDays));
  if (dataDays < kForecastMinDays) {
    return HubForecast(ready: false, dataDays: dataDays, startsOn: startsOn);
  }

  // A weekday counts as closed only when every branch in view is closed.
  final closedWeekdays = <int>{};
  if (branches.isNotEmpty) {
    for (var w = 1; w <= 7; w++) {
      final all = branches.every(
        (b) => b.closedDays
            .split(',')
            .map((s) => int.tryParse(s.trim()))
            .contains(w),
      );
      if (all) closedWeekdays.add(w);
    }
  }
  bool closed(DateTime d) => closedWeekdays.contains(d.weekday);

  // ── payments: from 1 Jan last year (year-on-year) ──
  final since = DateTime(now.year - 1, 1, 1);
  final pays =
      await (db.select(db.invoicePayments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.paidAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(since) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final byDay = <String, int>{};
  for (final p in pays) {
    final k = _key(p.paidAt);
    byDay[k] = (byDay[k] ?? 0) + p.amount;
  }
  int dayTotal(DateTime d) => byDay[_key(d)] ?? 0;

  // last 8 full weeks before today, open days only, grouped by weekday
  final perWeekday = {for (var w = 1; w <= 7; w++) w: <int>[]};
  final allDays = <int>[];
  for (var i = 1; i <= 56; i++) {
    final d = today.subtract(Duration(days: i));
    if (d.isBefore(first) || closed(d)) continue;
    perWeekday[d.weekday]!.add(dayTotal(d));
    allDays.add(dayTotal(d));
  }
  for (final l in perWeekday.values) {
    l.sort();
  }
  double mean(int w) => _mean(perWeekday[w]!);
  double p25(int w) => _pct(perWeekday[w]!, .25);
  double p75(int w) => _pct(perWeekday[w]!, .75);

  // ── 1. month end ──
  final monthStart = DateTime(now.year, now.month, 1);
  final dim = _daysIn(now.year, now.month);
  final cumulative = <int>[];
  var run = 0;
  for (var d = 1; d <= now.day; d++) {
    run += dayTotal(DateTime(now.year, now.month, d));
    cumulative.add(run);
  }
  final todaySoFar = dayTotal(today);
  final beforeToday = run - todaySoFar;
  var mid = beforeToday.toDouble(),
      low = beforeToday.toDouble(),
      high = beforeToday.toDouble();
  final projected = List<int>.filled(dim, 0);
  for (var d = now.day; d <= dim; d++) {
    final day = DateTime(now.year, now.month, d);
    if (!closed(day)) {
      final w = day.weekday;
      if (d == now.day) {
        mid += math.max(todaySoFar.toDouble(), mean(w));
        low += math.max(todaySoFar.toDouble(), p25(w));
        high += math.max(todaySoFar.toDouble(), p75(w));
      } else {
        mid += mean(w);
        low += p25(w);
        high += p75(w);
      }
    } else if (d == now.day) {
      mid += todaySoFar;
      low += todaySoFar;
      high += todaySoFar;
    }
    projected[d - 1] = mid.round();
  }
  final lastMonthStart = DateTime(now.year, now.month - 1, 1);
  var lastMonthTotal = 0;
  for (
    var d = lastMonthStart;
    d.isBefore(monthStart);
    d = DateTime(d.year, d.month, d.day + 1)
  ) {
    lastMonthTotal += dayTotal(d);
  }

  // confidence: more history and steadier days → higher
  var confidence = dataDays < 90
      ? 2
      : dataDays < 180
      ? 3
      : dataDays < 365
      ? 4
      : 5;
  final m = _mean(allDays);
  if (m > 0 && _sd(allDays) / m > .9) confidence--;
  confidence = confidence.clamp(1, 5);

  // ── 2. next 7 days ──
  final past =
      await (db.select(db.appointments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.isNotIn(const ['cancelled']) &
                t.startsAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(
                  today.subtract(const Duration(days: 56)),
                ) &
                t.startsAt.isSmallerThanValue(today) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final usualCount = {for (var w = 1; w <= 7; w++) w: 0};
  for (final a in past) {
    usualCount[a.startsAt.weekday] = usualCount[a.startsAt.weekday]! + 1;
  }
  final weeksSeen = math.max(
    1,
    math.min(8, today.difference(first).inDays ~/ 7),
  );
  final ahead =
      await (db.select(db.appointments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.isIn(const ['upcoming', 'waiting', 'inChair']) &
                t.startsAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(
                  today.add(const Duration(days: 1)),
                ) &
                t.startsAt.isSmallerThanValue(
                  today.add(const Duration(days: 31)),
                ) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  // weeks of history behind the averages (at most the 8 looked at)
  final weeks = math.max(1, math.min(8, today.difference(first).inDays ~/ 7));
  final week = [
    for (var i = 1; i <= 7; i++)
      () {
        final d = today.add(Duration(days: i));
        return HubDayAhead(
          day: d,
          closed: closed(d),
          booked: ahead.where((a) => _day(a.startsAt) == d).length,
          usual: usualCount[d.weekday]! / weeks,
          usualMoney: closed(d) ? 0 : mean(d.weekday).round(),
        );
      }(),
  ];

  // ── 3. next 30 days: money in vs money out ──
  var in30 = 0.0;
  for (var i = 1; i <= 30; i++) {
    final d = today.add(Duration(days: i));
    if (!closed(d)) in30 += mean(d.weekday);
  }
  final expStart = DateTime(now.year, now.month - 3, 1);
  final exps =
      await (db.select(db.expenses)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.paidAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(expStart) &
                t.paidAt.isSmallerThanValue(monthStart) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final expMonths = {
    for (final e in exps) '${e.paidAt.year}-${e.paidAt.month}',
  }.length;
  final out30 = expMonths == 0
      ? 0
      : exps.fold<int>(0, (s, e) => s + e.amount) / expMonths;

  // ── 4. owed money coming back (learned from bills 90–365 days old) ──
  final sampleInv =
      await (db.select(db.invoices)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.equals('cancelled').not() &
                t.total.isBiggerThanValue(0) &
                t.issuedAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(
                  today.subtract(const Duration(days: 365)),
                ) &
                t.issuedAt.isSmallerThanValue(
                  today.subtract(const Duration(days: 90)),
                ) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final paysByInvoice = <int, List<InvoicePaymentRow>>{};
  for (final p in pays) {
    (paysByInvoice[p.invoiceId] ??= []).add(p);
  }
  var tTotal = 0.0, pAll = 0.0;
  final pBy = {1: 0.0, 30: 0.0, 60: 0.0, 90: 0.0};
  for (final inv in sampleInv) {
    final issued = _day(inv.issuedAt);
    final ps = paysByInvoice[inv.id] ?? const <InvoicePaymentRow>[];
    tTotal += inv.total;
    double paidWithin(int days) => math
        .min(
          inv.total,
          ps
              .where(
                (p) =>
                    !_day(p.paidAt).isAfter(issued.add(Duration(days: days))),
              )
              .fold<int>(0, (s, p) => s + p.amount),
        )
        .toDouble();
    pAll += math.min(inv.total, ps.fold<int>(0, (s, p) => s + p.amount));
    for (final a in pBy.keys.toList()) {
      pBy[a] = pBy[a]! + paidWithin(a);
    }
  }
  final enough = sampleInv.length >= 15 && tTotal > 0;
  double rate(int a) {
    if (!enough) return 0;
    final left = tTotal - pBy[a]!;
    return left <= 0 ? 0 : ((pAll - pBy[a]!) / left).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
  }

  final buckets = List<int>.filled(4, 0);
  for (final o in owed) {
    buckets[o.days <= 30
            ? 0
            : o.days <= 60
            ? 1
            : o.days <= 90
            ? 2
            : 3] +=
        o.due;
  }
  final owedBack = [
    HubOwedBack('Up to 30 days old', buckets[0], rate(1)),
    HubOwedBack('31–60 days old', buckets[1], rate(30)),
    HubOwedBack('61–90 days old', buckets[2], rate(60)),
    HubOwedBack('Over 90 days old', buckets[3], rate(90)),
  ];

  // ── 5. regulars at risk ──
  final done =
      await (db.select(db.appointments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.equals('completed') &
                t.startsAt.isSmallerThanValue(
                  today.add(const Duration(days: 1)),
                ) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final billed =
      await (db.select(db.invoices)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.equals('cancelled').not() &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  final days = <int, Set<DateTime>>{};
  for (final a in done) {
    (days[a.patientId] ??= {}).add(_day(a.startsAt));
  }
  for (final i in billed) {
    if (i.issuedAt.isAfter(now)) continue;
    (days[i.patientId] ??= {}).add(_day(i.issuedAt));
  }
  final booked = {for (final a in ahead) a.patientId};
  final todayBooked =
      await (db.select(db.appointments)..where(
            (t) =>
                t.isDeleted.equals(false) &
                t.status.isIn(const ['upcoming', 'waiting', 'inChair']) &
                t.startsAt.isBiggerOrEqualValue(today) &
                inBranch(t.branchId),
          ))
          .get();
  booked.addAll(todayBooked.map((a) => a.patientId));

  final risky = <({int pid, int visits, int gap, int since})>[];
  for (final e in days.entries) {
    if (booked.contains(e.key)) continue;
    final sorted = e.value.toList()..sort();
    // visits within 14 days of each other are one course of treatment
    final starts = <DateTime>[];
    for (final d in sorted) {
      if (starts.isEmpty || d.difference(starts.last).inDays > 14) {
        starts.add(d);
      }
    }
    if (starts.length < 3) continue;
    final gaps = [
      for (var i = 1; i < starts.length; i++)
        starts[i].difference(starts[i - 1]).inDays,
    ]..sort();
    final usual = gaps[gaps.length ~/ 2];
    final since = today.difference(sorted.last).inDays;
    final lateAfter = math.max((usual * 1.5).round(), usual + 30);
    if (since > lateAfter && since < 730) {
      risky.add((pid: e.key, visits: starts.length, gap: usual, since: since));
    }
  }
  risky.sort((a, b) => (b.since - b.gap).compareTo(a.since - a.gap));
  final top = risky.take(8).toList();
  final people = top.isEmpty
      ? const <int, PatientRow>{}
      : {
          for (final p
              in await (db.select(db.patients)..where(
                    (t) =>
                        t.id.isIn(top.map((r) => r.pid)) &
                        t.isDeleted.equals(false),
                  ))
                  .get())
            p.id: p,
        };
  final atRisk = [
    for (final r in top)
      if (people[r.pid] != null)
        HubAtRisk(
          patientId: r.pid,
          name: people[r.pid]!.fullName,
          code: people[r.pid]!.code,
          phone: people[r.pid]!.phone ?? '',
          visits: r.visits,
          usualGap: r.gap,
          daysSince: r.since,
        ),
  ];

  // ── 6. when the chairs are full (last 8 weeks) ──
  var from = branches.isEmpty
      ? 9
      : branches.map((b) => b.openMinutes ~/ 60).reduce(math.min);
  var to = branches.isEmpty
      ? 21
      : branches.map((b) => (b.closeMinutes + 59) ~/ 60).reduce(math.max);
  if (to <= from) to = from + 8;
  from = from.clamp(0, 23);
  to = to.clamp(from + 1, 24);
  final heat = List.generate(7, (_) => List<double>.filled(to - from, 0));
  for (final a in past) {
    final h = a.startsAt.hour;
    if (h < from || h >= to) continue;
    heat[a.startsAt.weekday - 1][h - from] += 1 / weeks;
  }

  // ── 7. year ahead ──
  final months = List<int>.filled(12, 0);
  var lastYearTotal = 0;
  for (final p in pays) {
    if (p.paidAt.year == now.year) months[p.paidAt.month - 1] += p.amount;
    if (p.paidAt.year == now.year - 1) lastYearTotal += p.amount;
  }
  // whole months before this one (newest first) with data from day one
  final full = <int>[];
  for (var i = 1; i <= 6; i++) {
    final ms = DateTime(now.year, now.month - i, 1);
    if (ms.isBefore(DateTime(first.year, first.month + 1, 1))) break;
    full.add(
      ms.year == now.year
          ? months[ms.month - 1]
          : pays
                .where(
                  (p) => p.paidAt.year == ms.year && p.paidAt.month == ms.month,
                )
                .fold<int>(0, (s, p) => s + p.amount),
    );
  }
  // no whole month yet → a usual open day × open days in a month
  final avg = full.isEmpty
      ? _mean(allDays) * 30 * (7 - closedWeekdays.length) / 7
      : _mean(full.take(3));
  final spread = _sd(full);
  final ci = now.month - 1;
  months[ci] = mid.round();
  var yMid = 0.0, yLow = 0.0, yHigh = 0.0;
  for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
    if (i < ci) {
      yMid += months[i];
      yLow += months[i];
      yHigh += months[i];
    } else if (i == ci) {
      yMid += mid;
      yLow += low;
      yHigh += high;
    } else {
      months[i] = avg.round();
      yMid += avg;
      yLow += math.max(0, avg - spread);
      yHigh += avg + spread;
    }
  }

  return HubForecast(
    ready: true,
    dataDays: dataDays,
    startsOn: startsOn,
    confidence: confidence,
    monthSoFar: run,
    monthLow: low.round(),
    monthMid: mid.round(),
    monthHigh: high.round(),
    lastMonthTotal: lastMonthTotal,
    cumulative: cumulative,
    projected: projected,
    week: week,
    in30: in30.round(),
    out30: out30.round(),
    expenseMonths: expMonths,
    owedBack: owedBack,
    owedHistory: enough ? sampleInv.length : 0,
    atRisk: atRisk,
    atRiskTotal: risky.length,
    heat: heat,
    heatFrom: from,
    heatTo: to,
    months: months,
    monthIndex: ci,
    yearLow: yLow.round(),
    yearMid: yMid.round(),
    yearHigh: yHigh.round(),
    lastYearTotal: lastYearTotal,
    weekdayMean: [for (var w = 1; w <= 7; w++) mean(w)],
    weekdayLow: [for (var w = 1; w <= 7; w++) p25(w)],
    weekdayHigh: [for (var w = 1; w <= 7; w++) p75(w)],
    weekdayVisits: [for (var w = 1; w <= 7; w++) usualCount[w]! / weeksSeen],
    closedWeekdays: closedWeekdays,
    todaySoFar: todaySoFar,
    monthSpread: spread,
  );
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/sections/money_section.dart
import 'dart:math' as math;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';
import '../widgets/hub_ui.dart';

/// Money — collected, spent and kept for the period, day by day, where it
/// went, how it came in, and who still owes.
class MoneySection extends StatelessWidget {
  const MoneySection({
    super.key,
    required this.data,
    required this.period,
    required this.periodPicker,
  });

  final HubData data;
  final HubPeriod period;
  final Widget periodPicker;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final m = data.money;
    final vs = period.vs;
    final margin = m.collected <= 0
        ? null
        : (m.net / m.collected * 100).round();

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        HubSectionTitle(
          title: 'Money',
          subtitle: '${period.describe} · compared with ${period.vs}',
          trailing: periodPicker,
        ),
        HubGrid(
          minWidth: 230,
          maxColumns: 4,
          children: [
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Collected',
              amount: m.collected,
              sub: 'Money actually received',
              delta: hubDelta(m.collected, m.prevCollected, vs),
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Expenses',
              amount: m.expenses,
              sub: m.byCategory.isEmpty
                  ? 'No expenses recorded'
                  : 'Biggest: ${m.byCategory.first.name}',
              delta: hubDelta(m.expenses, m.prevExpenses, vs, upIsGood: false),
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Net profit',
              amount: m.net,
              valueColor: m.net < 0 ? d.alert : d.ok,
              sub: margin == null
                  ? 'Collected minus expenses'
                  : 'You keep $margin% of what comes in',
              delta: hubDelta(m.net, m.prevNet, vs),
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Billed',
              amount: m.billed,
              sub:
                  '${m.billedCount} invoice${m.billedCount == 1 ? '' : 's'} '
                  'raised in the period',
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        _DailyCard(money: m, period: period),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        HubTwoUp(
          left: _SplitCard(
            icon: Icons.pie_chart_outline_rounded,
            tone: d.warn,
            title: 'Where the money went',
            subtitle: 'Expenses by category',
            rows: m.byCategory,
            color: d.warn,
            empty: 'No expenses in this period.',
            link: const HubGoLink('Expenses', HubGo.expenses),
          ),
          right: _SplitCard(
            icon: Icons.account_balance_outlined,
            tone: d.ice,
            title: 'How patients paid',
            subtitle: 'Collected by payment method',
            rows: m.byMethod,
            color: d.ice,
            empty: 'No payments in this period.',
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        _OwedCard(money: m),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _DailyCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DailyCard({required this.money, required this.period});
  final HubMoney money;
  final HubPeriod period;

  static const _wd = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];

  String _name(DateTime t, {bool long = false}) => switch (money.unit) {
    HubBarUnit.hour => hubHour(t.hour * 60),
    HubBarUnit.day =>
      long ? '${_wd[t.weekday - 1]} ${hubShortDate(t)}' : '${t.day}',
    HubBarUnit.week => long ? 'Week of ${hubShortDate(t)}' : hubShortDate(t),
    HubBarUnit.month =>
      long ? '${hubMonthName(t)} ${t.year}' : hubMonthName(t).substring(0, 3),
  };

  String get _unitWord => switch (money.unit) {
    HubBarUnit.hour => 'hour',
    HubBarUnit.day => 'day',
    HubBarUnit.week => 'week',
    HubBarUnit.month => 'month',
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final hidden = HubScope.of(context).hidden;
    final bars = money.bars;
    final past = bars.where((b) => !b.future).toList();
    final top = bars.fold<int>(0, (m, b) => math.max(m, b.amount));
    // index of the best bar so far (records have no identity to compare)
    var bestAt = -1;
    for (var i = 0; i < bars.length; i++) {
      if (bars[i].future || bars[i].amount == 0) continue;
      if (bestAt < 0 || bars[i].amount > bars[bestAt].amount) bestAt = i;
    }
    final best = bestAt < 0 ? null : bars[bestAt];
    final avg = past.isEmpty ? 0 : (money.collected / past.length).round();
    final quiet = past.where((b) => b.amount == 0).length;
    final n = bars.length;
    final labelEvery = n <= 12 ? 1 : (n <= 31 ? 5 : (n / 8).ceil());
    final now = DateTime.now();

    Widget stat(String value, String label) => Expanded(
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Text(
            value,
            maxLines: 1,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
              color: d.text1,
              fontSize: 12.sp,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 2),
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.2.sp),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );

    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.bar_chart_rounded,
      tone: d.teal,
      title: 'Collected each $_unitWord',
      subtitle: '${period.describe} · hover a bar for the amount',
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 18, 18, 8),
            child: top == 0
                ? const HubEmpty(
                    'Nothing collected in this period yet.',
                    icon: Icons.bar_chart_rounded,
                  )
                : SizedBox(
                    height: 190,
                    child: Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                      children: [
                        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++)
                          Expanded(
                            child: _Bar(
                              label: _name(bars[i].start),
                              amount: bars[i].amount,
                              fraction: bars[i].amount / top,
                              future: bars[i].future,
                              current:
                                  !bars[i].future &&
                                  (i == n - 1 || bars[i + 1].future) &&
                                  period.end.isAfter(now),
                              best: i == bestAt,
                              tooltip:
                                  '${_name(bars[i].start, long: true)} · '
                                  '${hubRs(bars[i].amount, hidden: hidden)}',
                              showLabel:
                                  i == 0 || i == n - 1 || i % labelEvery == 0,
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
          ),
          Divider(height: 1, color: d.line),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                stat(hubRs(avg, hidden: hidden), 'Average per $_unitWord'),
                stat(
                  best == null || best.amount == 0
                      ? '—'
                      : hubRs(best.amount, hidden: hidden),
                  best == null || best.amount == 0
                      ? 'Best $_unitWord'
                      : 'Best · ${_name(best.start, long: true)}',
                ),
                stat(
                  '$quiet',
                  quiet == 1
                      ? '${_unitWord[0].toUpperCase()}${_unitWord.substring(1)} with nothing in'
                      : '${_unitWord[0].toUpperCase()}${_unitWord.substring(1)}s with nothing in',
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Bar extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Bar({
    required this.label,
    required this.amount,
    required this.fraction,
    required this.future,
    required this.current,
    required this.best,
    required this.tooltip,
    required this.showLabel,
  });

  final String label;
  final int amount;
  final double fraction;
  final bool future, current, best, showLabel;
  final String tooltip;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final color = future
        ? d.line
        : current
        ? d.ice
        : best
        ? d.tealDeep
        : d.teal;
    return Tooltip(
      message: future ? '$label · still to come' : tooltip,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 1.5),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
          children: [
            Flexible(
              child: FractionallySizedBox(
                heightFactor: future
                    ? 0.04
                    : math.max(amount == 0 ? 0.02 : 0.05, fraction),
                child: Container(
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: amount == 0 && !future ? d.line : color,
                    borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                      top: Radius.circular(4),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            SizedBox(
              height: 16,
              child: showLabel
                  ? FittedBox(
                      fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
                      child: Text(
                        label,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: current ? d.ice : d.text4,
                          fontSize: 9.sp,
                          fontWeight: current
                              ? FontWeight.w800
                              : FontWeight.w500,
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                  : null,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SplitCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SplitCard({
    required this.icon,
    required this.tone,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.rows,
    required this.color,
    required this.empty,
    this.link,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final Color tone, color;
  final String title, subtitle, empty;
  final List<({String name, int total})> rows;
  final Widget? link;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Six biggest, the rest folded into "Everything else".
    final shown = rows.take(6).toList();
    final rest = rows.skip(6).fold<int>(0, (s, r) => s + r.total);
    if (rest > 0) shown.add((name: 'Everything else', total: rest));
    final total = rows.fold<int>(0, (s, r) => s + r.total);

    return HubCard(
      icon: icon,
      tone: tone,
      title: title,
      subtitle: subtitle,
      trailing: link,
      child: shown.isEmpty
          ? HubEmpty(empty)
          : Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 10, 18, 14),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  for (final r in shown)
                    HubShareRow(
                      label: r.name,
                      amount: r.total,
                      fraction: total == 0 ? 0 : r.total / total,
                      color: color,
                      note: total == 0
                          ? null
                          : '${(r.total / total * 100).round()}%',
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _OwedCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _OwedCard({required this.money});
  final HubMoney money;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final total = money.owedBuckets.fold<int>(0, (s, v) => s + v);
    const labels = ['0–30 days', '31–60 days', '61–90 days', 'Over 90 days'];
    final tones = [d.teal, d.warn, const Color(0xFFF08A4B), d.alert];

    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.account_balance_wallet_outlined,
      tone: d.warn,
      title: 'Still owed to you',
      subtitle: 'Every unpaid invoice, by how long it has been waiting',
      trailing: const HubGoLink('Billing', HubGo.billing),
      child: total == 0
          ? const HubEmpty(
              'Nobody owes the clinic anything.',
              icon: Icons.verified_outlined,
            )
          : Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 16, 18, 10),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  HubSegBar([
                    for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                      (money.owedBuckets[i].toDouble(), tones[i]),
                  ], height: 10),
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  HubGrid(
                    minWidth: 170,
                    maxColumns: 4,
                    gap: 12,
                    children: [
                      for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++)
                        Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: d.surface2,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            border: Border(
                              left: BorderSide(color: tones[i], width: 3),
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                labels[i],
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: d.text3,
                                  fontSize: 9.2.sp,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 4),
                              HubAmount(
                                money.owedBuckets[i],
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                                  color: d.text1,
                                  fontSize: 12.5.sp,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                '${money.owedBucketCounts[i]} invoice'
                                '${money.owedBucketCounts[i] == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: d.text3,
                                  fontSize: 9.sp,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Text(
                    'WHO OWES THE MOST',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: d.text4,
                      fontSize: 9.sp,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                      letterSpacing: .8,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  for (final o in money.topOwing)
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                Text(
                                  o.patient,
                                  maxLines: 1,
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: d.text1,
                                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Text(
                                  '${o.code} · ${o.invoices} invoice'
                                  '${o.invoices == 1 ? '' : 's'} · oldest '
                                  '${o.oldestDays} day${o.oldestDays == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: d.text3,
                                    fontSize: 9.sp,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          HubPill(
                            o.oldestDays > 90
                                ? 'Over 90 days'
                                : o.oldestDays > 60
                                ? 'Over 60 days'
                                : o.oldestDays > 30
                                ? 'Over 30 days'
                                : 'Recent',
                            o.oldestDays > 60
                                ? d.alert
                                : o.oldestDays > 30
                                ? d.warn
                                : d.teal,
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          SizedBox(
                            width: 130,
                            child: HubAmount(
                              o.amount,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: d.text1,
                                fontSize: 10.4.sp,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/sections/dentists_section.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';
import '../widgets/hub_ui.dart';

/// Dentists — visits, no-shows, money collected, average bill and the
/// commission each one is owed for the period.
class DentistsSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const DentistsSection({
    super.key,
    required this.data,
    required this.period,
    required this.periodPicker,
  });

  final HubData data;
  final HubPeriod period;
  final Widget periodPicker;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final list = data.dentists;

    HubDentist? top(int Function(HubDentist) by) {
      HubDentist? best;
      for (final x in list) {
        if (by(x) > 0 && (best == null || by(x) > by(best))) best = x;
      }
      return best;
    }

    final busiest = top((x) => x.completed);
    final highest = top((x) => x.avgBill);
    HubDentist? mostNoShows;
    for (final x in list) {
      if (x.noShows == 0) continue;
      if (mostNoShows == null || x.noShowRate > mostNoShows.noShowRate) {
        mostNoShows = x;
      }
    }
    final due = list.fold<int>(0, (s, x) => s + (x.due > 0 ? x.due : 0));

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        HubSectionTitle(
          title: 'Dentists',
          subtitle: period.describe,
          trailing: periodPicker,
        ),
        if (list.isEmpty)
          HubCard(
            icon: Icons.groups_outlined,
            tone: d.ice,
            title: 'No dentist activity yet',
            child: const HubEmpty(
              'Appointments and payments with a dentist name appear here.',
              icon: Icons.groups_outlined,
            ),
          )
        else ...[
          HubGrid(
            minWidth: 230,
            maxColumns: 4,
            children: [
              HubKpi(
                label: 'Busiest dentist',
                value: busiest?.name ?? '—',
                sub: busiest == null
                    ? 'No finished visits yet'
                    : '${busiest.completed} visits finished',
              ),
              HubKpi(
                label: 'Highest average bill',
                value: highest?.name ?? '—',
                sub: highest == null
                    ? 'Nothing collected yet'
                    : '${hubRs(highest.avgBill, hidden: HubScope.of(context).hidden)} per visit',
              ),
              HubKpi(
                label: 'Most no-shows',
                value: mostNoShows?.name ?? 'None',
                valueColor: mostNoShows == null ? d.ok : null,
                sub: mostNoShows == null
                    ? 'Every booked patient came'
                    : '${mostNoShows.noShows} no-show'
                          '${mostNoShows.noShows == 1 ? '' : 's'} · '
                          '${(mostNoShows.noShowRate * 100).round()}% of visits',
              ),
              if (period.month == null)
                HubKpi(
                  label: 'Commission still to pay',
                  value: '—',
                  sub:
                      'Payouts are monthly — pick This month, Last month '
                      'or one whole month to see it',
                )
              else
                HubKpi(
                  label: 'Commission still to pay',
                  amount: due,
                  valueColor: due > 0 ? d.warn : null,
                  sub: due > 0
                      ? 'Pay from Expenses → Dentist payouts'
                      : 'Everyone is paid up',
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 18),
          HubCard(
            icon: Icons.groups_outlined,
            tone: d.ice,
            title: 'Each dentist',
            subtitle: 'Collected = money received on their patients’ bills',
            trailing: const HubGoLink('Payouts', HubGo.expenses),
            child: LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, c) => c.maxWidth >= 880
                  ? _Table(list: list)
                  : Column(
                      children: [
                        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) ...[
                          if (i > 0) Divider(height: 1, color: d.line),
                          _DentistTile(x: list[i]),
                        ],
                      ],
                    ),
            ),
          ),
          if (data.unassigned > 0) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 14),
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 12, 8, 12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: d.warn.withValues(alpha: .08),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                border: Border.all(color: d.warn.withValues(alpha: .3)),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.link_off_rounded, color: d.warn, size: 20),
                  const SizedBox(width: 12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      '${hubRs(data.unassigned, hidden: HubScope.of(context).hidden)} '
                      'collected in this period is not linked to any dentist, so '
                      'nobody earns commission on it. Link it in Dentist payouts.',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: d.text2,
                        fontSize: 9.8.sp,
                        height: 1.4,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const HubGoLink('Fix', HubGo.expenses),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Table extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Table({required this.list});
  final List<HubDentist> list;

  static const _flex = [26, 12, 11, 17, 14, 15, 15];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final maxCollected = list.fold<int>(
      1,
      (m, x) => x.collected > m ? x.collected : m,
    );

    Widget head(String t, int i, {bool end = false}) => Expanded(
      flex: _flex[i],
      child: Text(
        t,
        textAlign: end ? TextAlign.right : TextAlign.left,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: d.text4,
          fontSize: 9.sp,
          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
          letterSpacing: .6,
        ),
      ),
    );

    return Column(
      children: [
        Container(
          color: d.surface2,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18, vertical: 10),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              head('DENTIST', 0),
              head('VISITS', 1, end: true),
              head('NO-SHOWS', 2, end: true),
              head('COLLECTED', 3, end: true),
              head('AVG BILL', 4, end: true),
              head('COMMISSION', 5, end: true),
              head('STILL TO PAY', 6, end: true),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        for (final x in list)
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18, vertical: 12),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: d.line)),
            ),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  flex: _flex[0],
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        x.name,
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: d.text1,
                          fontSize: 10.2.sp,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 5),
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 24),
                        child: ClipRRect(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                          child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                            value: x.collected / maxCollected,
                            minHeight: 5,
                            backgroundColor: d.line,
                            valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(d.teal),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                _cell(d, _flex[1], '${x.completed} / ${x.booked}'),
                _cell(
                  d,
                  _flex[2],
                  x.noShows == 0
                      ? '0'
                      : '${x.noShows} · ${(x.noShowRate * 100).round()}%',
                  color: x.noShowRate >= .15 ? d.alert : null,
                ),
                _money(d, _flex[3], x.collected, bold: true),
                _money(d, _flex[4], x.avgBill),
                Expanded(
                  flex: _flex[5],
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                    children: [
                      HubAmount(
                        x.commission,
                        style: TextStyle(color: d.text1, fontSize: 10.sp),
                      ),
                      Text(
                        x.percent == 0
                            ? 'no rate set'
                            : '${x.percent.toStringAsFixed(x.percent % 1 == 0 ? 0 : 1)}%',
                        style: TextStyle(color: d.text4, fontSize: 9.2.sp),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                if (x.paidKnown)
                  _money(
                    d,
                    _flex[6],
                    x.due > 0 ? x.due : 0,
                    bold: true,
                    color: x.due > 0 ? d.warn : d.ok,
                  )
                else
                  _cell(d, _flex[6], '—'),
              ],
            ),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _cell(DentColors d, int flex, String text, {Color? color}) => Expanded(
    flex: flex,
    child: Text(
      text,
      textAlign: TextAlign.right,
      style: TextStyle(color: color ?? d.text2, fontSize: 10.sp),
    ),
  );

  Widget _money(
    DentColors d,
    int flex,
    int v, {
    bool bold = false,
    Color? color,
  }) => Expanded(
    flex: flex,
    child: Align(
      alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
      child: HubAmount(
        v,
        style: TextStyle(
          color: color ?? d.text1,
          fontSize: 10.sp,
          fontWeight: bold ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}

/// Narrow-window version of one table row.
class _DentistTile extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DentistTile({required this.x});
  final HubDentist x;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    Widget fact(String label, Widget value) => Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 6),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.6.sp),
            ),
          ),
          value,
        ],
      ),
    );
    final style = TextStyle(
      color: d.text1,
      fontSize: 10.sp,
      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
    );
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 14, 18, 14),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Text(
            x.name,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: d.text1,
              fontSize: 11.sp,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            ),
          ),
          fact(
            'Visits finished / booked',
            Text('${x.completed} / ${x.booked}', style: style),
          ),
          fact(
            'No-shows',
            Text(
              '${x.noShows} · ${(x.noShowRate * 100).round()}%',
              style: style.copyWith(
                color: x.noShowRate >= .15 ? d.alert : null,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          fact('Collected', HubAmount(x.collected, style: style)),
          fact('Average bill', HubAmount(x.avgBill, style: style)),
          fact('Commission', HubAmount(x.commission, style: style)),
          fact(
            'Still to pay',
            x.paidKnown
                ? HubAmount(
                    x.due > 0 ? x.due : 0,
                    style: style.copyWith(color: x.due > 0 ? d.warn : d.ok),
                  )
                : Text('—', style: style),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/sections/health_section.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';
import '../../data/hub_security.dart';
import '../widgets/hub_ui.dart';

/// System health — is every computer syncing, is WhatsApp sending, when
/// does the licence end, and who opened the Hub.
class HealthSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const HealthSection({super.key, required this.data});
  final HubData data;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final h = data.health;
    final left = h.licenceDaysLeft;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final stale = h.devices
        .where(
          (x) =>
              !x.isThis &&
              (x.lastSeen == null || now.difference(x.lastSeen!).inDays >= 3),
        )
        .length;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        HubSectionTitle(
          title: 'System health',
          subtitle:
              'Computers, sync, WhatsApp and licence · checked at '
              '${hubClock(data.loadedAt)}',
        ),
        HubGrid(
          minWidth: 220,
          maxColumns: 4,
          children: [
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Things to fix',
              value: h.issues == 0 ? 'None' : '${h.issues}',
              valueColor: h.issues == 0 ? d.ok : d.alert,
              sub: h.issues == 0
                  ? 'Everything is working'
                  : 'Listed on this page in red',
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Last sync on this computer',
              value: h.cloud ? hubAgo(h.lastSyncHere) : 'Offline plan',
              sub: h.cloud
                  ? (h.lastSyncHere == null
                        ? 'Never synced'
                        : '${hubDate(h.lastSyncHere!)} · ${hubClock(h.lastSyncHere!)}')
                  : 'This install has no cloud copy',
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Computers',
              value: '${h.devices.length}',
              valueColor: stale > 0 ? d.alert : null,
              sub: stale == 0
                  ? 'All syncing'
                  : '$stale not syncing for 3+ days',
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Licence',
              value: left == null
                  ? '—'
                  : left <= 0
                  ? 'Ends today'
                  : '$left days left',
              valueColor: left != null && left <= 14 ? d.alert : null,
              sub:
                  '${h.plan} plan'
                  '${h.expiresAt == null ? '' : ' · until ${hubDate(h.expiresAt!)}'}',
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        HubTwoUp(
          left: _Computers(h: h),
          right: _WhatsApp(h: h),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        HubTwoUp(
          left: _Plan(h: h),
          right: const _HubActivity(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

Widget _statusDot(Color c) => Container(
  width: 10,
  height: 10,
  decoration: BoxDecoration(color: c, shape: BoxShape.circle),
);

class _Computers extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Computers({required this.h});
  final HubHealth h;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.computer_rounded,
      tone: d.ice,
      title: 'Computers',
      subtitle: 'When each one last sent its work to the cloud',
      trailing: const HubGoLink('Settings', HubGo.settings),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          if (h.devices.isEmpty)
            const HubEmpty(
              'No computers registered yet.',
              icon: Icons.computer,
            ),
          for (final x in h.devices)
            () {
              final days = x.lastSeen == null
                  ? null
                  : now.difference(x.lastSeen!).inDays;
              final tone = x.isThis
                  ? d.ok
                  : days == null || days >= 3
                  ? d.alert
                  : days >= 1
                  ? d.warn
                  : d.ok;
              return Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 13, 18, 13),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: d.line)),
                ),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 36,
                      height: 36,
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: d.surface2,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        x.letter,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                          color: d.text1,
                          fontSize: 11.5.sp,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            x.name.isEmpty ? 'Computer ${x.letter}' : x.name,
                            maxLines: 1,
                            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              color: d.text1,
                              fontSize: 10.2.sp,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            [
                              if (x.platform.isNotEmpty) x.platform,
                              if (x.isThis) 'this computer',
                            ].join(' · '),
                            style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.sp),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                    _statusDot(tone),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    Text(
                      x.isThis
                          ? 'In use now'
                          : x.lastSeen == null
                          ? 'Never synced'
                          : 'Synced ${hubAgo(x.lastSeen)}',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: tone == d.ok ? d.text2 : tone,
                        fontSize: 9.6.sp,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              );
            }(),
          if (h.skipped > 0)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 12, 18, 14),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Icon(Icons.sync_problem_rounded, size: 18, color: d.warn),
                  const SizedBox(width: 8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      '${h.skipped} record${h.skipped == 1 ? '' : 's'} could not '
                      'sync last time and are only on this computer.',
                      style: TextStyle(color: d.text2, fontSize: 9.6.sp),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _WhatsApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const _WhatsApp({required this.h});
  final HubHealth h;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.chat_outlined,
      tone: d.teal,
      title: 'WhatsApp',
      subtitle: h.waChecked
          ? 'Connection and message queue · last 7 days'
          : (h.cloud
                ? "Connection only — couldn't reach the message queue"
                : 'Needs the cloud plan'),
      trailing: const HubGoLink('WhatsApp', HubGo.whatsapp),
      child: h.wa.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty('No branches yet.', icon: Icons.chat_outlined)
          : Column(
              children: [
                for (final w in h.wa)
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 13, 18, 13),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: d.line)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        _statusDot(
                          !w.on ? d.text4 : (w.connected ? d.ok : d.alert),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 12),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                w.branch,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: d.text1,
                                  fontSize: 10.2.sp,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                !w.on
                                    ? 'Turned off — app reminders only'
                                    : (w.connected
                                          ? 'Connected'
                                          : 'Disconnected — nothing is going out'),
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: w.on && !w.connected
                                      ? d.alert
                                      : d.text3,
                                  fontSize: 9.sp,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (w.failed != null && w.failed! > 0)
                          HubPill('${w.failed} failed', d.alert),
                        if (w.waiting != null && w.waiting! > 0) ...[
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                          HubPill('${w.waiting} waiting', d.warn),
                        ],
                        if (w.on &&
                            w.connected &&
                            (w.failed ?? 0) == 0 &&
                            (w.waiting ?? 0) == 0 &&
                            w.failed != null)
                          HubPill('All sent', d.ok),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _Plan extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Plan({required this.h});
  final HubHealth h;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    Widget usage(String label, int used, int max) {
      final f = max <= 0 ? 0.0 : used / max;
      final tone = f >= 1 ? d.alert : (f >= .8 ? d.warn : d.teal);
      return Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    label,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: d.text1,
                      fontSize: 10.sp,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                Text(
                  '$used of $max',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: tone == d.teal ? d.text2 : tone,
                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
              child: LinearProgressIndicator(
                value: f.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
                minHeight: 7,
                backgroundColor: d.line,
                valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(tone),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
    }

    final left = h.licenceDaysLeft;
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.verified_outlined,
      tone: left != null && left <= 14 ? d.alert : d.ok,
      title: 'Plan & licence',
      subtitle: h.expiresAt == null
          ? '${h.plan} plan'
          : '${h.plan} plan · renews ${hubDate(h.expiresAt!)}',
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 10, 18, 14),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            usage('Staff logins', h.users, h.maxUsers),
            usage('Branches', h.branches, h.maxBranches),
            const SizedBox(height: 6),
            Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.cloud_done_outlined, size: 18, color: d.text3),
                const SizedBox(width: 8),
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    h.cloud
                        ? 'Cloud backup: every sync keeps a copy of your records '
                              'in the cloud. X-ray and photo files stay on the '
                              'computer that took them.'
                        : 'Offline plan: no cloud copy. Keep a backup of this '
                              'computer.',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: d.text3,
                      fontSize: 9.4.sp,
                      height: 1.4,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _HubActivity extends ConsumerWidget {
  const _HubActivity();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final events = ref.watch(hubEventsProvider).value ?? const [];
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.history_rounded,
      tone: d.warn,
      title: 'Hub activity',
      subtitle: 'Who opened this screen, wrong PINs and PIN changes',
      child: events.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty('Nothing recorded yet.', icon: Icons.history_rounded)
          : Column(
              children: [
                for (final e in events)
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 11, 18, 11),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: d.line)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          e.kind == 'hub_pin_wrong' ||
                                  e.kind == 'hub_pin_locked'
                              ? Icons.warning_amber_rounded
                              : Icons.circle,
                          size:
                              e.kind == 'hub_pin_wrong' ||
                                  e.kind == 'hub_pin_locked'
                              ? 18
                              : 8,
                          color:
                              e.kind == 'hub_pin_wrong' ||
                                  e.kind == 'hub_pin_locked'
                              ? d.alert
                              : d.text4,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: 10),
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
