// ───────────────────────────── busy hours ─────────────────────────────

class _Heat extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Heat({required this.f});
  final HubForecast f;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    const days = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
    final hours = f.heatTo - f.heatFrom;
    var top = 0.0;
    var best = (w: 0, h: 0), quiet = (w: 0, h: 0);
    var quietV = double.infinity;
    for (var w = 0; w < f.heat.length; w++) {
      final rowTotal = f.heat[w].fold<double>(0, (s, v) => s + v);
      for (var h = 0; h < f.heat[w].length; h++) {
        final v = f.heat[w][h];
        if (v > top) {
          top = v;
          best = (w: w, h: h);
        }
        // quiet = least booked hour on a day the clinic is actually used
        if (rowTotal > 0 && v < quietV) {
          quietV = v;
          quiet = (w: w, h: h);
        }
      }
    }

    String slot(({int w, int h}) s) =>
        '${days[s.w]} ${hubHour((f.heatFrom + s.h) * 60)}';

    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.grid_on_rounded,
      tone: d.ice,
      title: 'When the chairs are full',
      subtitle: top == 0
          ? 'Not enough bookings yet'
          : 'Busiest ${slot(best)} · quietest ${slot(quiet)}',
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 14, 18, 8),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    const SizedBox(width: 40),
                    for (var h = 0; h < hours; h++)
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          h % 2 == 0
                              ? hubHour((f.heatFrom + h) * 60).split(' ').first
                              : '',
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: TextStyle(color: d.text4, fontSize: 9.sp),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                for (var w = 0; w < f.heat.length; w++)
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 2),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        SizedBox(
                          width: 40,
                          child: Text(
                            days[w],
                            style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.2.sp),
                          ),
                        ),
                        for (var h = 0; h < hours; h++)
                          Expanded(
                            child: Tooltip(
                              message:
                                  '${days[w]} ${hubHour((f.heatFrom + h) * 60)}: '
                                  '${f.heat[w][h].toStringAsFixed(1)} bookings a week',
                              child: Container(
                                height: 24,
                                margin: const EdgeInsets.all(1.5),
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: top == 0 || f.heat[w][h] == 0
                                      ? d.surface2
                                      : d.ice.withValues(
                                          alpha:
                                              .15 + .85 * (f.heat[w][h] / top),
                                        ),
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(5),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const _How(
            'Each square is the average number of bookings that start in that '
            'hour on that weekday, over the last 8 weeks. Darker means busier. '
            'Light squares in opening hours are the slots to offer first.',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── year ahead ─────────────────────────────

class _Year extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Year({required this.f});
  final HubForecast f;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final hidden = HubScope.of(context).hidden;
    final year = DateTime.now().year;
    const names = ['J', 'F', 'M', 'A', 'M', 'J', 'J', 'A', 'S', 'O', 'N', 'D'];
    final top = math.max(1, f.months.fold<int>(0, math.max)).toDouble();
    final vsLast = hubDelta(f.yearMid, f.lastYearTotal, '${year - 1}');

    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.calendar_month_rounded,
      tone: d.teal,
      title: '$year should end at about ${hubRs(f.yearMid, hidden: hidden)}',
      subtitle:
          'Likely between ${hubRs(f.yearLow, hidden: hidden)} and '
          '${hubRs(f.yearHigh, hidden: hidden)}',
      trailing: vsLast == null
          ? null
          : HubPill(
              vsLast.text,
              vsLast.good == null ? d.text3 : (vsLast.good! ? d.ok : d.alert),
            ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 16, 18, 8),
            child: SizedBox(
              height: 170,
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                children: [
                  for (var i = 0; i < 12; i++)
                    Expanded(
                      child: Tooltip(
                        message:
                            '${hubMonthName(DateTime(year, i + 1))}: '
                            '${hubRs(f.months[i], hidden: hidden)}'
                            '${i < f.monthIndex ? '' : ' (expected)'}',
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                          child: Column(
                            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                            children: [
                              Flexible(
                                child: FractionallySizedBox(
                                  heightFactor: math.max(
                                    .03,
                                    f.months[i] / top,
                                  ),
                                  child: Container(
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      color: i < f.monthIndex
                                          ? d.teal
                                          : (i == f.monthIndex
                                                ? d.ice
                                                : d.ice.withValues(alpha: .3)),
                                      borderRadius: const BorderRadius.vertical(
                                        top: Radius.circular(5),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: 6),
                              Text(
                                names[i],
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: i == f.monthIndex ? d.ice : d.text4,
                                  fontSize: 9.sp,
                                  fontWeight: i == f.monthIndex
                                      ? FontWeight.w800
                                      : FontWeight.w500,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const _How(
            'Months already over show what was actually collected. This month '
            'uses the month-end estimate above. Every later month uses the '
            'average of your last three whole months; the range widens by how '
            'much your months usually vary.',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/sections/right_now_section.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';
import '../widgets/hub_ui.dart';

/// Right now — today's pulse, what needs the owner, and the chairs.
class RightNowSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const RightNowSection({super.key, required this.data});
  final HubData data;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final hidden = HubScope.of(context).hidden;
    final t = data.today;
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final lastWeekDay = hubWeekday(now.subtract(const Duration(days: 7)));
    final monthShort = hubMonthName(now).substring(0, 3);
    final other = t.collected - t.collectedCash;
    final seen = t.count('completed'),
        inChair = t.count('inChair'),
        waiting = t.count('waiting'),
        toCome = t.count('upcoming'),
        noShows = t.count('noShow');
    final margin = t.monthCollected <= 0
        ? null
        : (t.monthNet / t.monthCollected * 100).round();

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        HubSectionTitle(
          title: 'Right now',
          subtitle:
              '${hubWeekday(now)} ${hubDate(now)} · what needs you and '
              'what is happening in the chairs',
        ),
        HubGrid(
          minWidth: 220,
          children: [
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Collected today',
              amount: t.collected,
              sub: t.collected == 0
                  ? 'Nothing collected yet today'
                  : 'Cash ${hubRs(t.collectedCash, hidden: hidden)} · '
                        'other ${hubRs(other, hidden: hidden)}',
              delta: hubDelta(
                t.collected,
                t.lastWeekSameTime,
                'last ${lastWeekDay.substring(0, 3)}',
              ),
              segments: [
                (t.collectedCash.toDouble(), d.teal),
                (other.toDouble(), d.ice),
              ],
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Patients today',
              value: '${t.patients}',
              sub:
                  '$seen seen · $inChair in chair · $waiting waiting · '
                  '$toCome to come',
              delta: noShows == 0
                  ? null
                  : HubDelta(
                      '$noShows no-show${noShows == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                      false,
                    ),
              segments: [
                (seen.toDouble(), d.teal),
                (inChair.toDouble(), d.ice),
                (waiting.toDouble(), d.warn),
                (toCome.toDouble(), d.text4.withValues(alpha: .45)),
              ],
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Collected this month',
              amount: t.monthCollected,
              sub: '1–${now.day} ${hubMonthName(now)}',
              delta: hubDelta(
                t.monthCollected,
                t.lastMonthToDate,
                'same days last month',
              ),
              // filled = how far past the same days last month
              segments: t.lastMonthToDate <= 0
                  ? [(1.0, d.teal)]
                  : [
                      (
                        t.monthCollected.clamp(0, t.lastMonthToDate).toDouble(),
                        d.teal,
                      ),
                      (
                        (t.lastMonthToDate - t.monthCollected)
                            .clamp(0, t.lastMonthToDate)
                            .toDouble(),
                        d.line,
                      ),
                    ],
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Net profit · $monthShort',
              amount: t.monthNet,
              valueColor: t.monthNet < 0 ? d.alert : null,
              sub:
                  'After ${hubRs(t.monthExpenses, hidden: hidden)} of expenses',
              delta: margin == null
                  ? null
                  : HubDelta(
                      margin >= 0
                          ? 'You keep $margin% of what comes in'
                          : 'Spending more than collected',
                      margin >= 0,
                    ),
              // kept vs spent, out of what came in
              segments: [
                ((t.monthNet > 0 ? t.monthNet : 0).toDouble(), d.teal),
                (t.monthExpenses.toDouble(), d.warn),
              ],
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Still owed to you',
              amount: t.owedTotal,
              valueColor: t.owedTotal > 0 ? d.warn : null,
              sub: t.owedCount == 0
                  ? 'Every invoice is paid'
                  : '${t.owedCount} invoice${t.owedCount == 1 ? '' : 's'} · '
                        '${hubRs(t.owedOver60, hidden: hidden)} over 60 days',
              segments: t.owedTotal == 0
                  ? [(1.0, d.ok)]
                  : [
                      ((t.owedTotal - t.owedOver60).toDouble(), d.warn),
                      (t.owedOver60.toDouble(), d.alert),
                    ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        HubTwoUp(
          breakAt: 1080,
          leftFlex: 5,
          rightFlex: 6,
          left: _NeedsYou(alerts: data.alerts),
          right: _Chairs(today: t),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Needs you ─────────────────────────────

class _NeedsYou extends StatelessWidget {
  const _NeedsYou({required this.alerts});
  final List<HubAlert> alerts;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    int n(HubLevel l) => alerts.where((a) => a.level == l).length;
    final parts = [
      if (n(HubLevel.urgent) > 0) '${n(HubLevel.urgent)} urgent',
      if (n(HubLevel.today) > 0) '${n(HubLevel.today)} today',
      if (n(HubLevel.later) > 0) '${n(HubLevel.later)} when you can',
    ];
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.notifications_active_outlined,
      tone: alerts.isEmpty ? d.ok : d.alert,
      title: 'Needs you',
      subtitle: parts.isEmpty ? 'All clear' : parts.join(' · '),
      child: alerts.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty(
              'Nothing needs you right now.\nThe clinic is running smoothly.',
              icon: Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
            )
          : Column(
              children: [
                for (var i = 0; i < alerts.length; i++) ...[
                  if (i > 0) Divider(height: 1, color: d.line),
                  _AlertRow(alert: alerts[i]),
                ],
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _AlertRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AlertRow({required this.alert});
  final HubAlert alert;

  static IconData icon(String k) => switch (k) {
    'cash' => Icons.point_of_sale_rounded,
    'wa' => Icons.chat_outlined,
    'sync' => Icons.sync_problem_rounded,
    'licence' => Icons.verified_outlined,
    'request' => Icons.event_available_outlined,
    'wait' => Icons.hourglass_bottom_rounded,
    'bill' => Icons.receipt_long_outlined,
    'noshow' => Icons.person_off_outlined,
    'owed' => Icons.account_balance_wallet_outlined,
    'payout' => Icons.payments_outlined,
    'lock' => Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
    _ => Icons.info_outline_rounded,
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final (tone, word) = switch (alert.level) {
      HubLevel.urgent => (d.alert, 'Urgent'),
      HubLevel.today => (d.warn, 'Today'),
      HubLevel.later => (d.ice, 'When you can'),
    };
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 14, 14, 14),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          hubIconTile(d, icon(alert.icon), tone, size: 38),
          const SizedBox(width: 13),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Wrap(
                  spacing: 8,
                  runSpacing: 4,
                  crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      alert.title,
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: d.text1,
                        fontSize: 10.4.sp,
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                      ),
                    ),
                    HubPill(word, tone),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 3),
                Text(
                  alert.detail,
                  maxLines: 2,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.text3,
                    fontSize: 9.4.sp,
                    height: 1.35,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          OutlinedButton(
            onPressed: () => HubScope.of(
              context,
            ).onGo(alert.go, arg: alert.arg, label: alert.action),
            style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
              foregroundColor: tone,
              side: BorderSide(color: tone.withValues(alpha: .4)),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 12),
            ),
            child: Text(
              alert.action,
              style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9.4.sp, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Today in the chairs ─────────────────────────

class _Chairs extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Chairs({required this.today});
  final HubToday today;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final visits = today.appts.where((a) => a.status != 'cancelled').toList();
    final lanes = <String, List<HubAppt>>{};
    for (final a in visits) {
      (lanes[a.dentist.isEmpty ? 'No dentist' : a.dentist] ??= []).add(a);
    }
    final names = lanes.keys.toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => lanes[b]!.length.compareTo(lanes[a]!.length));

    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.schedule_rounded,
      tone: d.ice,
      title: 'Today in the chairs',
      subtitle:
          '${hubHour(today.dayStart)} – ${hubHour(today.dayEnd)} · '
          'the red line is now',
      trailing: const HubGoLink('Appointments', HubGo.appointments),
      child: visits.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty(
              'No appointments today.',
              icon: Icons.event_busy_outlined,
            )
          : Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 14, 18, 16),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  _HoursRow(start: today.dayStart, end: today.dayEnd),
                  const SizedBox(height: 6),
                  for (final n in names)
                    _Lane(
                      name: n,
                      appts: lanes[n]!,
                      start: today.dayStart,
                      end: today.dayEnd,
                    ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Wrap(
                    spacing: 16,
                    runSpacing: 6,
                    children: [
                      for (final s in const [
                        'completed',
                        'inChair',
                        'waiting',
                        'upcoming',
                        'noShow',
                      ])
                        Row(
                          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                          children: [
                            Container(
                              width: 12,
                              height: 12,
                              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                color: _statusColor(d, s),
                                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(3),
                              ),
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 6),
                            Text(
                              _statusWord(s),
                              style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.sp),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

Color _statusColor(DentColors d, String s) => switch (s) {
  'completed' => d.teal,
  'inChair' => d.ice,
  'waiting' => d.warn,
  'noShow' => d.alert.withValues(alpha: .55),
  _ => d.text4.withValues(alpha: .35),
};

String _statusWord(String s) => switch (s) {
  'completed' => 'Done',
  'inChair' => 'In chair',
  'waiting' => 'Waiting',
  'noShow' => 'No-show',
  'cancelled' => 'Cancelled',
  _ => 'Coming',
};

const _laneLabel = 128.0;

class _HoursRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HoursRow({required this.start, required this.end});
  final int start, end;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final hours = (end - start) ~/ 60;
    final step = hours > 10 ? 2 : 1;
    return Row(
      children: [
        const SizedBox(width: _laneLabel),
        Expanded(
          child: LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, c) => SizedBox(
              height: 18,
              child: Stack(
                clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                children: [
                  for (var h = 0; h < hours; h += step)
                    Positioned(
                      left: c.maxWidth * h / hours,
                      child: Text(
                        hubHour(start + h * 60),
                        style: TextStyle(color: d.text4, fontSize: 9.sp),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Lane extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Lane({
    required this.name,
    required this.appts,
    required this.start,
    required this.end,
  });

  final String name;
  final List<HubAppt> appts;
  final int start, end;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final span = (end - start).toDouble();
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final nowMin = now.hour * 60 + now.minute;
    final done = appts.where((a) => a.status == 'completed').length;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 5),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: _laneLabel,
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 10),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    name,
                    maxLines: 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: d.text1,
                      fontSize: 9.8.sp,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Text(
                    '$done of ${appts.length} done',
                    style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.2.sp),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, c) {
                final w = c.maxWidth;
                double x(int minutes) =>
                    (w * (minutes - start) / span).clamp(0.0, w);
                return Container(
                  height: 38,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: d.surface2,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
                  ),
                  child: Stack(
                    clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                    children: [
                      for (final a in appts)
                        () {
                          final s = a.startsAt.hour * 60 + a.startsAt.minute;
                          final left = x(s);
                          final width = (x(s + a.minutes) - left).clamp(6.0, w);
                          final upcoming = a.status == 'upcoming';
                          final fill = _statusColor(d, a.status);
                          return Positioned(
                            left: left,
                            top: 4,
                            bottom: 4,
                            width: width - 2,
                            child: Tooltip(
                              message:
                                  '${hubClock(a.startsAt)} · ${a.patient}\n'
                                  '${a.procedure.isEmpty ? '' : '${a.procedure} · '}'
                                  '${_statusWord(a.status)}',
                              child: Container(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 6,
                                ),
                                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: fill,
                                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(7),
                                  border: upcoming
                                      ? Border.all(color: d.line)
                                      : null,
                                ),
                                child: width < 56
                                    ? null
                                    : Text(
                                        a.patient.split(' ').first,
                                        maxLines: 1,
                                        overflow: TextOverflow.clip,
                                        style: TextStyle(
                                          color:
                                              upcoming || a.status == 'noShow'
                                              ? d.text1
                                              : AppPalette.onAccent,
                                          fontSize: 9.sp,
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }(),
                      if (nowMin > start && nowMin < end)
                        Positioned(
                          left: x(nowMin) - 1,
                          top: -3,
                          bottom: -3,
                          width: 2,
                          child: Container(color: d.alert),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/sections/branches_section.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/features/cash/data/cash_repository.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';
import '../widgets/hub_ui.dart';

/// Branches side by side for the period: money, visits, no-shows, what is
/// owed, when the cash was last counted and whether WhatsApp is up.
class BranchesSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const BranchesSection({
    super.key,
    required this.data,
    required this.period,
    required this.periodPicker,
    required this.branchCount,
    required this.scopeName,
    this.onShowAll,
  });

  final HubData data;
  final HubPeriod period;
  final Widget periodPicker;

  /// How many branches the clinic has.
  final int branchCount;

  /// The one branch being looked at, or null for all branches.
  final String? scopeName;

  /// Switch the Hub to "All branches" — null when not allowed (an admin
  /// tied to one branch).
  final VoidCallback? onShowAll;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final list = data.branches;

    Widget notice(String title, String text, {Widget? action}) => HubCard(
      icon: Icons.store_mall_directory_outlined,
      tone: d.ice,
      title: title,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 18, 18, 20),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(
              text,
              style: TextStyle(color: d.text2, fontSize: 10.2.sp, height: 1.5),
            ),
            if (action != null) ...[const SizedBox(height: 14), action],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        HubSectionTitle(
          title: 'Branches',
          subtitle: '${period.describe} · side by side',
          trailing: list.isEmpty ? null : periodPicker,
        ),
        if (branchCount <= 1)
          notice(
            'One branch',
            'Your clinic has a single branch, so every other page of the Hub '
                'already shows all of it. Branches appear here side by side '
                'once you open a second one.',
          )
        else if (list.isEmpty)
          notice(
            'Showing ${scopeName ?? 'one branch'} only',
            'The comparison needs all branches.',
            action: onShowAll == null
                ? null
                : FilledButton.icon(
                    onPressed: onShowAll,
                    style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: d.ice,
                      foregroundColor: AppPalette.onAccent,
                    ),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.compare_arrows_rounded, size: 18),
                    label: Text(
                      'Compare all branches',
                      style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.sp),
                    ),
                  ),
          )
        else ...[
          _Compare(list: list),
          const SizedBox(height: 18),
          HubGrid(
            minWidth: 320,
            maxColumns: 3,
            children: [for (final b in list) _BranchCard(b: b)],
          ),
        ],
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Compare extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Compare({required this.list});
  final List<HubBranch> list;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final top = list.fold<int>(1, (m, b) => b.collected > m ? b.collected : m);
    final total = list.fold<int>(0, (s, b) => s + b.collected);
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.leaderboard_outlined,
      tone: d.teal,
      title: 'Collected by branch',
      subtitle: 'Bar length compares branches with each other',
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 10, 18, 14),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            for (final b in [
              ...list,
            ]..sort((x, y) => y.collected.compareTo(x.collected)))
              HubShareRow(
                label: b.name,
                amount: b.collected,
                fraction: b.collected / top,
                color: d.teal,
                note: total == 0
                    ? null
                    : '${(b.collected / total * 100).round()}% of clinic',
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _BranchCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _BranchCard({required this.b});
  final HubBranch b;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final today = dayKey(DateTime.now());
    final closedAgo = b.lastClosed == null
        ? null
        : DateTime.now().difference(parseDayKey(b.lastClosed!)).inDays;
    final (closeText, closeTone) = b.lastClosed == null
        ? ('Cash never closed', d.alert)
        : b.lastClosed == today
        ? ('Closed today', d.ok)
        : closedAgo == 1
        ? ('Last closed yesterday', d.ok)
        : (
            'Last closed $closedAgo days ago',
            closedAgo! > 3 ? d.alert : d.warn,
          );
    final due = b.completed + b.noShows;
    final noShowPct = due == 0 ? 0 : (b.noShows / due * 100).round();

    Widget line(String label, Widget value) => Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              label,
              style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.8.sp),
            ),
          ),
          value,
        ],
      ),
    );
    final valueStyle = TextStyle(
      color: d.text1,
      fontSize: 10.2.sp,
      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
    );

    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 16, 18, 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: d.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: d.line),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              hubIconTile(
                d,
                Icons.store_mall_directory_rounded,
                d.ice,
                size: 36,
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 12),
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  b.name,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                    color: d.text1,
                    fontSize: 12.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              HubPill(
                !b.waOn
                    ? 'WhatsApp off'
                    : (b.waConnected ? 'WhatsApp on' : 'WhatsApp down'),
                !b.waOn ? d.text3 : (b.waConnected ? d.ok : d.alert),
                icon: Icons.chat_outlined,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          Text(
            'COLLECTED',
            style: TextStyle(
              color: d.text4,
              fontSize: 9.sp,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
              letterSpacing: .8,
            ),
          ),
          HubAmount(
            b.collected,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
              color: d.text1,
              fontSize: 17.sp,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Divider(height: 1, color: d.line),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          line('Expenses', HubAmount(b.expenses, style: valueStyle)),
          line(
            'Net profit',
            HubAmount(
              b.net,
              style: valueStyle.copyWith(color: b.net < 0 ? d.alert : d.ok),
            ),
          ),
          line('Visits booked', Text('${b.appts}', style: valueStyle)),
          line('Visits finished', Text('${b.completed}', style: valueStyle)),
          line(
            'No-shows',
            Text(
              '${b.noShows} · $noShowPct%',
              style: valueStyle.copyWith(
                color: noShowPct >= 15 ? d.alert : null,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          line(
            'Still owed',
            HubAmount(
              b.owed,
              style: valueStyle.copyWith(color: b.owed > 0 ? d.warn : null),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
            child: HubPill(
              closeText,
              closeTone,
              icon: Icons.point_of_sale_rounded,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/sections/watch_list_section.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';
import '../../data/hub_security.dart';
import '../../data/hub_watch.dart';
import '../widgets/hub_ui.dart';

/// Watch list — discounts, cancelled bills, removed payments, deleted
/// expenses, price and rate changes, who took the money, who signed in.
class WatchListSection extends StatelessWidget {
  const WatchListSection({
    super.key,
    required this.data,
    required this.period,
    required this.periodPicker,
  });

  final HubData data;
  final HubPeriod period;
  final Widget periodPicker;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final w = data.watch;
    final hidden = HubScope.of(context).hidden;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        HubSectionTitle(
          title: 'Watch list',
          subtitle: '${period.describe} · things worth a second look',
          trailing: periodPicker,
        ),
        HubGrid(
          minWidth: 210,
          maxColumns: 5,
          children: [
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Discounts given',
              amount: w.discountTotal,
              valueColor: w.discountTotal > 0 ? d.warn : null,
              sub: w.discountCount == 0
                  ? 'No discounts'
                  : '${w.discountCount} bill${w.discountCount == 1 ? '' : 's'} · '
                        '${(w.discountShare * 100).toStringAsFixed(1)}% of billing',
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Bills cancelled',
              value: '${w.cancelledCount}',
              valueColor: w.cancelledCount > 0 ? d.alert : null,
              sub: w.cancelledCount == 0
                  ? 'None cancelled'
                  : '${hubRs(w.cancelledTotal, hidden: hidden)} in total',
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Payments removed',
              value: '${w.removedCount}',
              valueColor: w.removedCount > 0 ? d.alert : null,
              sub: w.removedCount == 0
                  ? 'None removed'
                  : '${hubRs(w.removedTotal, hidden: hidden)} taken off',
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Expenses deleted',
              value: '${w.deletedCount}',
              valueColor: w.deletedCount > 0 ? d.warn : null,
              sub: w.deletedCount == 0
                  ? 'None deleted'
                  : '${hubRs(w.deletedTotal, hidden: hidden)} in total',
            ),
            HubKpi(
              label: 'Failed sign-ins',
              value: '${w.failedSignIns}',
              valueColor: w.failedSignIns >= 3 ? d.alert : null,
              sub:
                  '${w.afterHoursSignIns} sign-in${w.afterHoursSignIns == 1 ? '' : 's'} '
                  'outside opening hours',
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        HubTwoUp(
          left: _Discounts(w: w),
          right: _TakenBy(w: w),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        _Changes(w: w),
        const SizedBox(height: 18),
        HubTwoUp(
          left: _SignIns(w: w),
          right: _Visits(
            w: w,
            noShows: w.noShows,
            cancelled: w.cancelledVisits,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Discounts extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Discounts({required this.w});
  final HubWatch w;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final hidden = HubScope.of(context).hidden;
    final top = w.discountByDentist.isEmpty
        ? 1
        : w.discountByDentist.first.total;
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.local_offer_outlined,
      tone: d.warn,
      title: 'Discounts',
      subtitle: 'Money taken off bills · 30% or more is marked',
      child: w.discounts.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty(
              'No discounts in this period.',
              icon: Icons.local_offer_outlined,
            )
          : Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 8, 18, 12),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  for (final x in w.discountByDentist)
                    HubShareRow(
                      label: x.name,
                      amount: x.total,
                      fraction: x.total / top,
                      color: d.warn,
                      note: '${x.count} bill${x.count == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                    ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Text(
                    'BIGGEST DISCOUNTS',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: d.text4,
                      fontSize: 9.sp,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                      letterSpacing: .8,
                    ),
                  ),
                  for (final x in w.discounts)
                    Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 7),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                Text(
                                  x.patient,
                                  maxLines: 1,
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: d.text1,
                                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Text(
                                  'Invoice ${x.invoiceNo} · ${hubShortDate(x.at)}'
                                  '${x.dentist.isEmpty ? '' : ' · ${x.dentist}'}'
                                  '${x.by.isEmpty ? '' : ' · given by ${x.by}'}',
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: d.text3,
                                    fontSize: 9.sp,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          HubPill(
                            '${(x.share * 100).round()}% off',
                            x.big ? d.alert : d.text3,
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 10),
                          SizedBox(
                            width: 110,
                            child: Text(
                              hubRs(x.amount, hidden: hidden),
                              textAlign: TextAlign.right,
                              style: TextStyle(
                                color: d.text1,
                                fontSize: 10.sp,
                                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _TakenBy extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TakenBy({required this.w});
  final HubWatch w;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final total = w.takenBy.fold<int>(0, (s, x) => s + x.total);
    final top = w.takenBy.isEmpty ? 1 : w.takenBy.first.total;
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.badge_outlined,
      tone: d.ice,
      title: 'Who took the money',
      subtitle: 'Payments by the staff member who recorded them',
      child: w.takenBy.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty(
              'No payments in this period.',
              icon: Icons.badge_outlined,
            )
          : Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 8, 18, 12),
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  for (final x in w.takenBy)
                    HubShareRow(
                      label: x.name,
                      amount: x.total,
                      fraction: x.total / top,
                      color: d.ice,
                      note:
                          '${x.count} payment${x.count == 1 ? '' : 's'} · '
                          '${total == 0 ? 0 : (x.total / total * 100).round()}%',
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _Changes extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Changes({required this.w});
  final HubWatch w;

  static (IconData, Color) _look(HubChangeKind k, DentColors d) => switch (k) {
    HubChangeKind.invoiceCancelled => (Icons.receipt_long_outlined, d.alert),
    HubChangeKind.paymentRemoved => (Icons.money_off_rounded, d.alert),
    HubChangeKind.expenseDeleted => (Icons.delete_outline_rounded, d.warn),
    HubChangeKind.rateChanged => (Icons.percent_rounded, d.ice),
    HubChangeKind.priceEdited => (Icons.sell_outlined, d.ice),
    HubChangeKind.userRemoved => (Icons.person_remove_outlined, d.warn),
    HubChangeKind.signInFailed => (Icons.lock_outline_rounded, d.alert),
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final hidden = HubScope.of(context).hidden;
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.manage_search_rounded,
      tone: d.alert,
      title: 'Cancelled, removed and changed',
      subtitle: 'Newest first · names are who did it',
      child: w.changes.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty(
              'Nothing was cancelled, removed or changed in this period.',
              icon: Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
            )
          : Column(
              children: [
                for (final c in w.changes)
                  () {
                    final (icon, tone) = _look(c.kind, d);
                    return Container(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 11, 18, 11),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: d.line)),
                      ),
                      child: Row(
                        children: [
                          hubIconTile(d, icon, tone, size: 34),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Column(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                Text(
                                  c.title,
                                  maxLines: 1,
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: d.text1,
                                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Text(
                                  [
                                    if (c.detail.isNotEmpty) c.detail,
                                    if (c.who.isNotEmpty) 'by ${c.who}',
                                  ].join(' · '),
                                  maxLines: 2,
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: d.text3,
                                    fontSize: 9.2.sp,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 12),
                          Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
                            children: [
                              if (c.amount != null)
                                Text(
                                  hubRs(c.amount!, hidden: hidden),
                                  style: TextStyle(
                                    color: d.text1,
                                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              Text(
                                '${hubShortDate(c.at)} · ${hubClock(c.at)}',
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: d.text3,
                                  fontSize: 9.sp,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    );
                  }(),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

class _SignIns extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SignIns({required this.w});
  final HubWatch w;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return HubCard(
      icon: Icons.login_rounded,
      tone: d.ice,
      title: 'Sign-ins',
      subtitle:
          'Recorded from this update on · outside hours = 30 min before opening or after closing',
      child: w.signIns.isEmpty
          ? const HubEmpty(
              'No sign-ins recorded in this period yet.',
              icon: Icons.login_rounded,
            )
          : Column(
              children: [
                for (final s in w.signIns)
                  Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 11, 18, 11),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: d.line)),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                s.name,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: d.text1,
                                  fontSize: 10.2.sp,
                                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                                ),
                              ),
                              Text(
                                [
                                  '${s.count} sign-in${s.count == 1 ? '' : 's'}',
                                  if (s.devices.isNotEmpty)
                                    'computer ${(s.devices.toList()..sort()).join(', ')}',
                                  if (s.last != null)
                                    'last ${hubShortDate(s.last!)} ${hubClock(s.last!)}',
                                ].join(' · '),
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: d.text3,
                                  fontSize: 9.sp,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (s.afterHours > 0) ...[
                          HubPill('${s.afterHours} outside hours', d.warn),
                          const SizedBox(width: 6),
                        ],
                        if (s.failed > 0)
                          HubPill('${s.failed} failed', d.alert),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            ),
    );
  }
}

// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/hub_entry.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../data/hub_security.dart';

/// The Hub's own route — outside the shell, so it replaces the sidebar.
const kHubRoute = '/hub';

/// The app's theme before the Hub switched to dark, put back on exit.
class _ThemeBefore extends Notifier<ThemeMode?> {
  @override
  ThemeMode? build() => null;
  void set(ThemeMode? v) => state = v;
}

final hubThemeBeforeProvider = NotifierProvider<_ThemeBefore, ThemeMode?>(
  _ThemeBefore.new,
);

/// Confirm → open the Hub. On a plan without it, the upgrade page instead.
Future<void> openReportingHub(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) async {
  final access = ref.read(hubAccessProvider);
  if (!access.allowed) return;
  if (!access.onPlan) {
    await showHubUpgrade(context);
    return;
  }
  final ok = await showHubConfirm(
    context,
    icon: Icons.insights_rounded,
    tone: context.dent.ice,
    title: 'Open the Detailed Reporting Hub?',
    message:
        'The Hub takes over the whole screen and asks for your Hub PIN. '
        'Your clinic screens come back when you press Exit.',
    confirm: 'Open Hub',
    cancel: 'Not now',
  );
  if (ok && context.mounted) {
    // The Hub always opens dark; the app's own theme comes back on exit.
    // Switched here, in the same frame as the route change, so nothing
    // flashes light first.
    ref.read(hubThemeBeforeProvider.notifier).set(ref.read(themeModeProvider));
    ref.read(themeModeProvider.notifier).set(ThemeMode.dark);
    context.go(kHubRoute);
  }
}

/// Top bar button. Hidden for anyone who may not open the Hub; shows a
/// lock on plans that don't include it.
class ReportingHubButton extends ConsumerWidget {
  const ReportingHubButton({super.key, this.compact = false, this.gap = 10});

  /// Icon only (narrow top bar).
  final bool compact;

  /// Space before the button — kept inside so a hidden button leaves no gap.
  final double gap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final access = ref.watch(hubAccessProvider);
    if (!access.allowed) return const SizedBox.shrink();
    final d = context.dent;
    final locked = !access.onPlan;

    final body = Container(
      height: 38,
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: compact ? 10 : 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        gradient: locked
            ? null
            : LinearGradient(
                colors: [
                  d.ice.withValues(alpha: .16),
                  d.teal.withValues(alpha: .12),
                ],
              ),
        color: locked ? d.surface2 : null,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
        border: Border.all(
          color: locked ? d.line : d.ice.withValues(alpha: .35),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Icon(
            locked ? Icons.lock_outline_rounded : Icons.insights_rounded,
            size: 18,
            color: locked ? d.text3 : d.ice,
          ),
          if (!compact) ...[
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text(
              'Reporting Hub',
              style: TextStyle(
                color: locked ? d.text3 : d.text1,
                fontSize: 9.8.sp,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );

    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.only(left: gap),
      child: Tooltip(
        message: locked
            ? 'Detailed Reporting Hub · Standard & Premium'
            : 'Detailed Reporting Hub',
        child: Material(
          color: Colors.transparent,
          child: InkWell(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
            onTap: () => openReportingHub(context, ref),
            child: body,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A calm, consistent yes/no dialog for everything the Hub asks.
Future<bool> showHubConfirm(
  BuildContext context, {
  required IconData icon,
  required Color tone,
  required String title,
  required String message,
  required String confirm,
  String cancel = 'Cancel',
  bool danger = false,
}) async {
  final r = await showDialog<bool>(
    context: context,
    builder: (ctx) {
      final d = ctx.dent;
      final action = danger ? d.alert : d.ice;
      return Dialog(
        backgroundColor: d.surface,
        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 460),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(26, 26, 26, 20),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Container(
                  width: 48,
                  height: 48,
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: tone.withValues(alpha: .13),
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                  ),
                  child: Icon(icon, color: tone, size: 24),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                    color: d.text1,
                    fontSize: 13.5.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 8),
                Text(
                  message,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.text2,
                    fontSize: 10.2.sp,
                    height: 1.5,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 22),
                Row(
                  children: [
                    const Spacer(),
                    TextButton(
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, false),
                      style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: d.text2),
                      child: Text(cancel, style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.sp)),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 8),
                    FilledButton(
                      autofocus: true,
                      onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx, true),
                      style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                        backgroundColor: action,
                        foregroundColor: danger
                            ? Colors.white
                            : AppPalette.onAccent,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 20,
                          vertical: 14,
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        confirm,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontSize: 10.sp,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
  );
  return r ?? false;
}

/// What a Basic / Offline clinic sees when it taps the Hub button.
Future<void> showHubUpgrade(BuildContext context) => showDialog<void>(
  context: context,
  builder: (ctx) {
    final d = ctx.dent;
    const points = [
      'Everything happening right now on one screen — and what needs you',
      'Money in and out, owed money by age, payment methods',
      'Each dentist: visits, collections, no-shows and commission due',
      'Branches side by side, computers, WhatsApp and licence health',
      'Protected by its own PIN, locks itself when you walk away',
    ];
    return Dialog(
      backgroundColor: d.surface,
      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22)),
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 520),
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(30, 30, 30, 22),
          child: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Container(
                width: 64,
                height: 64,
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  gradient: LinearGradient(
                    colors: [
                      d.ice.withValues(alpha: .2),
                      d.teal.withValues(alpha: .12),
                    ],
                    begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                    end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                  ),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(19),
                ),
                child: Icon(Icons.insights_rounded, color: d.ice, size: 30),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 10,
                  vertical: 4,
                ),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: d.warn.withValues(alpha: .13),
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                ),
                child: Text(
                  'Standard & Premium',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.warn,
                    fontSize: 9.2.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 12),
              Text(
                'Detailed Reporting Hub',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                  color: d.text1,
                  fontSize: 15.sp,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 6),
              Text(
                'One full-screen view of the whole clinic for the owner.',
                textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: d.text3,
                  fontSize: 10.4.sp,
                  height: 1.5,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 18),
              Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(16, 10, 16, 10),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: d.surface2,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    for (final p in points)
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
                        child: Row(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Icon(
                              Icons.check_circle_rounded,
                              size: 18,
                              color: d.tealDeep,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(width: 10),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Text(
                                p,
                                style: TextStyle(
                                  color: d.text2,
                                  fontSize: 10.sp,
                                  height: 1.4,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              Consumer(
                builder: (_, ref, __) => Text(
                  'Your plan: ${ref.watch(entitlementsProvider).tierLabel}  ·  '
                  'Contact DentOS support to upgrade',
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.6.sp),
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 16),
              SizedBox(
                width: double.infinity,
                child: OutlinedButton(
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(ctx),
                  style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                    foregroundColor: d.text2,
                    side: BorderSide(color: d.line),
                    minimumSize: const Size.fromHeight(44),
                  ),
                  child: Text('Close', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.sp)),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  },
);
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/reporting_hub_screen.dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:ui' show ImageFilter;

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/router/app_routes.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/features/cash/data/cash_repository.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/utils/app_activity.dart';

import '../data/hub_data.dart';
import '../data/hub_pdf.dart';
import '../data/hub_security.dart';
import 'hub_entry.dart';
import 'sections/branches_section.dart';
import 'sections/dentists_section.dart';
import 'sections/health_section.dart';
import 'sections/money_section.dart';
import 'sections/right_now_section.dart';
import 'sections/watch_list_section.dart';
import 'sections/whats_coming_section.dart';
import 'widgets/hub_loading.dart';
import 'widgets/hub_pin_gate.dart';
import 'widgets/hub_ui.dart';

enum HubSection {
  rightNow('Right now', Icons.dashboard_outlined),
  whatsComing("What's coming", Icons.auto_graph_rounded),
  money('Money', Icons.payments_outlined),
  dentists('Dentists', Icons.groups_outlined),
  watchList('Watch list', Icons.policy_outlined),
  branches('Branches', Icons.store_mall_directory_outlined),
  health('System health', Icons.monitor_heart_outlined);

  const HubSection(this.label, this.icon);
  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
}

enum _Phase { starting, gate, loading, ready, failed }

/// The Detailed Reporting Hub — a full-screen mode for the owner (and
/// admins the owner allows). Replaces the sidebar and every clinic screen
/// until Exit.
///
/// Safety: Hub PIN on every visit, 5 wrong → 5 min lockout, numbers blur
/// after 5 quiet minutes, the Hub closes itself after 15, and every visit
/// is recorded.
class ReportingHubScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const ReportingHubScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<ReportingHubScreen> createState() => _ReportingHubScreenState();
}

class _ReportingHubScreenState extends ConsumerState<ReportingHubScreen> {
  static const _lockAfter = Duration(minutes: 5);
  static const _exitAfter = Duration(minutes: 15);
  static const _refreshEvery = Duration(seconds: 60);

  _Phase _phase = _Phase.starting;
  String? _fatal;

  HubSecurity? _sec;
  AuthSession? _session;
  User? _user;
  HubGateMode _gateMode = HubGateMode.unlock;

  HubSection _section = HubSection.rightNow;
  HubPeriod _period = HubPeriod.of(HubPreset.thisMonth);
  String? _scope;
  bool _scopeFixed = false;

  HubData? _data;
  int _step = 0, _gen = 0, _lockCount = 0;
  Object? _loadError;

  bool _hidden = false,
      _locked = false,
      _changingPin = false,
      _refreshing = false,
      _opened = false,
      _left = false;

  DateTime _lastActivity = DateTime.now();
  DateTime? _openedAt;
  Timer? _idleTimer, _autoTimer;

  /// Puts the app's own theme back when the Hub closes (it opens dark).
  VoidCallback? _restoreTheme;
  final _scroll = ScrollController();

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    HardwareKeyboard.instance.addHandler(_onKey);
    _idleTimer = Timer.periodic(
      const Duration(seconds: 15),
      (_) => _checkIdle(),
    );
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) => _start());
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    HardwareKeyboard.instance.removeHandler(_onKey);
    _idleTimer?.cancel();
    _autoTimer?.cancel();
    _scroll.dispose();
    // Not during unmount itself — providers can't change mid-build.
    final restore = _restoreTheme;
    if (restore != null) Future.microtask(restore);
    // Closed by the app (sign-out, access removed) rather than Exit.
    if (_opened && !_left) {
      _sec?.log(
        _session,
        'hub_exit',
        detail: '${_minutesOpen()} min · closed by the app',
      );
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  // ───────────────────────────── lifecycle ─────────────────────────────

  Future<void> _start() async {
    final s = ref.read(authControllerProvider);
    if (s == null) return; // the router is already sending us to login
    // Remember the app's theme, and make sure the Hub is dark even when it
    // was opened some other way than the top bar button.
    final themes = ref.read(themeModeProvider.notifier);
    final ThemeMode before =
        ref.read(hubThemeBeforeProvider) ??
        ref.read(themeModeProvider) ??
        ThemeMode.light;
    ref.read(hubThemeBeforeProvider.notifier).set(null);
    _restoreTheme = () => themes.set(before);
    if (ref.read(themeModeProvider) != ThemeMode.dark) {
      themes.set(ThemeMode.dark);
    }
    final sec = ref.read(hubSecurityProvider);
    _sec = sec;
    _session = s;
    _scope = s.branchId; // an admin tied to a branch sees only that branch
    _scopeFixed = s.branchId != null;
    final u = await sec.userFor(s);
    if (!mounted) return;
    if (u == null) {
      setState(() {
        _phase = _Phase.failed;
        _fatal =
            'Your login was not found on this computer. Sync from Settings, '
            'then open the Hub again.';
      });
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      _user = u;
      _gateMode = sec.hasPin(u) ? HubGateMode.unlock : HubGateMode.setup;
      _phase = _Phase.gate;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _reloadUser() async {
    final s = _session;
    if (s == null) return;
    final u = await _sec!.userFor(s);
    if (u != null && mounted) setState(() => _user = u);
  }

  /// The PIN gate said yes — first entry, after an idle lock, or after a
  /// PIN change.
  Future<void> _unlocked() async {
    await _reloadUser();
    if (!mounted) return;
    _lastActivity = DateTime.now();
    if (!_opened) {
      _opened = true;
      _openedAt = DateTime.now();
      _sec?.log(_session, 'hub_open', detail: _scopeName(null));
    }
    if (_phase == _Phase.ready) {
      setState(() {
        _locked = false;
        _changingPin = false;
      });
      _load(full: false);
      return;
    }
    _autoTimer ??= Timer.periodic(_refreshEvery, (_) {
      if (_phase == _Phase.ready && !_locked && !_changingPin && !_refreshing) {
        _load(full: false, quiet: true);
      }
    });
    _load(full: true);
  }

  Future<void> _load({required bool full, bool quiet = false}) async {
    final gen = ++_gen;
    setState(() {
      if (full) {
        _phase = _Phase.loading;
        _step = 0;
        _loadError = null;
      } else if (!quiet) {
        _refreshing = true;
      }
    });
    final started = DateTime.now();
    try {
      final ent = ref.read(entitlementsProvider);
      final data = await loadHubData(
        db: ref.read(appDatabaseProvider),
        cash: ref.read(cashRepositoryProvider),
        allBranches: await ref.read(branchesStreamProvider.future),
        licence: ref.read(licenseControllerProvider).value?.license,
        ent: ent,
        branchId: _scope,
        period: _period,
        showPayouts: true, // the Hub needs Standard+, which has payouts
        onStep: full
            ? (i) {
                if (mounted && gen == _gen) setState(() => _step = i);
              }
            : null,
      );
      if (full) {
        if (mounted && gen == _gen) {
          setState(() => _step = hubLoadSteps.length);
        }
        // Long enough to read the checklist; never a flash.
        final spent = DateTime.now().difference(started);
        const least = Duration(milliseconds: 1100);
        await Future<void>.delayed(
          spent < least ? least - spent : const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
        );
      }
      if (!mounted || gen != _gen) return;
      setState(() {
        _data = data;
        _phase = _Phase.ready;
        _refreshing = false;
        _loadError = null;
      });
    } catch (e) {
      if (!mounted || gen != _gen) return;
      setState(() {
        _refreshing = false;
        if (full || _data == null) {
          _phase = _Phase.loading;
          _loadError = e;
        }
      });
      if (!full && !quiet && mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(
          context,
        ).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Could not refresh: $e')));
      }
    }
  }

  // ───────────────────────────── idle / lock ─────────────────────────────

  bool _onKey(KeyEvent _) {
    _touch();
    return false; // never swallow the key
  }

  void _touch() {
    if (_locked || _left) return;
    _lastActivity = DateTime.now();
    // keeps the app's auto sign-out from firing while the Hub is in use
    ref.read(appActivityProvider.notifier).ping();
  }

  void _checkIdle() {
    if (!mounted || _left || _phase == _Phase.starting) return;
    final idle = DateTime.now().difference(_lastActivity);
    if (idle >= _exitAfter) {
      _leave(idle: true);
    } else if (_phase == _Phase.ready && !_locked && idle >= _lockAfter) {
      _lock(auto: true);
    }
  }

  void _lock({bool auto = false}) {
    setState(() {
      _locked = true;
      _changingPin = false;
      _lockCount++;
    });
    _sec?.log(
      _session,
      'hub_idle_lock',
      detail: auto ? 'no activity for 5 minutes' : 'locked by the user',
    );
  }

  int _minutesOpen() =>
      _openedAt == null ? 0 : DateTime.now().difference(_openedAt!).inMinutes;

  // ───────────────────────────── leaving ─────────────────────────────

  void _leave({String? to, String? label, bool idle = false}) {
    if (_left || !mounted) return;
    _left = true;
    if (_opened) {
      _sec?.log(
        _session,
        idle ? 'hub_idle_exit' : 'hub_exit',
        detail:
            '${_minutesOpen()} min'
            '${label == null ? '' : ' · went to $label'}',
      );
    }
    context.go(to ?? AppRoutes.dashboard);
    // same frame as the route change → the clinic screens open in the
    // app's own theme, with no flash
    _restoreTheme?.call();
    _restoreTheme = null;
  }

  Future<void> _confirmExit() async {
    final ok = await showHubConfirm(
      context,
      icon: Icons.logout_rounded,
      tone: context.dent.alert,
      title: 'Leave the Reporting Hub?',
      message:
          'You will go back to the Dashboard. Opening the Hub again asks '
          'for your PIN.',
      confirm: 'Exit Hub',
      cancel: 'Stay',
      danger: true,
    );
    if (ok) _leave();
  }

  Future<void> _go(HubGo go, {String? arg, String? label}) async {
    if (go == HubGo.watchList) {
      setState(() => _section = HubSection.watchList);
      if (_scroll.hasClients) _scroll.jumpTo(0);
      return;
    }
    final (route, name) = switch (go) {
      HubGo.appointments => (AppRoutes.appointments, 'Appointments'),
      HubGo.billing => (AppRoutes.billing, 'Billing'),
      HubGo.closeDay => (AppRoutes.billing, 'Day closing'),
      HubGo.expenses => (AppRoutes.expenses, 'Expenses'),
      HubGo.requests => ('${AppRoutes.dashboard}/requests', 'Booking requests'),
      HubGo.whatsapp => (AppRoutes.whatsapp, 'WhatsApp'),
      HubGo.settings => (AppRoutes.settings, 'Settings'),
      HubGo.watchList => (AppRoutes.dashboard, 'Watch list'), // handled above
    };
    final ok = await showHubConfirm(
      context,
      icon: Icons.open_in_new_rounded,
      tone: context.dent.ice,
      title: 'Open $name?',
      message:
          'This leaves the Reporting Hub and opens $name. Coming back asks '
          'for your PIN again.',
      confirm: 'Open $name',
      cancel: 'Stay in Hub',
    );
    if (!ok || !mounted) return;
    if (go == HubGo.closeDay && arg != null) {
      ref.read(dayCloseTargetProvider.notifier).set(arg);
      ref.read(billingTabProvider.notifier).set(1);
    }
    _leave(to: route, label: name);
  }

  // ───────────────────────────── helpers ─────────────────────────────

  String _scopeName(List<Branch>? branches) {
    if (_scope == null) return 'All branches';
    final list = branches ?? ref.read(branchesStreamProvider).value ?? const [];
    return list.where((b) => b.uuid == _scope).map((b) => b.name).firstOrNull ??
        'One branch';
  }

  /// Owner report PDF of everything the Hub shows right now.
  Future<void> _exportPdf() async {
    final data = _data;
    if (data == null) return;
    final clinic = ref.read(clinicProfileProvider).value?.name ?? 'Clinic';
    final scope = _scopeName(null);
    final period = _period.preset == HubPreset.custom
        ? _period.describe
        : '${_period.label} (${_period.describe})';
    _sec?.log(_session, 'hub_export', detail: '$scope · $period');
    await showPdfOutput(
      context,
      build: () => buildHubPdf(
        data,
        clinicName: clinic,
        scopeName: scope,
        periodLabel: period,
        preparedFor: _session?.fullName ?? '',
      ),
      filename: 'dentos-owner-report.pdf',
    );
  }

  void _setScope(String? v) {
    if (v == _scope) return;
    setState(() => _scope = v);
    _load(full: false);
  }

  void _setPeriod(HubPeriod p) {
    if (p == _period) return;
    setState(() => _period = p);
    _load(full: false);
  }

  // ───────────────────────────── build ─────────────────────────────

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    // Watched so the list stays loaded for the whole visit.
    final branches =
        ref.watch(branchesStreamProvider).value ?? const <Branch>[];
    // Watched here, in build — never inside the LayoutBuilder below.
    final clinic =
        ref.watch(clinicProfileProvider).value?.name ?? 'Your clinic';
    final dark = ref.watch(themeModeProvider) == ThemeMode.dark;

    return HubScope(
      hidden: _hidden,
      onGo: _go,
      child: Listener(
        behavior: HitTestBehavior.translucent,
        onPointerDown: (_) => _touch(),
        onPointerHover: (_) => _touch(),
        onPointerSignal: (_) => _touch(),
        child: Scaffold(
          backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).scaffoldBackgroundColor,
          body: LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, c) {
              final compact = c.maxWidth < 1280;
              return Column(
                children: [
                  _header(d, branches, compact, clinic: clinic, dark: dark),
                  Divider(height: 1, color: d.line),
                  Expanded(child: _body(d, branches, compact)),
                ],
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _body(DentColors d, List<Branch> branches, bool compact) {
    switch (_phase) {
      case _Phase.starting:
        return Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator(color: d.ice));
      case _Phase.failed:
        return HubEmpty(
          _fatal ?? 'Something went wrong.',
          icon: Icons.error_outline,
        );
