      case _Phase.gate:
        return HubPinGate(
          key: ValueKey(_gateMode),
          mode: _gateMode,
          user: _user!,
          session: _session!,
          onDone: _unlocked,
          onExit: _confirmExit,
        );
      case _Phase.loading:
        return HubLoading(
          step: _step,
          error: _loadError,
          onRetry: () => _load(full: true),
          onExit: _confirmExit,
        );
      case _Phase.ready:
        final covered = _locked || _changingPin;
        return Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            _rail(d, compact),
            VerticalDivider(width: 1, color: d.line),
            Expanded(
              child: Stack(
                children: [
                  Positioned.fill(
                    child: AbsorbPointer(
                      absorbing: covered,
                      child: ImageFiltered(
                        enabled: covered,
                        imageFilter: ImageFilter.blur(sigmaX: 18, sigmaY: 18),
                        child: _content(d, branches),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (covered)
                    Positioned.fill(
                      child: ColoredBox(
                        color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: .18),
                        child: _locked
                            ? HubPinGate(
                                key: ValueKey('relock$_lockCount'),
                                mode: HubGateMode.unlock,
                                relock: true,
                                user: _user!,
                                session: _session!,
                                onDone: _unlocked,
                                onExit: _confirmExit,
                              )
                            : HubPinGate(
                                key: const ValueKey('change'),
                                mode: HubGateMode.change,
                                user: _user!,
                                session: _session!,
                                onDone: () async {
                                  await _unlocked();
                                  if (!mounted) return;
                                  ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                    const SnackBar(
                                      content: Text(
                                        'Your Hub PIN was changed.',
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  );
                                },
                                onExit: _confirmExit,
                                onCancel: () =>
                                    setState(() => _changingPin = false),
                              ),
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        );
    }
  }

  // ───────────────────────────── header ─────────────────────────────

  Widget _header(
    DentColors d,
    List<Branch> branches,
    bool compact, {
    required String clinic,
    required bool dark,
  }) {
    final ready = _phase == _Phase.ready && !_locked && !_changingPin;
    final canPickScope = !_scopeFixed && branches.length > 1;

    Widget headerButton({
      required IconData icon,
      required String label,
      required VoidCallback? onTap,
      Color? color,
    }) {
      final fg = color ?? d.text2;
      return compact
          ? IconButton(
              tooltip: label,
              onPressed: onTap,
              icon: Icon(icon, size: 20, color: fg),
            )
          : OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: onTap,
              style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                foregroundColor: fg,
                side: BorderSide(
                  color: color == null ? d.line : color.withValues(alpha: .45),
                ),
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                  horizontal: 14,
                  vertical: 14,
                ),
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
                ),
              ),
              icon: Icon(icon, size: 18),
              label: Text(
                label,
                style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9.8.sp, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
              ),
            );
    }

    return Container(
      height: 72,
      color: d.surface,
      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: compact ? 14 : 22),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            width: 42,
            height: 42,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: d.accentGradient,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
            child: const Icon(
              Icons.insights_rounded,
              color: AppPalette.onAccent,
              size: 22,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 13),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'DETAILED REPORTING HUB',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.ice,
                    fontSize: 9.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                    letterSpacing: 1.1,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text(
                  '$clinic · ${_scopeName(branches)}',
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                    color: d.text1,
                    fontSize: 12.5.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (ready && _data != null) ...[
            if (!compact)
              Text(
                'Updated ${hubClock(_data!.loadedAt)}',
                style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.4.sp),
              ),
            IconButton(
              tooltip: 'Refresh',
              onPressed: _refreshing ? null : () => _load(full: false),
              icon: _refreshing
                  ? SizedBox(
                      width: 18,
                      height: 18,
                      child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                        strokeWidth: 2,
                        color: d.ice,
                      ),
                    )
                  : Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, color: d.text2),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            if (canPickScope) ...[
              _scopePicker(d, branches),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
            ],
            headerButton(
              icon: Icons.picture_as_pdf_outlined,
              label: 'Owner report',
              onTap: _exportPdf,
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            headerButton(
              icon: _hidden
                  ? Icons.visibility_outlined
                  : Icons.visibility_off_outlined,
              label: _hidden ? 'Show numbers' : 'Hide numbers',
              onTap: () => setState(() => _hidden = !_hidden),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            PopupMenuButton<String>(
              tooltip: 'More',
              icon: Icon(Icons.more_horiz_rounded, color: d.text2),
              color: d.surface,
              onSelected: (v) {
                if (v == 'pin') setState(() => _changingPin = true);
                if (v == 'lock') _lock();
              },
              itemBuilder: (_) => [
                PopupMenuItem(
                  value: 'pin',
                  child: _menuItem(d, Icons.password_rounded, 'Change Hub PIN'),
                ),
                PopupMenuItem(
                  value: 'lock',
                  child: _menuItem(d, Icons.lock_outline_rounded, 'Lock now'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
          ],
          IconButton(
            tooltip: dark ? 'Light mode' : 'Dark mode',
            onPressed: () => ref.read(themeModeProvider.notifier).toggle(),
            icon: Icon(
              dark ? Icons.light_mode_rounded : Icons.dark_mode_rounded,
              color: d.text2,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 6),
          headerButton(
            icon: Icons.logout_rounded,
            label: 'Exit Hub',
            onTap: _confirmExit,
            color: d.alert,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _menuItem(DentColors d, IconData icon, String text) => Row(
    children: [
      Icon(icon, size: 18, color: d.text2),
      const SizedBox(width: 10),
      Text(
        text,
        style: TextStyle(color: d.text1, fontSize: 10.sp),
      ),
    ],
  );

  Widget _scopePicker(DentColors d, List<Branch> branches) => Container(
    height: 40,
    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(
      color: d.surface2,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
      border: Border.all(color: d.line),
    ),
    child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
      child: DropdownButton<String?>(
        value: branches.any((b) => b.uuid == _scope) ? _scope : null,
        dropdownColor: d.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        icon: Icon(Icons.expand_more_rounded, size: 19, color: d.text3),
        style: TextStyle(color: d.text1, fontSize: 9.8.sp),
        items: [
          DropdownMenuItem<String?>(
            value: null,
            child: Text(
              'All branches',
              style: TextStyle(color: d.text1, fontSize: 9.8.sp),
            ),
          ),
          for (final b in branches)
            DropdownMenuItem<String?>(
              value: b.uuid,
              child: Text(
                b.name,
                style: TextStyle(color: d.text1, fontSize: 9.8.sp),
              ),
            ),
        ],
        onChanged: _setScope,
      ),
    ),
  );

  // ───────────────────────────── rail ─────────────────────────────

  Widget _rail(DentColors d, bool compact) {
    final data = _data;
    int? badge(HubSection s) => switch (s) {
      HubSection.rightNow => data?.urgent,
      HubSection.health => data?.health.issues,
      HubSection.watchList => data?.watch.flags,
      _ => null,
    };

    return Container(
      width: compact ? 78 : 236,
      color: d.surface,
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(
        compact ? 10 : 14,
        18,
        compact ? 10 : 14,
        16,
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          for (final s in HubSection.values) ...[
            _railItem(d, s, compact, badge(s)),
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
          ],
          const Spacer(),
          if (!compact)
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(13),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: d.surface2,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.shield_outlined, size: 16, color: d.ok),
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      Text(
                        'Protected',
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: d.text1,
                          fontSize: 9.6.sp,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    'Numbers hide after 5 quiet minutes; the Hub closes '
                    'after 15. Signed in as ${_session?.fullName ?? ''}.',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      color: d.text3,
                      fontSize: 9.sp,
                      height: 1.4,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _railItem(DentColors d, HubSection s, bool compact, int? badge) {
    final on = s == _section;
    final fg = on ? d.ice : d.text2;
    final count = badge ?? 0;
    final showBadge = count > 0;
    final badgeTone = s == HubSection.rightNow ? d.alert : d.warn;

    final item = Material(
      color: on ? d.ice.withValues(alpha: .12) : Colors.transparent,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
      child: InkWell(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        onTap: () {
          if (on) return;
          setState(() => _section = s);
          if (_scroll.hasClients) _scroll.jumpTo(0);
        },
        child: Padding(
          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
            horizontal: compact ? 0 : 12,
            vertical: 12,
          ),
          child: compact
              ? Stack(
                  clipBehavior: Clip.none,
                  alignment: Alignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Icon(s.icon, size: 22, color: fg),
                    if (showBadge)
                      Positioned(
                        right: 10,
                        top: -4,
                        child: _badge(d, count, badgeTone),
                      ),
                  ],
                )
              : Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(s.icon, size: 20, color: fg),
                    const SizedBox(width: 12),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        s.label,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          color: on ? d.text1 : d.text2,
                          fontSize: 10.4.sp,
                          fontWeight: on ? FontWeight.w800 : FontWeight.w600,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (showBadge) _badge(d, count, badgeTone),
                  ],
                ),
        ),
      ),
    );
    return compact ? Tooltip(message: s.label, child: item) : item;
  }

  Widget _badge(DentColors d, int n, Color tone) => Container(
    constraints: const BoxConstraints(minWidth: 22),
    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 6, vertical: 2),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(
      color: tone,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
    ),
    child: Text(
      '$n',
      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
      style: TextStyle(
        color: Colors.white,
        fontSize: 9.sp,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
      ),
    ),
  );

  // ───────────────────────────── content ─────────────────────────────

  Widget _content(DentColors d, List<Branch> branches) {
    final data = _data!;
    final picker = _PeriodPicker(period: _period, onChanged: _setPeriod);

    final page = switch (_section) {
      HubSection.rightNow => RightNowSection(data: data),
      HubSection.whatsComing => WhatsComingSection(data: data),
      HubSection.watchList => WatchListSection(
        data: data,
        period: _period,
        periodPicker: picker,
      ),
      HubSection.money => MoneySection(
        data: data,
        period: _period,
        periodPicker: picker,
      ),
      HubSection.dentists => DentistsSection(
        data: data,
        period: _period,
        periodPicker: picker,
      ),
      HubSection.branches => BranchesSection(
        data: data,
        period: _period,
        periodPicker: picker,
        branchCount: branches.length,
        scopeName: _scope == null ? null : _scopeName(branches),
        onShowAll: _scopeFixed ? null : () => _setScope(null),
      ),
      HubSection.health => HealthSection(data: data),
    };

    return Column(
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          height: 3,
          child: _refreshing
              ? LinearProgressIndicator(
                  minHeight: 3,
                  backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                  valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(d.ice),
                )
              : null,
        ),
        Expanded(
          // Uses the whole width on any screen; only the side padding grows
          // with the window, so big monitors get more columns, not margins.
          child: LayoutBuilder(
            builder: (context, c) {
              final side = (c.maxWidth * .018).clamp(18.0, 36.0);
              return Scrollbar(
                controller: _scroll,
                child: SingleChildScrollView(
                  controller: _scroll,
                  padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(side, 22, side, 48),
                  child: AnimatedSwitcher(
                    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
                    switchInCurve: Curves.easeOut,
                    layoutBuilder: (current, previous) => Stack(
                      alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
                      children: [...previous, if (current != null) current],
                    ),
                    child: KeyedSubtree(key: ValueKey(_section), child: page),
                  ),
                ),
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// "This month ▾" — the presets plus a custom date range.
class _PeriodPicker extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PeriodPicker({required this.period, required this.onChanged});
  final HubPeriod period;
  final ValueChanged<HubPeriod> onChanged;

  Future<void> _custom(BuildContext context) async {
    final today = DateTime.now();
    final picked = await showDateRangePicker(
      context: context,
      firstDate: DateTime(2020),
      lastDate: DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day),
      initialDateRange: DateTimeRange(
        start: period.start,
        end: period.end.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)).isAfter(today)
            ? DateTime(today.year, today.month, today.day)
            : period.end.subtract(const Duration(days: 1)),
      ),
      helpText: 'Pick the days to look at',
      saveText: 'Show',
    );
    if (picked == null || !context.mounted) return;
    final days = picked.end.difference(picked.start).inDays + 1;
    if (days > HubPeriod.maxCustomDays) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(content: Text('Pick up to 2 years at a time.')),
      );
      return;
    }
    onChanged(HubPeriod.range(picked.start, picked.end));
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return PopupMenuButton<HubPreset>(
      tooltip: 'Change dates',
      color: d.surface,
      position: PopupMenuPosition.under,
      onSelected: (p) =>
          p == HubPreset.custom ? _custom(context) : onChanged(HubPeriod.of(p)),
      itemBuilder: (_) => [
        for (final p in HubPeriod.presets)
          PopupMenuItem(
            value: p,
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(
                  p == period.preset
                      ? Icons.radio_button_checked_rounded
                      : Icons.radio_button_off_rounded,
                  size: 18,
                  color: p == period.preset ? d.ice : d.text4,
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Text(
                  p.label,
                  style: TextStyle(color: d.text1, fontSize: 10.sp),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        const PopupMenuDivider(),
        PopupMenuItem(
          value: HubPreset.custom,
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.date_range_rounded, size: 18, color: d.ice),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Text(
                'Custom range…',
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: d.text1,
                  fontSize: 10.sp,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
      child: Container(
        height: 40,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: d.surface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(11),
          border: Border.all(color: d.line),
        ),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.calendar_today_rounded, size: 16, color: d.ice),
            const SizedBox(width: 8),
            Text(
              period.label,
              style: TextStyle(
                color: d.text1,
                fontSize: 10.sp,
                fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Icon(Icons.expand_more_rounded, size: 18, color: d.text3),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/widgets/hub_pin_gate.dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_pin.dart';
import '../../data/hub_security.dart';

enum HubGateMode { setup, unlock, reset, change }

enum _Step { password, current, code, pin, confirm }

/// Everything about the Hub PIN in one card: first-time setup (password →
/// PIN → again), unlock, "Forgot PIN?" with a vendor reset code, and
/// change PIN. Five wrong tries lock this computer for five minutes.
class HubPinGate extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const HubPinGate({
    super.key,
    required this.mode,
    required this.user,
    required this.session,
    required this.onDone,
    required this.onExit,
    this.onCancel,
    this.relock = false,
  });

  final HubGateMode mode;
  final User user;
  final AuthSession session;

  /// Unlocked, or a new PIN was saved.
  final VoidCallback onDone;

  /// Leave the Hub.
  final VoidCallback onExit;

  /// Change PIN only — back to the Hub without changing anything.
  final VoidCallback? onCancel;

  /// Locked after a quiet spell (different wording).
  final bool relock;

  @override
  ConsumerState<HubPinGate> createState() => _HubPinGateState();
}

class _HubPinGateState extends ConsumerState<HubPinGate> {
  late HubGateMode _mode = widget.mode;
  late List<_Step> _steps = _stepsFor(_mode);
  int _i = 0;

  final _field = TextEditingController();
  final _focus = FocusNode();
  bool _busy = false, _showPassword = false;
  String? _error;
  String _firstPin = '', _code = '';
  DateTime? _lockedUntil;
  Timer? _ticker;
  late final Future<String?> _clinicId = ref
      .read(appDatabaseProvider)
      .currentClinicId();

  _Step get _step => _steps[_i];
  bool get _isPin =>
      _step == _Step.pin || _step == _Step.confirm || _step == _Step.current;

  static List<_Step> _stepsFor(HubGateMode m) => switch (m) {
    HubGateMode.setup => const [_Step.password, _Step.pin, _Step.confirm],
    HubGateMode.unlock => const [_Step.current],
    HubGateMode.reset => const [_Step.code, _Step.pin, _Step.confirm],
    HubGateMode.change => const [_Step.current, _Step.pin, _Step.confirm],
  };

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _refreshLock();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _ticker?.cancel();
    _field.dispose();
    _focus.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _refreshLock() async {
    final until = await ref.read(hubSecurityProvider).lockedUntil(widget.user);
    if (!mounted) return;
    _setLock(until);
  }

  void _setLock(DateTime? until) {
    _ticker?.cancel();
    setState(() => _lockedUntil = until);
    if (until == null) return;
    _ticker = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) {
      if (!mounted) return;
      if (DateTime.now().isAfter(until)) {
        _ticker?.cancel();
        setState(() {
          _lockedUntil = null;
          _error = null;
        });
        _focus.requestFocus();
      } else {
        setState(() {}); // countdown
      }
    });
  }

  void _go(HubGateMode m) => setState(() {
    _mode = m;
    _steps = _stepsFor(m);
    _i = 0;
    _error = null;
    _firstPin = '';
    _code = '';
    _field.clear();
    _focus.requestFocus();
  });

  void _next() => setState(() {
    _i++;
    _error = null;
    _field.clear();
    _focus.requestFocus();
  });

  void _fail(String msg, {bool clear = true}) => setState(() {
    _error = msg;
    if (clear) _field.clear();
    _focus.requestFocus();
  });

  void _handle(HubCheck c, String wrongWord) {
    switch (c.result) {
      case HubCheckResult.ok:
        return;
      case HubCheckResult.wrong:
        _fail(
          'Wrong $wrongWord · ${c.triesLeft} '
          '${c.triesLeft == 1 ? 'try' : 'tries'} left',
        );
      case HubCheckResult.locked:
        _field.clear();
        _error = null;
        _setLock(c.until);
    }
  }

  Future<void> _submit() async {
    if (_busy || _lockedUntil != null) return;
    final v = _field.text.trim();
    final sec = ref.read(hubSecurityProvider);
    setState(() => _busy = true);
    try {
      switch (_step) {
        case _Step.password:
          if (v.isEmpty) return _fail('Enter your login password.');
          // the password exactly as typed — spaces can be part of it
          final pw = await sec.checkPassword(
            widget.user,
            widget.session,
            _field.text,
          );
          if (pw.ok) return _next();
          _handle(pw, 'password');

        case _Step.current:
          if (v.length < HubPin.minLength) {
            return _fail(
              'Enter your ${HubPin.minLength}–${HubPin.maxLength} digit PIN.',
            );
          }
          final pinCheck = await sec.checkPin(widget.user, widget.session, v);
          if (!pinCheck.ok) return _handle(pinCheck, 'PIN');
          if (_mode == HubGateMode.unlock) return widget.onDone();
          _next();

        case _Step.code:
          final codeCheck = await sec.checkResetCode(widget.user, _field.text);
          if (codeCheck.error != null) {
            return _fail(codeCheck.error!, clear: false);
          }
          _code = _field.text;
          _next();

        case _Step.pin:
          final weak = HubPin.problem(v);
          if (weak != null) return _fail(weak);
          _firstPin = v;
          _next();

        case _Step.confirm:
          if (v != _firstPin) {
            setState(() {
              _i = _steps.indexOf(_Step.pin);
              _firstPin = '';
              _field.clear();
              _error = "The two PINs didn't match. Choose your PIN again.";
            });
            _focus.requestFocus();
            return;
          }
          if (_mode == HubGateMode.reset) {
            final err = await sec.redeemReset(
              widget.user,
              widget.session,
              _code,
              v,
            );
            if (err != null) {
              setState(() {
                _i = 0;
                _error = err;
                _field.text = _code;
              });
              return;
            }
          } else {
            await sec.setPin(
              widget.user,
              widget.session,
              v,
              kind: _mode == HubGateMode.change
                  ? 'hub_pin_changed'
                  : 'hub_pin_set',
            );
          }
          widget.onDone();
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _fail('Something went wrong: $e');
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _busy = false);
    }
  }

  // ───────────────────────────── text ─────────────────────────────

  String get _title => switch (_step) {
    _Step.password => "First, confirm it's you",
    _Step.current =>
      _mode == HubGateMode.change
          ? 'Enter your current PIN'
          : (widget.relock ? 'The Hub is locked' : 'Enter your Hub PIN'),
    _Step.code => 'Reset your Hub PIN',
    _Step.pin =>
      _mode == HubGateMode.change ? 'Choose a new PIN' : 'Choose your Hub PIN',
    _Step.confirm => 'Enter the PIN again',
  };

  String get _message => switch (_step) {
    _Step.password =>
      'Enter your login password once. Then you will choose a Hub PIN that '
          'protects this screen every time it opens.',
    _Step.current =>
      _mode == HubGateMode.change
          ? 'To change your PIN, enter the one you use now.'
          : (widget.relock
                ? 'Nothing happened for 5 minutes, so the numbers were '
                      'hidden. Enter your PIN to carry on.'
                : 'This screen shows every money figure in the clinic.'),
    _Step.code =>
      'Contact Inover Studio support with your clinic ID and username below. '
          'They will send you a reset code that works once, for one day.',
    _Step.pin => '4 to 6 digits. Avoid 1234, 0000 or your phone number.',
    _Step.confirm => 'Type the same PIN once more so we know it is right.',
  };

  String get _button => switch (_step) {
    _Step.password => 'Continue',
    _Step.current => _mode == HubGateMode.change ? 'Continue' : 'Unlock',
    _Step.code => 'Check code',
    _Step.pin => 'Continue',
    _Step.confirm => 'Save PIN',
  };

  IconData get _icon => switch (_mode) {
    HubGateMode.setup => Icons.shield_outlined,
    HubGateMode.unlock => Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
    HubGateMode.reset => Icons.key_rounded,
    HubGateMode.change => Icons.password_rounded,
  };

  // ───────────────────────────── build ─────────────────────────────

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final locked = _lockedUntil != null;
    final left = locked ? _lockedUntil!.difference(DateTime.now()) : null;

    return Center(
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 460),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(30, 30, 30, 22),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: d.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
              border: Border.all(color: d.line),
              boxShadow: [
                BoxShadow(
                  color: Colors.black.withValues(alpha: .12),
                  blurRadius: 40,
                  offset: const Offset(0, 18),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Row(
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      width: 50,
                      height: 50,
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        gradient: LinearGradient(
                          colors: [
                            d.ice.withValues(alpha: .2),
                            d.teal.withValues(alpha: .12),
                          ],
                        ),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
                      ),
                      child: Icon(
                        locked ? Icons.timer_outlined : _icon,
                        color: locked ? d.warn : d.ice,
                        size: 25,
                      ),
                    ),
                    const Spacer(),
                    if (_steps.length > 1) _dots(d),
                  ],
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 18),
                Text(
                  locked ? 'Too many wrong tries' : _title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                    color: d.text1,
                    fontSize: 14.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text(
                  locked
                      ? 'For safety this computer is locked for a few '
                            'minutes. You can try again in '
                            '${left!.inMinutes}:${(left.inSeconds % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0')}.'
                      : _message,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.text2,
                    fontSize: 10.2.sp,
                    height: 1.5,
                  ),
                ),
                if (!locked && _step == _Step.code) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 14),
                  _whoAmI(d),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 18),
                if (!locked) _input(d),
                if (_error != null && !locked) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Icon(
                        Icons.error_outline_rounded,
                        size: 17,
                        color: d.alert,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(width: 6),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          _error!,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: d.alert,
                            fontSize: 9.8.sp,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                            height: 1.35,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
                const SizedBox(height: 18),
                SizedBox(
                  height: 48,
                  child: FilledButton(
                    onPressed: (_busy || locked) ? null : _submit,
                    style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: d.ice,
                      foregroundColor: AppPalette.onAccent,
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: _busy
                        ? const SizedBox(
                            width: 20,
                            height: 20,
                            child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                              strokeWidth: 2.2,
                              color: AppPalette.onAccent,
                            ),
                          )
                        : Text(
                            _button,
                            style: TextStyle(
                              fontSize: 10.6.sp,
                              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                            ),
                          ),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                _links(d),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _dots(DentColors d) => Row(
    children: [
      for (var k = 0; k < _steps.length; k++)
        AnimatedContainer(
          duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
          margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 6),
          width: k == _i ? 22 : 8,
          height: 8,
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: k <= _i ? d.ice : d.line,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          ),
        ),
    ],
  );

  Widget _input(DentColors d) {
    final border = OutlineInputBorder(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
      borderSide: BorderSide(color: d.line),
    );
    final focused = OutlineInputBorder(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(13),
      borderSide: BorderSide(color: d.ice, width: 1.6),
    );

    if (_step == _Step.code) {
      return TextField(
        controller: _field,
        focusNode: _focus,
        autofocus: true,
        minLines: 3,
        maxLines: 5,
        enabled: !_busy,
        style: AppTypography.mono(size: 9.4.sp, color: d.text1),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText: 'DRH1.…  paste the whole code here',
          hintStyle: TextStyle(color: d.text4, fontSize: 9.6.sp),
          filled: true,
          fillColor: d.surface2,
          border: border,
          enabledBorder: border,
          focusedBorder: focused,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
        ),
      );
    }

    if (_step == _Step.password) {
      return TextField(
        controller: _field,
        focusNode: _focus,
        autofocus: true,
        obscureText: !_showPassword,
        enabled: !_busy,
        onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 11.sp, color: d.text1),
        decoration: InputDecoration(
          hintText: 'Login password',
          hintStyle: TextStyle(color: d.text4, fontSize: 10.4.sp),
          prefixIcon: Icon(Icons.lock_outline_rounded, color: d.text3),
          suffixIcon: IconButton(
            tooltip: _showPassword ? 'Hide' : 'Show',
            icon: Icon(
              _showPassword
                  ? Icons.visibility_off_outlined
                  : Icons.visibility_outlined,
              color: d.text3,
            ),
            onPressed: () => setState(() => _showPassword = !_showPassword),
          ),
          filled: true,
          fillColor: d.surface2,
          border: border,
          enabledBorder: border,
          focusedBorder: focused,
          contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 16),
        ),
      );
    }

    // PIN steps
    assert(_isPin);
    return TextField(
      controller: _field,
      focusNode: _focus,
      autofocus: true,
      obscureText: true,
      obscuringCharacter: '●',
      enabled: !_busy,
      keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
      textAlign: TextAlign.center,
      inputFormatters: [
        FilteringTextInputFormatter.digitsOnly,
        LengthLimitingTextInputFormatter(HubPin.maxLength),
      ],
      onSubmitted: (_) => _submit(),
      style: TextStyle(
        fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
        fontSize: 20.sp,
        letterSpacing: 14,
        color: d.text1,
        fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
      ),
      decoration: InputDecoration(
        hintText: '••••',
        hintStyle: TextStyle(
          color: d.text4.withValues(alpha: .5),
          fontSize: 18.sp,
          letterSpacing: 14,
        ),
        filled: true,
        fillColor: d.surface2,
        border: border,
        enabledBorder: border,
        focusedBorder: focused,
        contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
      ),
    );
  }

  /// Clinic ID + username, to read out to support.
  Widget _whoAmI(DentColors d) => FutureBuilder<String?>(
    future: _clinicId,
    builder: (context, snap) {
      Widget row(String label, String value) => Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            SizedBox(
              width: 92,
              child: Text(
                label,
                style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.4.sp),
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: SelectableText(
                value,
                style: AppTypography.mono(size: 10.sp, color: d.text1),
              ),
            ),
            IconButton(
              tooltip: 'Copy',
              visualDensity: VisualDensity.compact,
              icon: Icon(Icons.copy_rounded, size: 16, color: d.text3),
              onPressed: () => Clipboard.setData(ClipboardData(text: value)),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      );
      return Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 6, 6, 6),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: d.surface2,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            row('Clinic ID', snap.data ?? '…'),
            row('Username', widget.user.username),
          ],
        ),
      );
    },
  );

  Widget _links(DentColors d) {
    TextButton link(String text, VoidCallback onTap, {Color? color}) =>
        TextButton(
          onPressed: _busy ? null : onTap,
          style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: color ?? d.text2),
          child: Text(
            text,
            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9.8.sp, fontWeight: FontWeight.w600),
          ),
        );

    final items = <Widget>[];
    switch (_mode) {
      case HubGateMode.unlock:
        items.add(link('Forgot PIN?', () => _go(HubGateMode.reset)));
        items.add(link('Exit Hub', widget.onExit, color: d.alert));
      case HubGateMode.reset:
        items.add(
          link(
            'Back',
            () => _go(
              widget.mode == HubGateMode.reset
                  ? HubGateMode.unlock
                  : widget.mode,
            ),
          ),
        );
        items.add(link('Exit Hub', widget.onExit, color: d.alert));
      case HubGateMode.change:
        items.add(link('Forgot PIN?', () => _go(HubGateMode.reset)));
        items.add(link('Cancel', widget.onCancel ?? widget.onExit));
      case HubGateMode.setup:
        items.add(link('Exit Hub', widget.onExit, color: d.alert));
    }
    return Row(
      mainAxisAlignment: items.length == 1
          ? MainAxisAlignment.center
          : MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
      children: items,
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/widgets/hub_settings_panel.dart
import 'package:drift/drift.dart' hide Column;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_security.dart';
import '../hub_entry.dart';
import 'hub_ui.dart';

/// Settings → Detailed Reporting Hub (owner only): let admins in or not,
/// the owner's PIN status, and the latest Hub activity.
class HubSettingsPanel extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const HubSettingsPanel({super.key});

  @override
  ConsumerState<HubSettingsPanel> createState() => _HubSettingsPanelState();
}

class _HubSettingsPanelState extends ConsumerState<HubSettingsPanel> {
  bool _saving = false;

  Future<void> _setAdmins(bool allowed) async {
    final ok = await showHubConfirm(
      context,
      icon: allowed ? Icons.admin_panel_settings_outlined : Icons.block_rounded,
      tone: allowed ? context.dent.warn : context.dent.ice,
      title: allowed
          ? 'Let admins open the Hub?'
          : 'Stop admins opening the Hub?',
      message: allowed
          ? 'Every admin will see all money figures, each dentist’s earnings '
                'and every branch. Each admin sets their own Hub PIN.'
          : 'Admins inside the Hub right now are sent back to the Dashboard.',
      confirm: allowed ? 'Allow admins' : 'Stop access',
    );
    if (!ok) return;
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    try {
      final db = ref.read(appDatabaseProvider);
      final clinicId = await db.currentClinicId() ?? '';
      final now = DateTime.now();
      final existing =
          await (db.select(db.rolePermissions)..where(
                (t) =>
                    t.role.equals(AppRole.admin.name) &
                    t.key.equals(Perm.viewReportingHub),
              ))
              .getSingleOrNull();
      if (existing == null) {
        await db
            .into(db.rolePermissions)
            .insert(
              RolePermissionsCompanion.insert(
                clinicId: clinicId,
                role: AppRole.admin.name,
                key: Perm.viewReportingHub,
                allowed: Value(allowed),
                updatedAt: Value(now),
              ),
            );
      } else {
        await (db.update(db.rolePermissions)..where(
              (t) =>
                  t.role.equals(AppRole.admin.name) &
                  t.key.equals(Perm.viewReportingHub),
            ))
            .write(
              RolePermissionsCompanion(
                allowed: Value(allowed),
                updatedAt: Value(now), // → pushed on the next sync
              ),
            );
      }
      await ref
          .read(hubSecurityProvider)
          .log(
            ref.read(authControllerProvider),
            allowed ? 'hub_admins_on' : 'hub_admins_off',
          );
    } catch (e) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(
          context,
        ).showSnackBar(SnackBar(content: Text('Could not save: $e')));
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) setState(() => _saving = false);
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final onPlan = ref.watch(entitlementsProvider).reportingHub;
    final perms = ref.watch(permissionsProvider).value ?? const {};
    final adminsAllowed =
        perms['${AppRole.admin.name}|${Perm.viewReportingHub}'] ?? false;
    final pinSetAt = ref.watch(myHubPinSetAtProvider).value;
    final events = ref.watch(hubEventsProvider).value ?? const [];

    Widget row({
      required IconData icon,
      required String title,
      required String sub,
      required Widget trailing,
    }) => Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 18, vertical: 14),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        border: Border(bottom: BorderSide(color: d.line)),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 20, color: d.text3),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  title,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.text1,
                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text(
                  sub,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.text3,
                    fontSize: 9.sp,
                    height: 1.35,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          trailing,
        ],
      ),
    );

    return DentPanel(
      title: 'Detailed Reporting Hub',
      subtitle: onPlan
          ? 'Full-screen owner view · protected by a PIN'
          : 'Standard & Premium plans',
      trailing: OutlinedButton.icon(
        onPressed: () => openReportingHub(context, ref),
        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
          foregroundColor: onPlan ? d.ice : d.text3,
          side: BorderSide(color: d.line),
        ),
        icon: Icon(
          onPlan ? Icons.insights_rounded : Icons.lock_outline_rounded,
          size: 16,
        ),
        label: const Text('Open Hub'),
      ),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          row(
            icon: Icons.admin_panel_settings_outlined,
            title: 'Admins can open the Hub',
            sub: adminsAllowed
                ? 'On — admins see every money figure, using their own Hub PIN.'
                : 'Off — only you can open it. Other roles never can.',
            trailing: _saving
                ? const SizedBox(
                    width: 22,
                    height: 22,
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2),
                  )
                : Switch(
                    value: adminsAllowed,
                    activeColor: d.ice,
                    onChanged: onPlan ? _setAdmins : null,
                  ),
          ),
          row(
            icon: Icons.pin_outlined,
            title: 'Your Hub PIN',
            sub: pinSetAt == null
                ? 'Not set yet — you create it the first time you open the Hub.'
                : 'Set on ${hubDate(pinSetAt)}. Change it from the Hub’s menu. '
                      'Forgot it? Inover Studio can send a one-time reset code.',
            trailing: Icon(
              pinSetAt == null
                  ? Icons.radio_button_unchecked_rounded
                  : Icons.check_circle_rounded,
              color: pinSetAt == null ? d.text4 : d.ok,
              size: 22,
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 14, 18, 6),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Text(
                  'RECENT HUB ACTIVITY',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.text4,
                    fontSize: 9.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w800,
                    letterSpacing: .8,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (events.isEmpty)
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 4, 18, 16),
              child: Align(
                alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                child: Text(
                  'Nothing yet.',
                  style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.4.sp),
                ),
              ),
            )
          else
            for (final e in events.take(5))
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 6, 18, 6),
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      e.kind == 'hub_pin_wrong' || e.kind == 'hub_pin_locked'
                          ? Icons.warning_amber_rounded
                          : Icons.history_rounded,
                      size: 16,
                      color:
                          e.kind == 'hub_pin_wrong' ||
                              e.kind == 'hub_pin_locked'
                          ? d.alert
                          : d.text4,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: 10),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        '${hubEventLabel(e.kind)}'
                        '${e.userName.isEmpty ? '' : ' · ${e.userName}'}',
                        maxLines: 1,
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: TextStyle(color: d.text2, fontSize: 9.4.sp),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Text(
                      '${hubShortDate(e.happenedAt)} · ${hubClock(e.happenedAt)}',
                      style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.sp),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/widgets/hub_loading.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';

/// "Getting your clinic ready" — the real steps the loader is working on,
/// ticked off as each one finishes. Shown until every section has data.
class HubLoading extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubLoading({
    super.key,
    required this.step,
    this.error,
    this.onRetry,
    this.onExit,
  });

  /// Index into [hubLoadSteps] of the step running now.
  final int step;
  final Object? error;
  final VoidCallback? onRetry, onExit;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final total = hubLoadSteps.length;
    final progress = (step.clamp(0, total) / total).toDouble();

    return Center(
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 480),
          child: Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(30, 30, 30, 26),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: d.surface,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(22),
              border: Border.all(color: d.line),
            ),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                Text(
                  error == null
                      ? 'Getting your clinic ready'
                      : "Couldn't load the Hub",
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                    color: d.text1,
                    fontSize: 14.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text(
                  error == null
                      ? 'Reading this computer’s records. Nothing leaves the clinic.'
                      : '$error',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: error == null ? d.text3 : d.alert,
                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                    height: 1.45,
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 22),
                for (var i = 0; i < total; i++) _row(d, i),
                const SizedBox(height: 18),
                ClipRRect(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                  child: TweenAnimationBuilder<double>(
                    tween: Tween(end: progress),
                    duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 350),
                    builder: (_, v, __) => LinearProgressIndicator(
                      value: v,
                      minHeight: 8,
                      backgroundColor: d.line,
                      valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(
                        error == null ? d.ice : d.alert,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                if (error != null) ...[
                  const SizedBox(height: 20),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      if (onExit != null)
                        TextButton(
                          onPressed: onExit,
                          style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: d.text2),
                          child: Text(
                            'Exit Hub',
                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.sp),
                          ),
                        ),
                      const Spacer(),
                      if (onRetry != null)
                        FilledButton.icon(
                          onPressed: onRetry,
                          style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: d.ice,
                            foregroundColor: AppPalette.onAccent,
                          ),
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, size: 18),
                          label: Text(
                            'Try again',
                            style: TextStyle(fontSize: 10.sp),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _row(DentColors d, int i) {
    final done = i < step;
    final doing = i == step && error == null;
    final failed = i == step && error != null;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 22,
            height: 22,
            child: done
                ? Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: d.teal,
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                    ),
                    child: const Icon(
                      Icons.check_rounded,
                      size: 15,
                      color: AppPalette.onAccent,
                    ),
                  )
                : failed
                ? Icon(Icons.error_rounded, size: 22, color: d.alert)
                : doing
                ? Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(2),
                    child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                      strokeWidth: 2.4,
                      color: d.ice,
                    ),
                  )
                : Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                      border: Border.all(color: d.line, width: 2),
                    ),
                  ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              hubLoadSteps[i],
              style: TextStyle(
                color: doing || failed ? d.text1 : (done ? d.text2 : d.text4),
                fontSize: 10.4.sp,
                fontWeight: doing ? FontWeight.w700 : FontWeight.w500,
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
// ===== lib/features/hub/presentation/widgets/hub_ui.dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:sizer/sizer.dart';
import 'package:is_dental/core/constants/views.dart';

import '../../data/hub_data.dart';

/// Shared state every Hub widget can read without passing it down:
/// whether amounts are hidden, and how to leave the Hub for a screen.
class HubScope extends InheritedWidget {
  const HubScope({
    super.key,
    required this.hidden,
    required this.onGo,
    required super.child,
  });

  final bool hidden;
  final void Function(HubGo go, {String? arg, String? label}) onGo;

  static HubScope of(BuildContext context) =>
      context.dependOnInheritedWidgetOfExactType<HubScope>()!;

  @override
  bool updateShouldNotify(HubScope old) => old.hidden != hidden;
}

// ─────────────────────────────── formatting ───────────────────────────────

String hubDigits(int v) => v.abs().toString().replaceAllMapped(
  RegExp(r'(\d)(?=(\d{3})+$)'),
  (m) => '${m[1]},',
);

/// "Rs 1,24,500"-style amount with its sign; masked when [hidden].
String hubRs(int v, {bool hidden = false}) {
  if (hidden) return 'Rs ••••••';
  return '${v < 0 ? '– ' : ''}Rs ${hubDigits(v)}';
}

const _mon = [
  'Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', //
  'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec',
];
const _monLong = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', //
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December',
];
const _wd = [
  'Monday',
  'Tuesday',
  'Wednesday',
  'Thursday',
  'Friday',
  'Saturday',
  'Sunday',
];

String hubClock(DateTime t) {
  final h = t.hour % 12 == 0 ? 12 : t.hour % 12;
  return '$h:${t.minute.toString().padLeft(2, '0')} ${t.hour < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM'}';
}

String hubHour(int minutes) {
  final h24 = (minutes ~/ 60) % 24;
  final h = h24 % 12 == 0 ? 12 : h24 % 12;
  return '$h ${h24 < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM'}';
}

String hubDate(DateTime d) => '${d.day} ${_mon[d.month - 1]} ${d.year}';
String hubShortDate(DateTime d) => '${d.day} ${_mon[d.month - 1]}';
String hubMonthName(DateTime d) => _monLong[d.month - 1];
String hubWeekday(DateTime d) => _wd[d.weekday - 1];

/// "3 min ago", "2 h ago", "4 days ago".
String hubAgo(DateTime? t) {
  if (t == null) return 'never';
  final d = DateTime.now().difference(t);
  if (d.inMinutes < 1) return 'just now';
  if (d.inMinutes < 60) return '${d.inMinutes} min ago';
  if (d.inHours < 24) return '${d.inHours} h ago';
  final days = d.inDays;
  return days == 1 ? 'yesterday' : '$days days ago';
}

/// A change against an earlier figure, e.g. "▲ 12% vs last Wed".
class HubDelta {
  const HubDelta(this.text, this.good);
  final String text;

  /// null = neutral.
  final bool? good;
}

HubDelta? hubDelta(int cur, int prev, String vs, {bool upIsGood = true}) {
  if (prev <= 0) return cur > 0 ? HubDelta('New vs $vs', null) : null;
  final pct = ((cur - prev) / prev * 100).round();
  if (pct == 0) return HubDelta('Same as $vs', null);
  final up = pct > 0;
  return HubDelta('${up ? '▲' : '▼'} ${pct.abs()}% vs $vs', up == upIsGood);
}

// ─────────────────────────────── building blocks ──────────────────────────

Widget hubIconTile(
  DentColors d,
  IconData icon,
  Color tone, {
  double size = 40,
}) => Container(
  width: size,
  height: size,
  alignment: Alignment.center,
  decoration: BoxDecoration(
    color: tone.withValues(alpha: .13),
    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(size * .3),
  ),
  child: Icon(icon, size: size * .48, color: tone),
);

/// Amount text that turns into dots when numbers are hidden.
class HubAmount extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubAmount(this.amount, {super.key, required this.style});
  final int amount;
  final TextStyle style;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Text(
    hubRs(amount, hidden: HubScope.of(context).hidden),
    maxLines: 1,
    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
    style: style,
  );
}

class HubPill extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubPill(
    this.text,
    this.tone, {
    super.key,
    this.icon,
    this.maxLines = 1,
  });
  final String text;
  final Color tone;
  final IconData? icon;
  final int maxLines;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Container(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 9, vertical: 4),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(
      color: tone.withValues(alpha: .12),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
    ),
    child: Row(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        if (icon != null) ...[
          Icon(icon, size: 13, color: tone),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
        ],
        Flexible(
          child: Text(
            text,
            maxLines: maxLines,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: tone,
              fontSize: 9.2.sp,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ],
    ),
  );
}

/// A card with an icon, a title line and its content.
class HubCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubCard({
    super.key,
    required this.icon,
    required this.tone,
    required this.title,
    required this.child,
    this.subtitle,
    this.trailing,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final Color tone;
  final String title;
  final String? subtitle;
  final Widget? trailing;
  final Widget child;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: d.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: d.line),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 16, 14, 14),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                hubIconTile(d, icon, tone, size: 38),
                const SizedBox(width: 12),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        title,
                        style: TextStyle(
                          fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                          color: d.text1,
                          fontSize: 12.sp,
                          fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                        ),
                      ),
                      if (subtitle != null)
                        Text(
                          subtitle!,
                          style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.4.sp),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                if (trailing != null) trailing!,
              ],
            ),
          ),
          Divider(height: 1, color: d.line),
          child,
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// One headline figure: label, value, a line of detail, a change pill and
/// an optional split bar.
class HubKpi extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubKpi({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    this.amount,
    this.value,
    this.sub,
    this.delta,
    this.segments = const [],
    this.valueColor,
  }) : assert(amount != null || value != null);

  final String label;

  /// Money — hidden with "Hide numbers". Use [value] for counts.
  final int? amount;
  final String? value;
  final String? sub;
  final HubDelta? delta;
  final List<(double, Color)> segments;
  final Color? valueColor;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final valueStyle = TextStyle(
      fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
      color: valueColor ?? d.text1,
      fontSize: 17.sp,
      fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
      height: 1.15,
    );
    final deltaTone = delta?.good == null
        ? d.text3
        : (delta!.good! ? d.ok : d.alert);
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(18, 16, 18, 16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: d.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        border: Border.all(color: d.line),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            maxLines: 1,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: TextStyle(
              color: d.text3,
              fontSize: 9.6.sp,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          // Shrinks a long figure to fit instead of cutting it to "Rs 24,…".
          FittedBox(
            fit: BoxFit.scaleDown,
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
            child: amount != null
                ? HubAmount(amount!, style: valueStyle)
                : Text(value!, maxLines: 1, style: valueStyle),
          ),
          if (sub != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 4),
            Text(
              sub!,
              maxLines: 2,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.2.sp, height: 1.35),
            ),
          ],
          if (segments.isNotEmpty) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            HubSegBar(segments),
          ],
          if (delta != null) ...[
            const SizedBox(height: 10),
            HubPill(delta!.text, deltaTone, maxLines: 2),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A thin bar split into coloured parts (fractions of the whole).
class HubSegBar extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubSegBar(this.segments, {super.key, this.height = 7});
  final List<(double, Color)> segments;
  final double height;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    final total = segments.fold<double>(0, (s, e) => s + e.$1);
    return ClipRRect(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(height),
      child: Container(
        height: height,
        color: d.line,
        child: total <= 0
            ? null
            : Row(
                children: [
                  for (final s in segments)
                    if (s.$1 > 0)
                      Expanded(
                        flex: (s.$1 / total * 1000).round().clamp(1, 1000),
                        child: Container(color: s.$2),
                      ),
                ],
              ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Label · amount row with a bar showing its share.
class HubShareRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubShareRow({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.amount,
    required this.fraction,
    required this.color,
    this.note,
  });

  final String label;
  final int amount;
  final double fraction;
  final Color color;
  final String? note;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 7),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  label,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style: TextStyle(
                    color: d.text1,
                    fontSize: 10.sp,
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
              if (note != null) ...[
                Text(
                  note!,
                  style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 9.2.sp),
                ),
                const SizedBox(width: 10),
              ],
              HubAmount(
                amount,
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: d.text1,
                  fontSize: 10.sp,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 6),
          ClipRRect(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(6),
            child: LinearProgressIndicator(
              value: fraction.clamp(0, 1).toDouble(),
              minHeight: 7,
              backgroundColor: d.line,
              valueColor: AlwaysStoppedAnimation(color),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class HubEmpty extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubEmpty(this.text, {super.key, this.icon = Icons.inbox_outlined});
  final String text;
  final IconData icon;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 34, horizontal: 20),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Icon(icon, size: 28, color: d.text4),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),
          Text(
            text,
            textAlign: TextAlign.center,
            style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 10.sp, height: 1.4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Big section heading at the top of each Hub page.
class HubSectionTitle extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubSectionTitle({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    this.trailing,
  });

  final String title, subtitle;
  final Widget? trailing;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 18),
      child: Wrap(
        spacing: 16,
        runSpacing: 12,
        alignment: WrapAlignment.spaceBetween,
        crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.end,
        children: [
          Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                title,
                style: TextStyle(
                  fontFamily: AppFonts.display,
                  color: d.text1,
                  fontSize: 16.sp,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.w700,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 3),
              Text(
                subtitle,
                style: TextStyle(color: d.text3, fontSize: 10.sp),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          if (trailing != null) trailing!,
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// Lays children out in equal columns that wrap on narrow windows.
class HubGrid extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubGrid({
    super.key,
    required this.children,
    this.minWidth = 230,
    this.maxColumns = 5,
    this.gap = 16,
    this.equalHeight = true,
  });

  final List<Widget> children;
  final double minWidth, gap;
  final int maxColumns;

  /// Cards in one row share the tallest card's height, so their tops,
  /// bottoms and bars line up. Children must not contain a LayoutBuilder.
  final bool equalHeight;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => LayoutBuilder(
    builder: (context, c) {
      if (children.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
      final cols = (c.maxWidth / minWidth).floor().clamp(1, maxColumns);
      final n = children.length < cols ? children.length : cols;
      if (!equalHeight) {
        final w = (c.maxWidth - gap * (n - 1)) / n;
        return Wrap(
          spacing: gap,
          runSpacing: gap,
          children: [for (final ch in children) SizedBox(width: w, child: ch)],
        );
      }
      final rows = <Widget>[];
      for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i += n) {
        final row = children.sublist(i, (i + n).clamp(0, children.length));
        rows.add(
          IntrinsicHeight(
            child: Row(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
              children: [
                for (var j = 0; j < n; j++) ...[
                  if (j > 0) SizedBox(width: gap),
                  Expanded(
                    child: j < row.length ? row[j] : const SizedBox.shrink(),
                  ),
                ],
              ],
            ),
          ),
        );
      }
      return Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          for (var k = 0; k < rows.length; k++) ...[
            if (k > 0) SizedBox(height: gap),
            rows[k],
          ],
        ],
      );
    },
  );
}

/// Two panes side by side on wide windows, stacked on narrow ones.
class HubTwoUp extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubTwoUp({
    super.key,
    required this.left,
    required this.right,
    this.breakAt = 980,
    this.leftFlex = 1,
    this.rightFlex = 1,
  });

  final Widget left, right;
  final double breakAt;
  final int leftFlex, rightFlex;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => LayoutBuilder(
    builder: (context, c) => c.maxWidth < breakAt
        ? Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: [left, const SizedBox(height: 18), right],
          )
        : Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Expanded(flex: leftFlex, child: left),
              const SizedBox(width: 18),
              Expanded(flex: rightFlex, child: right),
            ],
          ),
  );
}

/// The small "Open …" link in a card's corner. Leaving the Hub asks first.
class HubGoLink extends StatelessWidget {
  const HubGoLink(this.label, this.go, {super.key, this.arg});
  final String label;
  final HubGo go;
  final String? arg;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final d = context.dent;
    return TextButton.icon(
      onPressed: () => HubScope.of(context).onGo(go, arg: arg, label: label),
      style: TextButton.styleFrom(foregroundColor: d.ice),
      icon: Icon(Icons.open_in_new_rounded, size: 15, color: d.ice),
      label: Text(
        label,
        style: TextStyle(fontSize: 9.6.sp, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
      ),
    );
  }
}
